Zhang,Z. Zhang,J. Zhao,Y. Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文利用LHCb探测器在13 TeV质心能量下收集的6 fb^-1质子-质子碰撞数据（2015-2018年），测量了B^0_s → J/ψπ^+π^-衰变中的时间依赖CP不对称性。测得CP破坏相角φ_s = -0.077 ± 0.034 ± 0.007 rad，直接CP破坏参数|λ| = 0.993 ± 0.026 ± 0.007，以及重质量本征态衰变宽度Γ_H = 0.610 ± 0.002 ± 0.004 ps^-1。结果与标准模型预期及此前测量一致，组合所有LHCb测量后φ_s = -0.041 ± 0.017 rad。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量B^0_s介子系统中的CP破坏相角φ_s，以检验标准模型预测并寻找超出标准模型的新物理信号。B^0_s → J/ψπ^+π^-衰变是测量φ_s的重要黄金通道，利用更大数据集（13 TeV，6 fb^-1）提高测量精度。

Method: 使用LHCb探测器在2015-2018年收集的质子-质子碰撞数据，对B^0_s → J/ψπ^+π^-衰变进行时间依赖的CP不对称性分析。通过拟合衰变时间分布提取CP破坏相角φ_s、直接CP破坏参数|λ|及重质量本征态衰变宽度Γ_H。随后将结果与此前7 TeV和8 TeV数据的结果进行组合，并进一步与所有LHCb其他测量组合。

Result: 在13 TeV数据中测得：φ_s = -0.077 ± 0.034 ± 0.007 rad，|λ| = 0.993 ± 0.026 ± 0.007，Γ_H = 0.610 ± 0.002 ± 0.004 ps^-1。与此前7、8 TeV数据组合后得φ_s = -0.046 ± 0.031 rad，|λ| = 0.975 ± 0.024，Γ_H = 0.610 ± 0.004 ps^-1。包含所有LHCb测量时φ_s = -0.041 ± 0.017 rad。

Conclusion: 所有测量结果与标准模型预测及此前测量结果一致，未发现超出标准模型的新物理迹象。组合测量显著提高了φ_s的精度，为约束标准模型参数和探寻新物理提供了更精确的实验输入。

Abstract: The time-dependent $C\!P$ asymmetry in $B^{0}_{s} \!\to J/ψπ^{+}π^{-}$ decays is measured using proton-proton collision data, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $6\,\text{fb}^{-1}$, collected with the LHCb detector at a centre-of-mass energy of $13\,\text{TeV}$ during $\mbox{2015--2018}$. The $C\!P$-violating phase, $φ_{s}$, the direct $C\!P$-violation parameter, $\left|λ\right|$, and the decay width of the heavy mass eigenstate in the $B^{0}_{s}$ system, $Γ_{\mathrm{ H}}$, are measured respectively to be $φ_{s} = -0.077 \pm 0.034 \pm 0.007\,\text{rad}$, $\left|λ\right| = 0.993 \pm 0.026 \pm 0.007$ and $Γ_{\mathrm{ H}} = 0.610 \pm 0.002 \pm 0.004\,\text{ps}^{-1}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. These results are consistent with previous measurements and the expectation based on the Standard Model. The combination with previous measurements in $B^{0}_{s} \!\to J/ψπ^{+}π^{-}$ decays using $7\,\text{TeV}$ and $8\,\text{TeV}$ proton-proton collision data yields $φ_{s} = -0.046 \pm 0.031\,\text{rad}$, $\left|λ\right| = 0.975 \pm 0.024$ and $Γ_{\mathrm{ H}} = 0.610 \pm 0.004\,\text{ps}^{-1}$, while the combination including all other LHCb measurements gives $φ_{s} = -0.041 \pm 0.017\,\text{rad}$.

</details>

### [2] [Adjacency-Based Spectral Proxy Control of Mobile Communication Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13616)
*Mariana del Castillo,Federico Larroca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对异构移动代理网络中的通信代理重定位问题，提出A-Fiedler控制器，用邻接矩阵的主特征向量（图嵌入）替代传统Fiedler向量作为网络连通性控制的替代目标，从而实现更易于分布式估计的频谱图控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代数连通度控制依赖Fiedler向量，其分布式估计需要无界数量的通信轮次才能收敛，不适合实时在线控制。作者希望通过识别Fiedler梯度控制器的结构分解，寻找更易于分布式估计的替代嵌入向量，以克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 作者首先将Fiedler梯度控制器分解为局部交互规则与图嵌入两个组件，然后提出A-Fiedler方法，用邻接矩阵的主特征向量（即节点在潜在几何空间中的图嵌入表示）替代Fiedler向量。该方法更符合局部通信约束下的分布式实现需求。

Result: 在无通信约束场景下，A-Fiedler与经典Fiedler梯度控制器性能相当；在分布式估计条件下，A-Fiedler具有更好的鲁棒性。例如在相同通信轮数下，Fiedler梯度方法可能收敛到不连通配置，而A-Fiedler仍能维持网络性能。

Conclusion: A-Fiedler为分布式网络控制提供了更简单可行的路径，通过使用更易估计的图嵌入替代Fiedler向量，在保持性能的同时显著提升分布式实现的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We consider a heterogeneous mobile-agent network composed of uncontrolled task agents and controllable communication agents. The objective is to reposition communication agents online as task agents move. Since throughput-based objectives are generally unsuitable for real-time control, spectral graph metrics such as algebraic connectivity are commonly adopted as surrogate objectives. However, controlling algebraic connectivity relies on the eigenvector corresponding to the second-smallest eigenvalue of a graph's Laplacian matrix (i.e., the Fiedler vector), whose distributed estimation requires an unbounded number of communication rounds to converge.
  In this work, we identify a structural decomposition of this Fiedler-gradient controller into a local interaction rule and a graph embedding component, suggesting the use of alternative embeddings that are easier to estimate distributively than the Fiedler vector. As a particular instance, we propose A-Fiedler, which replaces the Fiedler embedding with the dominant eigenvector of the adjacency matrix, commonly used as a graph embedding of nodes into a latent geometry. This representation is more naturally suited for distributed implementation under local communication constraints.
  We evaluate A-Fiedler against the classical Fiedler-gradient controller. Results show comparable network performance in the absence of communication constraints and improved robustness under distributed estimation. For instance, under the same number of communication rounds, the Fielder-gradient may even converge to disconnected configurations whereas our proposition maintains performance. We believe our contribution provides a simpler path toward distributed network control.

</details>


### [3] [hint$^2$: Hierarchical World Models for Inference-Time Temporal Logic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13678)
*Moritz Zoellner,Anastasios Manganaris,Ahmed H. Qureshi,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出hint²，一种通过层次化世界模型在推理时引导短视域策略满足复杂线性时序逻辑（LTL）规范的方法。高电平模型预测任务相关原子命题的动作诱导转换以推进LTL自动机进展，低电平模型预测即时状态演化以实现局部安全引导，在CALVIN基准和真实UR5e机械臂上均取得优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习的目标之一是使机器人能够执行运行时指定的丰富指令。大规模语言条件策略虽已取得显著进展，但仍难以处理时间结构和安全性约束。LTL提供了一种表达复杂非马尔可夫指令的有力语言，但现代策略生成短视域动作块并进行闭环重规划，而几乎所有LTL规范都是在长视域轨迹上评估的，导致引导学习型操作策略满足LTL规范的挑战尚未解决。

Method: hint²的核心思想是利用层次化世界模型在推理时推导两个独立的引导目标。高电平世界模型预测未来动作诱导的任务相关原子命题转换，用于引导策略在LTL自动机中的进展；低电平动力学模型预测即时状态演化，用于提供准确的局部安全引导。通过这两个不同抽象层级的引导目标相结合，使短视域策略能够在推理时满足复杂LTL规范。

Result: hint²克服了现有LTL引导扩散方法的局限性，在CALVIN基准上优于现有的推理时转向方法，且比语言条件替代方法更优雅地完成了具有复杂活性（liveness）和安全性约束的指令。此外，在真实UR5e机械臂上成功处理了复杂指令。

Conclusion: hint²通过层次化世界模型在推理时为短视域策略提供LTL引导，有效弥合了短视域动作生成与长视域LTL规范之间的鸿沟，同时在仿真和真实机器人平台上验证了其处理复杂时间结构和安全性约束指令的能力。

Abstract: A central goal of robot learning is to enable robots to execute rich instructions specified at runtime. Large-scale language-conditioned policies have made substantial progress toward this goal, yet still struggle with temporal structure and safety constraints. Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) provides a powerful language to express complex, non-Markovian instructions. However, guiding learned manipulation policies toward LTL satisfaction remains challenging because modern policies generate short-horizon action chunks and replan in closed loop, while almost all LTL specifications are evaluated over long-horizon trajectories. In this paper, we introduce hint$^2$, a method for guiding short-horizon policies toward satisfying complex LTL specifications at inference time using hierarchical world models. Our key idea is to derive two separate guidance objectives using each world model's abstraction level. A high-level model predicts future action-induced transitions in task-relevant atomic propositions to guide progress through the LTL automaton, while a low-level dynamics model predicts immediate state evolution for accurate local safety guidance. Our results show that hint$^2$ overcomes the limitations of current LTL-guided diffusion methods, outperforms existing inference-time steering methods in CALVIN, and successfully completes instructions with complex liveness and safety constraints more elegantly than language-conditioned alternatives. Finally, we demonstrate that hint$^2$ can handle complex instructions on a real UR5e manipulator.

</details>


### [4] [Graph-MambaNav: Spatial-Temporal Graph Mamba Leveraging Object-Relation Knowledge for Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13723)
*Leyuan Sun,Genxin Chen,Linwei Ye,Yan Zhang,Xi Kan,Yanfei Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Graph-MambaNav，一种基于目标感知的时空图编码框架，利用LLM先验对物体进行启发式排序，结合空间图Mamba和时序Mamba建模，提升目标导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的物体目标导航方法虽在特征或注意力层面具有目标感知能力，但保持置换不变性，缺乏对信息传播顺序的显式控制，难以建模目标相关的物体重要性和长距离依赖关系。

Method: 提出Graph-MambaNav框架：(1) 基于LLM推导的物体常识关系初始化节点排序和边权重，将更相关的物体置于后续处理以聚合更丰富上下文；(2) 空间模块结合局部消息传递和基于Graph-Mamba的选择性扫描进行全局推理；(3) 时序模块通过Mamba对物体维度时序顺序建模，选择性聚合历史上下文支持长距离时序推理。

Result: 在AI2-THOR和RoboTHOR数据集上取得改进的导航性能和泛化能力，并通过真实机器人部署进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式设计目标相关的节点排序机制和LLM统一先验，Graph-MambaNav有效克服了置换不变性限制，验证了节点优先级在目标导航推理中的关键作用，并展示了从仿真到真实世界部署的实用性。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation requires an agent to reason over object relationships and prioritize target-relevant objects for efficient decision making in unseen environments. While existing graph-based methods incorporate target-awareness at the feature or attention level, they remain permutation-invariant and lack an explicit mechanism to control information propagation order, limiting their ability to model target-dependent importance and long-range dependencies. In contrast, Graph-Mamba highlights that node prioritization through sequence ordering is critical for effective global reasoning. In this work, we investigate the node prioritization mechanism in Graph-Mamba and study its role in object navigation. We propose Graph-MambaNav, a target-aware spatial-temporal graph encoding framework that introduces a heuristic ordering over objects based on their relevance to the target, allowing more informative objects to be processed later to aggregate richer context. Both node ordering and edge weights are initialized from LLM-derived commonsense object relationships, providing a unified prior for structured reasoning. A spatial module integrates local message passing with global GraphMamba-based selective scanning, while a temporal module applies Mamba-based sequence modeling over object-wise temporal orders, allowing selective aggregation of historical context for long-range temporal reasoning. Experiments on AI2-THOR and RoboTHOR demonstrate improved navigation performance with generalization, and additional real-world robot deployment further validates the effectiveness of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [5] [Knowledge-Data-Dual-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Vehicle Control in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13878)
*Jie Fang,Wei Zheng,Mengyun Xu,Eui-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出知识-数据双驱动强化学习框架（KDDRL），通过条件深度生成模型合成意图感知的未来轨迹、融合物理约束与数据驱动信息，并利用耦合模块实现连续跟驰与离散换道的异步多时间尺度优化，在混合交通场景中显著提升自动驾驶的安全、效率与舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通中的自动驾驶决策面临三大挑战：基于物理先验的强化学习模型无法捕捉潜在的交互意图和多样化的驾驶员行为，限制了主动推理能力；周围车辆的突然机动导致非平稳性，使得长尾安全事件未被充分探索；混合动作空间中连续跟驰与离散换道的不同时间尺度导致统一强化学习训练不稳定。

Method: KDDRL框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 条件深度生成模型用于合成意图感知的未来轨迹，将被动感知转化为主动预测状态；(2) 知识-数据双驱动范式在预测状态上进行操作，融合概率数据驱动信息与物理约束，引导安全关键场景中的安全探索；(3) 耦合模块将意图感知轨迹与物理约束压缩为紧凑共享嵌入，实现连续跟驰与离散换道的异步多时间尺度优化，同时保留互信息。

Result: 在数据集校准的仿真评估中，KDDRL有效处理了意图不确定性，加速了训练收敛，并在安全性、效率和舒适性指标上优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: KDDRL通过知识-数据双驱动范式和统一表示学习，有效解决了混合交通中多尺度决策、意图不确定性及长尾安全问题，为自动驾驶决策提供了兼顾安全性与性能的解决方案。

Abstract: In mixed traffic, decision-making for autonomous vehicles (AVs) confronts three interrelated challenges. First, physics-based priors incorporated into reinforcement learning (RL) models fail to capture latent interactive vehicle intentions and diverse driver behaviors, limiting the proactive reasoning capabilities. Second, abrupt maneuvers by surrounding vehicles cause non-stationarity, leaving long-tail safety events under-explored. Third, hybrid action spaces destabilize unified RL training due to the different temporal scales of continuous car-following and discrete lane-changing maneuvers. To address these issues, we propose Knowledge-Data Dual-driven Reinforcement Learning (KDDRL). First, a conditional deep generative model synthesizes intention-aware future trajectories, converting passive perception into proactive predictive states. Second, a knowledge-data dual-driven paradigm operates on these predictive states, fusing probabilistic data-driven insights with physical constraints to guide safe exploration through safety-critical scenarios. Third, a coupling module compresses both intention-aware trajectories and physical constraints into compact shared embeddings. This unified representation enables asynchronous multi-timescale optimization of continuous car-following and discrete lane-changing while preserving mutual information. Evaluations on dataset-calibrated simulations demonstrate that KDDRL effectively handles intention uncertainty, accelerates training convergence, and outperforms conventional baseline methods in terms of safety, efficiency, and comfort.

</details>


### [6] [Ontology-Grounded World Models for Failure Diagnosis and Closed-Loop Repair in Physical AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13901)
*Kailin Wang,Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Jiacheng Zhou,Zhiyou Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Onto-EV-WM，一个基于本体论（ontology）的诊断与验证门控校正接口，叠加在EV-WM世界模型之上。通过任务局部TBox定义实体与谓词签名，源特定接地映射到ABox，确定性规则保留缺失谓词与其参数并分配修正路由标签。在PointMaze、LIBERO-Goal和LIBERO-Plus基准上均取得出色表现（94%、93.8%、85%成功率），相比EV-WM显著降低了最终状态距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EV-WM的候选质量通过特征分数和事件分数表示，但这些分数没有显式记录未满足的任务谓词、可用的修正机制的路由标签，以及修正后的接受结果。这导致系统无法结构性地诊断失败原因并指导修正，需要一个更明确的符号接口来提升可解释性和校正能力。

Method: Onto-EV-WM作为EV-WM之上的分层接口，而非替代性世界模型架构。具体包括：1) 任务局部TBox定义实体类型、谓词签名和约束；2) 源特定接地将预测或模拟器观测状态映射到任务ABox；3) 确定性规则在分配路由标签时保留每个缺失谓词及其参数；4) 学习或启发式提议者与该符号接口分离，由原生任务谓词决定接受，有界协议决定验证失败后是否重试。

Result: 在PointMaze上，EV-WM和Onto-EV-WM均报告94%成功率，但平均最终状态距离分别为0.90573和0.61177；单独预算搜索达100%成功率。LIBERO-Goal上完整配置在seed 0报告93.8%修正窗口成功率，跨四个评估采样种子为94.05±0.30%。LIBERO-Plus固定10,030任务注册表上，Onto-EV-WM成功8,526任务（85.00%），其中LIBERO-10为65.98%，LIBERO-Goal为91.39%，LIBERO-Object和LIBERO-Spatial均为91.38%。

Conclusion: Onto-EV-WM通过本体论驱动的诊断和验证门控修正接口有效提升了任务成功率，特别是显著减小了最终状态距离。但需注意：这些数字报告的是完整本体配置在测试模拟器协议下的表现，本体单独因果贡献未单独测量，真实机器人恢复能力未评估。

Abstract: EV-WM represents candidate quality with feature and event scores, but these scores do not explicitly record an unmet task predicate, a route label for an available correction mechanism, or a post-correction acceptance result. We present Onto-EV-WM, an ontology-grounded diagnosis and verification-gated correction interface layered above EV-WM rather than a replacement world-model architecture. The implemented task-local TBox defines entity types, predicate signatures, and constraints; source-specific grounding maps predicted or simulator-observed states to task ABoxes; and deterministic rules retain each missing predicate and its arguments when assigning a route label. Learned or heuristic proposers remain separate from this symbolic interface; native task predicates determine acceptance, and the bounded protocol determines whether a failed verification is retried. In the aligned PointMaze evaluation, EV-WM and Onto-EV-WM both report 94% success, with mean final-state distances of 0.90573 and 0.61177, respectively; the separately budgeted search reaches 100% success. On LIBERO-Goal, the ontology represents failed task conditions as typed records, retains their predicate arguments, and associates them with the declared source/joint correction route and predicate-gated acceptance; the complete configuration reports 93.8% corrected-window success on seed 0 and 94.05 +- 0.30% across four evaluation-sampling seeds. On the fixed 10,030-task LIBERO-Plus registry, Onto-EV-WM succeeds on 8,526 tasks (85.00%), with suite-level success rates of 65.98% for LIBERO-10, 91.39% for LIBERO-Goal, and 91.38% for both LIBERO-Object and LIBERO-Spatial. These numbers report the performance of the complete ontology-grounded configurations under the tested simulator protocols; an ontology-only causal share is not measured separately, and real-robot recovery is not evaluated.

</details>


### [7] [Communication in modular robotic motor control: Bilateral controllers under realistic constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13904)
*Jingwen Li,Levin Kuhlmann,Jason Friedman,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受大脑双侧半球组织启发的模块化循环控制器，由两个GRU模块通过可学习的延迟胼胝体连接通道通信，在可微分的双臂肌肉骨骼模拟器中端到端训练。实验表明，该模块化架构在到达和保持任务中显著优于容量匹配的整体式基线，且模块间通信改善了末端精度、降低了非零延迟下的能量消耗并减少了肌肉共收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉骨骼系统中的机器人运动控制需要在信号依赖噪声和能量成本的现实约束下实现快速准确运动与鲁棒姿势稳定。模块化控制器理论上可以分布这些竞争性需求，但尚不清楚其在现实约束下是否优于整体式架构，以及模块间通信如何影响控制策略。

Method: 设计一个由两个GRU模块组成的循环控制器，模块间通过一个可学习的、带时间延迟的通信通道相连，模拟大脑双侧半球通过胼胝体通信的组织方式。控制器在可微分的双臂肌肉骨骼模拟器中端到端训练，并在到达和保持两类任务上评估其性能，与容量匹配的整体式基线和无通信模块化基线进行对比。

Result: 模块化架构在到达和保持任务中大幅超越容量匹配的整体式基线。与无通信的模块化控制器相比，可学习的模块间通信重新塑造了控制策略：提高了末端精度，在非零延迟条件下降低了能量成本，并减少了肌肉共收缩。

Conclusion: 受生物学启发的模块化控制器为机器人运动控制提供了在噪声和能量约束下实现稳健运动的实用路径，模块间通信作为一种调节精度、稳定性和驱动成本之间权衡的机制。

Abstract: Robotic motor control in musculoskeletal systems requires fast, accurate movement and robust postural stabilization under signal-dependent noise (where motor command variance scales with command magnitude) and energetic cost. Modular controllers can distribute these competing demands across interacting submodules, but it remains unclear whether they outperform monolithic architectures under realistic constraints, and how inter-module communication shapes the resulting strategy. Inspired by the bilateral hemispheric organization of the brain, we introduce a recurrent controller of two GRU-based modules connected by a learnable, delayed inter-hemispheric channel, trained end-to-end in a differentiable two-arm musculoskeletal simulator. Across reaching and holding tasks, the modular architecture substantially outperforms a capacity-matched monolithic baseline. Compared to a matched modular controller without communication, learned inter-hemispheric communication reshapes the solution: improved endpoint precision, lower energetic cost in non-zero-delay regimes, and reduced muscle co-contraction. Our findings show that for robotics, biologically inspired modular controllers offer a practical route to robust movement under noise and energetic constraints, with inter-module communication providing a mechanism to tune trade-offs between precision, stability, and actuation cost.

</details>


### [8] [BICPO-VLA: Behavior-Identified Continuation Preference Optimization for Smooth Asynchronous Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13924)
*Ming Shang,Yuchen Huang,Jiaoyang Chen,Haoyuan Hu,Han Yu,Liping Song,Luyun Feng,Shuo Bao,Wei Dong,Xinzhou Wang,Fuchun Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BICPO-VLA通过三个顺序机制解决请求到交接间隙（request-to-handoff gap）问题：指令感知的因果历史编码器消除行为意图歧义、序列Haar子空间生成将动作分块分解为互补的支架与残差系数以缩短交接延迟、参考相对Flow-DPO在行为匹配候选中滚动已知输出动作以适配残余不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作生成过程中存在"请求到交接"间隙，该问题源于三个相互耦合的来源：请求时行为意图的歧义、动作生成期间物理状态的漂移、以及新动作接管时的残余不兼容性。这些因素共同导致机器人无法平滑地完成动作交接，需要系统化方法逐一解决。

Method: BICPO-VLA采用三步策略：(1) 指令感知的因果历史编码器，用于识别命令和当前任务进度所支持的行为；(2) 序列Haar子空间生成，将每个动作分块分解为成对的支架系数和残差系数，通过两个专门化生成阶段加精确重建，减少原始动作空间中的迭代细化从而缩短新分块可用前的时间间隔；(3) 将已知输出动作滚动到实际交接状态，并应用参考相对Flow-DPO在行为匹配的候选中进行适配，修正剩余的请求到交接不匹配而不改变预期行为。

Result: 论文提出的BICPO-VLA能够系统性地解决请求到交接间隙的三个耦合来源：通过指令感知编码解决行为歧义，通过Haar子空间生成减少交接延迟，通过Flow-DPO适配残余不匹配，实现更平滑高效的动作交接。

Conclusion: BICPO-VLA通过分解并依次处理请求到交接间隙的三个来源，实现了行为意图明确、延迟缩短且交接适配的机器人动作生成，在保持预期行为不变的前提下提高了动作交接的平滑性和可靠性。

Abstract: The request-to-handoff gap has three coupled sources: ambiguity about the behavior intended at request time, physical-state drift accumulated during action generation, and residual incompatibility when the new action finally assumes control. BICPO-VLA addresses them in sequence. First, an instruction-aware causal history encoder identifies the behavior supported by the command and current task progress. Second, sequential Haar subspace generation decomposes each action chunk into complementary pairwise scaffold and residual coefficients, enabling two specialized generation stages followed by exact reconstruction. By reducing iterative refinement in the original action space, it shortens the interval over which the robot continues moving before the new chunk becomes available. Finally, BICPO rolls the known outgoing actions to the actual handoff state and applies reference-relative Flow-DPO among behaviorally matched candidates, adapting the generated chunk to the remaining request-to-handoff mismatch without changing its intended behavior.

</details>

*Zhiyue Zhao,Jingyi Wu,Hairuo Liu,Mingyu Liu,Liyang Li,Hengdi Zhang,Tong He,Zhengxue Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AdvDex，一个统一的视觉-语言-动作框架，用于从人类和机器人示教中学习灵巧操作。通过引入大规模多模态人类示教数据集OmniShare、联合对齐动作空间（JAAS）和域对抗学习，实现了跨具身灵巧操作的泛化与零样本人机技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作是具身智能的基础能力，但规模化困难：机器人示教数据采集成本高昂、不同具身之间的动作空间不一致，且异构数据训练的策略容易将任务相关视觉线索与具身特定外观纠缠在一起，限制了跨具身泛化能力。

Method: 方法包含三个关键组件：(1) OmniShare，一个大规模多模态人类操作示教数据集，提供高质量运动学监督和触觉测量，减少对遥操作的依赖；(2) 联合对齐动作空间（JAAS），一种规范化的动作表示，由SE(3)腕部姿态和15个手指关节组成，功能上对齐了人手、灵巧机械手和平行夹爪；(3) 域对抗学习，用于减少学习到的视觉表征中的具身特定信息。

Result: 在手部动作预测和真实世界灵巧操作实验中，AdvDex相比基线方法取得了一致的性能提升，实现了有效的零样本人-机器人技能迁移，推广到未见物体和环境，并支持数据高效的少样本适配。

Conclusion: AdvDex通过数据、动作空间表示和表征学习三个层面的协同设计，有效解决了灵巧操作跨具身泛化的难题，展示了从人类示教到机器人灵巧操作的规模化学习路径。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, but scaling it remains difficult because robot demonstrations are expensive to collect and action spaces vary across embodiments. Policies trained on heterogeneous data can also entangle task-relevant visual cues with embodiment-specific appearance, limiting cross-embodiment generalization. We present AdvDex, a unified Vision-Language-Action framework for learning dexterous manipulation from human and robot demonstrations. First, we introduce OmniShare, a large-scale multimodal dataset of human manipulation demonstrations that provides high-quality kinematic supervision and tactile measurements while reducing reliance on robot teleoperation. Second, we propose the Joint-Aligned Action Space (JAAS), a canonical action representation comprising an $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ wrist pose and 15 finger joints, thereby functionally aligning human hands, dexterous robot hands, and parallel grippers. Finally, we use domain-adversarial learning to reduce embodiment-specific information in the learned visual representation. Experiments on hand-action prediction and real-world dexterous manipulation show consistent improvements over baselines, effective zero-shot human-to-robot skill transfer, generalization to unseen objects and environments, and data-efficient few-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [10] [Demonstration of Space Robot Teleoperation over a Lossy and Delayed Network using ATMOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14031)
*Inkyu Jang,Gregorio Marchesini,Nicola De Carli,Byeongjun Kim,Sunwoo Hwang,Dabin Kim,Elias Krantz,Youngkyoung Kong,Frank J. Jiang,Annika Wong,Pedro Roque,Prasetyo W. L. Sanjaya,Nicola Bastianello,Mani H. Dhullipalla,Karl H. Johansson,Hyungbo Shim,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,H. Jin Kim*

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ATMOS（多用途轨道系统自主测试平台），一种平面航天器模拟机器人，用于在微重力条件下的制导与控制策略硬件在环评估。研究者开发并实验验证了一种结合状态预测与轨迹跟踪控制的策略，以在存在随机通信延迟的情况下完成对接机动，并通过首尔至斯德哥尔摩的洲际远程控制实验进行了验证。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用ATMOS平面航天器模拟机器人作为物理测试平台；设计并实现结合状态预测和轨迹跟踪控制的控制架构；在首尔与斯德哥尔摩之间进行长距离远程控制实验，引入真实的洲际通信延迟与变异性来验证控制策略。

Result: 实验结果验证了所提出的预测与跟踪组合控制策略能够有效应对时变随机通信延迟，成功完成对接机动；ATMOS平台展示了支持快速、可靠且成本有效的航天器遥操作概念测试的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在微重力环境模拟条件下稳健验证在轨操作概念的第一步，为未来航天器遥操作控制架构的开发和验证提供了实用的地面测试平台和有效的方法论框架。

Abstract: We present a demonstration showcasing the Autonomy Testbed for Multi-purpose Orbiting Systems (ATMOS), a planar spacecraft-analog robot designed for hardware-in-the-loop evaluation of guidance and control strategies in microgravity-like conditions. Using ATMOS as the physical test platform, we investigate the design, analysis, and performance evaluation of control architectures for remotely operated spacecraft under round-trip communication delays. In this work, we develop and experimentally validate a control strategy that combines state prediction and trajectory tracking control to perform a docking maneuver, accounting for time-varying random communication latency between ground operators and the ATMOS system. The demonstration includes a long-distance remote control experiment between Seoul and Stockholm, introducing realistic intercontinental delays and variability. The results highlight the capability of ATMOS to support rapid, reliable, and cost-effective testing of spacecraft teleoperation concepts, establishing a first step toward robust validation of on-orbit operations in microgravity-like environments.

</details>


### [11] [Evolve Vision-Language-Action Model into an Agent with On-the-fly Tool-use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14047)
*Yi Ding,Yanzhao Yu,Xili Dai,Xianbiao Qi,Peiwen Sun,Xueqian Wang,Xiangyu Yue,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ART（Agentic Robot with Tool-use）框架，将端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型与agentic工具使用相结合，通过工具注入技术降低动作解空间复杂度，从而提升跨任务泛化能力并降低数据依赖。实验表明ART在仿真和真实场景中比主流基线方法成功率高出20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vanilla VLA模型拥有完整的连续动作解空间，导致泛化能力受限且对海量训练数据高度依赖，制约了VLA系统在复杂真实世界场景中的实际部署。

Method: ART是一种工具注入框架，能够将任意VLA模型调优为利用现成工具模块（包括低层视觉、高层affordance和具身增强）的agentic系统。该方法通过工具使用缩小动作解空间，并设计了30K轨迹的训练数据集以及面向长轨迹工具使用推理的训练方案。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界任务（如黑暗环境中的新型视角抓取放置）中，ART比主流基线方法的成功率高出20%；同时展现了更高效的训练、轻量化的部署和可扩展的新工具集成能力。

Conclusion: 基于agent的工具模块化利用能够提升VLA系统的鲁棒性、适应性和可扩展性，为复杂真实场景中的VLA系统实际部署铺平了道路。

Abstract: This paper integrates end-to-end Visual-Language-Action (VLA) models with agentic tool-use to propose Agentic Robot with Tool-use (ART). ART is a tool-injection framework that tunes any VLA model to leverage off-the-shelf tool modules for low-level vision, high-level affordance, and embodiment enhancement. Compared to vanilla VLA models with a whole continuous action solution space, ART reduces the complexity of the action solution space through tool-use, which not only improves generalizability across different tasks but also reduces data dependency. To demonstrate the advantages (high generalizability and low data dependency) of this framework, we first built a dataset of 30K tool-use trajectories and action demonstrations, which is much smaller than those used by baseline methods. We then designed a training regimen for long-trajectory tool-use reasoning in challenging environments. Experiments show that ART achieves a 20% higher success rate than mainstream baselines on simulation and real-world tasks, such as pick-and-place in the dark at novel viewpoints. Empirical results highlight the benefits of an agent-based approach: modular tool utilization enables more efficient training, lightweight deployment, and scalable integration of new tools. This design fosters robustness, adaptability, and extensibility, paving the way for the practical deployment of VLA systems in complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [12] [FlatLab: A Unified Methodology Framework and Simulation-Based Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation of Flat Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14049)
*Xingyu Zhu,Wenshuo Han,Zhouyu Wang,Yuran Wang,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong,Fan Tang,Hechang Chen,Hyung Jin Chang,Yixing Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将扁平物体（flat object）的机器人操作解耦为策略生成器与动作执行模块，并引入FlatLab仿真基准，用于系统评估未知物体和类别上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扁平物体的机器人操作面临难以抓取（ungraspable）及物体几何与材质差异大等挑战。现有方法依赖启发式预操作，且多在封闭环境中评估、泛化能力有限。因此需要一个统一的、可泛化的框架以及系统性的评估基准。

Method: 方法包含两个模块：(1) 策略生成器：从物体点云中预测操作策略，通过模拟数据变换和对比学习学习策略中心、物体无关的表示；(2) 动作执行模块：在预测策略的条件下，将长时程操作分解为可复用的动作原语，并动态组合生成稳定轨迹。此外提出FlatLab仿真基准，提供高保真物理仿真、多样化刚性/可变形扁平物体、自动多模态数据采集以及标准化任务定义和评估协议。

Result: 在FlatLab中的实验表明，所提方法能够有效泛化到未见过的物体和类别，性能优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 解耦策略生成与动作执行的统一框架，结合对比学习构建物体无关表征和可复用动作原语，为扁平物体操作提供了可行且可泛化的方案；FlatLab为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of flat objects is challenging due to the ungraspable configurations and strong variations in object geometry and material. Existing methods rely on heuristic pre-manipulation and are often evaluated in closed settings with limited generalization. We propose a unified framework that decouples the manipulation into a strategy generator and an action execution module. The strategy generator predicts appropriate manipulation strategies from object point clouds by learning strategy-centric, object-invariant representations via simulated data transformation and contrastive learning. Conditioned on the predicted strategy, the execution module decomposes long-horizon manipulation into reusable action primitives and dynamically composes them to generate stable trajectories. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce FlatLab, a comprehensive simulation benchmark for robotic flat object manipulation. FlatLab provides high-fidelity physical simulation of diverse rigid and deformable flat objects, automated multi-modal data collection, and standardized task definitions and evaluation protocols. Experiments conducted in FlatLab demonstrate that our approach generalizes effectively to unseen objects and categories, outperforming existing baselines. The project page and the code are provided at https://flatlab-web.github.io/.

</details>


### [13] [PILOT: Privileged Imitation Learning for End-to-End Motion Planning of Autonomous UAVs under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14082)
*Qingrui Zhang,Feng Xue,Xiang Zhou,Chenghao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PILOT框架，一种基于视觉的端到端UAV运动规划方法，通过从最优控制专家中蒸馏策略，并结合时空感知融合模块与轨迹参数化层，在部分可观测环境下实现高效、安全的自主导航。仿真与实物部署均验证其性能接近特权专家且计算开销降低80%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在杂乱环境中的自主导航面临部分可观测性和动态约束两大难题。传统方法依赖计算密集的最优控制或环境地图，难以在端到端视觉规划中兼顾实时性与安全性，需要一种既能处理部分观测、又能满足安全和动力学约束的高效规划框架。

Method: 采用特权模仿学习框架，将计算密集型的最优控制专家策略蒸馏到轻量级学生策略中，并通过双目标损失函数对学生策略施加安全与动力学约束正则化。为解决部分可观测性，设计了基于时序卷积网络（TCN）的时空感知融合模块，融合历史深度图像与里程计信息以推断任务相关潜上下文；同时引入轨迹参数化层，将网络输出映射为结构化轨迹，支持训练中显式施加连续性、动力学一致性及障碍惩罚软约束。

Result: 在四旋翼与固定翼飞机的仿真实验中，PILOT取得了与特权专家相当的性能，同时将计算开销降低超过80%。室内和室外场景的零样本实物部署成功，验证了该规划器的实际可行性与跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: PILOT框架通过特权模仿学习与时空感知融合，在部分可观测约束下实现了高效、安全的端到端UAV运动规划，并展示了良好的实际部署潜力和跨域泛化性。然而，该方法对未见观测的约束满足仅通过软惩罚鼓励，缺乏形式化安全保证。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in cluttered environments is hampered by partial observability and dynamic constraints. This paper presents PILOT, a constraint-aware privileged imitation learning framework for vision-based end-to-end UAV motion planning under partial observability. The framework distills planning strategies from a computationally intensive optimal control expert into a student policy regularized toward safety and dynamic requirements via a dual-objective loss function. To mitigate partial observability, a spatiotemporal perception fusion module using a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) is developed to integrate historical depth images and odometry. This module infers task-relevant latent context from historical observations, enhancing spatial awareness beyond the instantaneous FOV without maintaining persistent map memory. A trajectory parameterization layer mapping network outputs to a structured trajectory, while enabling explicit continuity, dynamic-consistency, and obstacle soft penalties during training, encouraging constraint satisfaction for unseen observations without formal guarantees. Simulations on quadrotor and fixed-wing aircraft demonstrate that PILOT achieves performance comparable to the privileged expert while reducing computational overhead by over 80\%. Successful indoor and outdoor zero-shot deployment confirms the practical feasibility and cross-domain generalization of the planner.

</details>


### [14] [AgilePE: Autonomous UAV Pursuit-Evasion via Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14135)
*Wenhao Tang,Tianyang Chen,Zhejun Cui,Boyuan An,Jiayu Chen,Ruize Zhang,Huidong Liu,Tianyue Wu,Qingmin Liao,Fei Gao,Yu Wang,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgilePE，一个基于自对弈强化学习的无人机自主追逃完整系统，端到端地将机载状态观测映射为集体推力和机体角速率命令，通过竞争性自对弈训练实现敏捷机动策略，并实现零样本的sim-to-real部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机自主追逃面临动力学强耦合和对手行为持续变化的挑战，传统基于规则或微分博弈的方法难以处理高维空中交互和敏捷机动场景，需要一种能够端到端学习敏捷追逃策略的新方法。

Method: AgilePE采用：（1）端到端的策略映射，直接输出CTBR命令，无需中间轨迹规划器或航点控制器；（2）使用带优先级的虚构自对弈（PFSP）和多样化对手池进行竞争性自对弈训练，以稳定优化并减少策略振荡；（3）构建与硬件对齐的仿真管线，建模执行器响应动态、通信延迟和域随机化，支持零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 学习到的策略能够零样本迁移到真实四旋翼无人机上，无需任务特定调参。真实世界实验复现了仿真中观察到的追逃战术（如快速闪避和侧翼包抄），并展示了交互式双智能体零样本部署能力。

Conclusion: AgilePE验证了通过自对弈强化学习可以在统一框架内实现无人机敏捷追逃的端到端控制与真实部署，为高动态自主空中对抗提供了可行且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous pursuit-evasion is a fundamental challenge for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), requiring rapid decision-making under tightly coupled dynamics and continuously changing opponent behaviors. Traditional rule-based or differential-game approaches often struggle with high-dimensional aerial interactions and agile maneuvering. We present AgilePE, a complete system for autonomous UAV pursuit-evasion via self-play reinforcement learning. AgilePE integrates agile low-level control, competitive policy optimization, and sim-to-real deployment in a unified framework. The policy directly maps onboard state observations to Collective Thrust and Body Rates (CTBR) commands, enabling end-to-end agile maneuvering without intermediate trajectory planners or waypoint controllers. For training, we use competitive self-play with Prioritized Fictitious Self-Play (PFSP) and a diversified opponent pool, enabling agents to improve against historical policies while stabilizing optimization and reducing policy oscillation. This process leads to the emergence of sophisticated pursuit and evasion strategies. For real-world deployment, we develop a hardware-aligned simulation pipeline that models actuator-response dynamics, communication latency, and domain randomization. The learned policies transfer zero-shot to real quadrotors without task-specific tuning. Real-world experiments reproduce pursuit-evasion tactics observed in simulation, including rapid dodging and flanking, and demonstrate interactive two-agent zero-shot deployment.

</details>


### [15] [OccPlanner: Goal-Aware Occupancy-Conditioned Diffusion Planner for Pixel-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14160)
*Binling Huang,Nianjin Ye,Xi Yang,Liang Hu,Zhou Huang,Shuang Wei,Longrui Yang,Yanchi Chen,Lanpeng Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OccPlanner，一种基于目标感知的占用条件扩散规划器，将像素级导航目标在机器人自身度量空间中接地，并利用时序视觉上下文和局部3D占用特征进行条件化；同时提出L3ROcc方法，从单目RGB导航视频中通过几何重建和射线可见性推理生成大规模机器人中心局部3D占用标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级目标导航直接在智能体相机视野中指定目标，但目标像素不提供度量深度或可通行性信息，导致3D目标接地和无碰撞连续规划困难。

Method: OccPlanner采用目标感知的占用条件扩散规划框架：首先将像素目标在自身度量空间中接地，然后依次利用时序视觉上下文和学习到的局部3D占用特征对目标表示进行条件化。同时提出L3ROcc，通过几何重建和基于射线的可见性推理，将单目RGB导航视频转换为机器人中心的局部3D占用标注，用于大规模监督训练。

Result: 在InternData-N1上训练，并在InternScenes的四个未见场景类别和两个目标距离范围上做闭环仿真评估。在5-8米设置下，OccPlanner相比NavDP将平均成功率从20.81%提升至71.55%，在cluttered-easy和cluttered-hard场景中分别达到86.20%和84.92%。在Unitree Go2上的真实世界开环实验初步验证了sim-to-real迁移和L3ROcc生成监督的适应性。

Conclusion: OccPlanner通过融合3D占用感知与扩散规划，显著提升了像素级目标导航的成功率和碰撞避免能力；L3ROcc为大规模训练提供了可扩展的占用监督生成方案，并初步证明其在真实机器人上的迁移潜力。

Abstract: Pixel-goal navigation specifies targets directly in the agent's camera view, but a target pixel provides neither metric depth nor traversability, making 3D goal grounding and collision-free continuous planning challenging. We present OccPlanner, a goal-aware occupancy-conditioned diffusion planner that grounds pixel goals in egocentric metric space and sequentially conditions the goal representation on temporal visual context and learned local 3D occupancy features. To provide occupancy supervision at scale, we introduce L3ROcc, which converts monocular RGB navigation videos into robot-centric local 3D occupancy annotations through geometric reconstruction and ray-based visibility reasoning. We train OccPlanner on InternData-N1 and evaluate it in closed-loop simulation across four unseen scene categories from InternScenes and two goal-distance ranges. In the 5-8 m setting, OccPlanner increases the average success rate (SR) over NavDP from 20.81% to 71.55% across the four categories, reaching 86.20% and 84.92% in cluttered-easy and cluttered-hard scenes, respectively. Real-world open-loop experiments on a Unitree Go2 further provide initial evidence of sim-to-real transfer and adaptation with L3ROcc-generated supervision.

</details>

Conclusion: MMUSV-Sim是一个功能完善的多USV海事感知仿真与数据生成平台，其生成的物理一致且几何准确的多模态数据可有效支持多USV协同感知系统的开发与评估。

Abstract: Cooperative perception among multiple unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) combines complementary observations to extend maritime target sensing beyond the view range and field of a single platform. Developing such systems at scale calls for a unified workflow for configurable multi-USV scenarios, multimodal acquisition, and shared annotations. We present MMUSV-Sim, a perception-oriented maritime simulation and data-generation platform built on Unreal Engine 5 and Project AirSim. It provides island, open-sea, and port environments; configurable weather, time of day, and wave conditions; a diverse vessel asset library; and spline-based multi-vessel motion. MMUSV-Sim acquires RGB, depth, semantic, LiDAR, and radar observations across multiple USVs and captures a common world state for per-agent annotation export. Experiments verify that the configured wave settings produce the intended changes in vessel heave, roll, and pitch, and evaluate the geometric consistency between projected annotations and semantic renderings. In LiDAR-based cooperative BEV vessel detection experiments on the generated multi-USV dataset, Early Fusion achieves an AP@0.5 of 72.74, compared with 45.54 using a single USV.

### [17] [Vibration Suppression in Collaborative Flexible Payload Manipulation Using Passive Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14244)
*Alaa Abderrahim,Antonio Rosales,Ferdinando Milella,Markku Suomalainen,Shuai Li*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于主从结构的协作载荷操纵控制策略，用于抑制大型柔性载荷在运动过程中的横向振动。领导者机器人通过整形速度指令引导运动，跟随者机器人通过导纳控制器对领导者施加的外力保持柔顺性，从而实现重型和大型柔性物体的协作搬运。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用领导者-跟随者配置的两台异构工业机器人进行协作载荷操纵。领导者机器人以整形速度指令引导运动，跟随者机器人通过导纳控制器根据估计的外力保持柔顺性。将系统动力学建模为等效质量-弹簧-阻尼模型，并证明在合适的导纳参数下系统总能量被动耗散，同时提供稳定性证明。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提方法的有效性，实验结果进一步证明了该方法在实际系统中的可行性。与现有方法相比，该方案能够处理更重、更大的柔性物体，并解决了振动抑制和异构机器人规格等额外挑战。

Conclusion: 通过主从协作架构与导纳控制相结合，实现了对柔性载荷横向振动的有效抑制，扩展了协作操纵在重型大型柔性物体上的应用范围，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: In large and heavy structures, vibrations arise during motion, posing significant challenges for precise manipulation. To accomplish the desired motion, control algorithms must effectively suppress these structural vibrations. In cutting edge projects, such as remote maintenance of future fusion energy reactors (tokamaks), the manipulation of this type of structure is defined as a crucial task. This paper presents a control strategy to suppress transverse vibrations in flexible payloads during motion using a collaborative payload manipulation approach. Two different industrial robot arms are arranged in a leader follower configuration for the manipulation strategy. The leader robot guides the motion with shaped velocity commands, while the follower robot ensures compliance with the estimated external forces applied by the leader on the payload through an admittance controller. Unlike existing methods, the proposed approach enables collaborative manipulation of heavier and larger flexible objects, addressing additional challenges such as vibration suppression and heterogeneous robot specifications. The dynamics of the leader follower payload system are modeled using an equivalent mass spring damper model, and it is shown that, with appropriate admittance parameters, the total energy of the system is passively dissipated. A stability proof is also provided. Numerical simulations validate the proposed method, and experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [18] [Accelerating Large-scale Bundle Adjustment for LiDAR Mapping via Parallel Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14266)
*Yixi Cai,Rundong Li,Yuhan Xie,Qingwen Zhang,Patric Jensfelt,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个全并行计算框架来加速大规模地图构建中的LiDAR束调整，通过自适应异步数据加载、自底向上体素化特征提取和基于majorization-minimization的并行优化求解，实现了高达十倍的效率提升，同时保持与最先进方法相当的制图精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR束调整是构建全局一致点云地图的关键技术，但现有方法在大规模地图构建场景下计算效率低下，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 提出三项关键技术：(1)自适应异步数据加载策略，在内存受限的GPU上高效处理大规模点云；(2)自底向上体素化方法用于全并行化的平面特征提取；(3)基于majorization-minimization公式对优化中的计算密集型任务（残差、雅可比、海森矩阵计算及并行增量求解器）进行并行加速。

Result: 在多个大规模公开数据集和不同计算平台上验证了方法的鲁棒性和适应性，相比现有方法实现了高达10倍的计算效率提升，同时保持了与最先进方法相当的制图精度。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个全并行LiDAR束调整框架，通过理论分析和实验验证表明其在效率和精度之间取得了良好平衡，并开源代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LiDAR bundle adjustment is widely utilized in mapping to construct globally consistent point cloud maps. In this paper, we propose the first fully parallel computing framework to accelerate LiDAR bundle adjustment for large-scale mapping, incorporating three key techniques. First, we design an adaptive, asynchronous data loading strategy to efficiently process large-scale point cloud datasets on memory-constrained GPUs. Secondly, we present a novel bottom-up voxelization method for extracting planar features, enabling fully parallelized pre-processing. Thirdly, we build upon a majorization-minimization formulation to accelerate compute-intensive tasks in the optimization via parallel computation, including the computation of residuals, Jacobian and Hessian matrices, and a parallel increment solver. To support our design, we provide both theoretical and experimental analysis of the time complexity of our approach. Extensive benchmarking on large-scale public datasets across various computational platforms validates the robustness and adaptability of our approach, achieving up to a tenfold improvement in computational efficiency while preserving mapping accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art methods. To benefit future research, the implementation code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [19] [PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5: A Toolkit for Robot Process Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14284)
*Yuyang Liu,Yanqing Shen,Ruike Chen,Jifan Zhao,Yuxuan Tian,Yichi Zhang,Tianfeng Long,Zixuan Yin,Yipu Wang,Ziheng Qin,Wenxing Tan,Yang Shi,Mingyu Cao,Runze Xiao,Ziqi Wang,Zhixin Yin,Shiwei Chu,Yi-Fan Zhang,Yao Mu,Yuheng Ji,Yihao Wang,Jun Yan,Zhongyuan Wang,Pengwei Wang,Xiaolong Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5，一个用于机器人过程评估的工具包，通过将滚动视频转化为密集进度曲线，衍生出多个精细指标，超越传统的二元成功率和基于规则的过程评分，并提供RoboPulse++评估PRM的可靠性，以及完整的评估套件支持可复现的操纵过程评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人评估方法主要依赖二元成功率和基于规则的过程评分，无法细致刻画具身模型在多步骤操作过程中的失败位置、恢复行为和执行质量，缺乏对过程层面能力的全面理解。

Method: PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5构建在1.0版本基础上，引入三个新指标：失败侧进度（characterize failure-side progress）、回撤后恢复（post-drawdown recovery）、成功侧执行质量（success-side execution quality）。通过将基准测试的滚动视频转化为密集进度曲线，对具身模型进行综合评估；同时引入RoboPulse++平台评估过程奖励模型（PRM）的可靠性，并发布包含基准、指标实现和可视化工具的完整评估套件。

Result: 基于基准测试的滚动视频对多种具身模型进行了全面评估，提供了精细指标结果和关键发现；RoboPulse++为PRM的可靠性提供了更准确的测试平台，评估套件的发布支持了可复现的操纵过程评估。

Conclusion: 本文呼吁社区重新思考机器人评估方式，将透明、过程化和可复现的评估作为下一代具身智能的基础，推动从单纯的结果判断向过程理解的范式转变。

Abstract: Fine-grained robotic evaluation matters for understanding embodied models, going beyond binary success rates and rule-based process scores. We present PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5, a toolkit for robot process assessment that turns rollout videos into dense progress curves and derives multiple fine metrics. PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5 introduces three metrics, building on version 1.0, that characterize failure-side progress, post-drawdown recovery, and success-side execution quality, helping users understand embodied model capability. Based on the rollout videos from benchmarks, we perform a comprehensive assessment of the embodied models, providing some fine-grained metric results and key findings. We further introduce RoboPulse++ to evaluate the reliability of process reward models (PRM), providing evaluators with a more accurate testing platform. Moreover, we release a user-friendly assessment suite, including the benchmark, metric implementation, and visualization tools, to support reproducible manipulation process evaluation. We call on the community to rethink how robots are evaluated and establish transparent, procedural, and reproducible assessment as a foundation for the next generation of embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [20] [CORAL: Curriculum-Optimized Reward Adaptation for LiDAR-Based Goal-Directed Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14332)
*Anisa Saleem,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORAL提出了一种用于自动驾驶长时域目标导向导航的分阶段课程学习与阶段感知奖励机制，通过五阶段课程和动态奖励权重结合多流actor-critic网络，在CARLA仿真中显著超越PPO基线，并实现零样本跨城镇泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在城市自动驾驶中很有前景，但长时域目标导向导航要求策略同时获得多种竞争性行为（到达远处目标、跟踪路线、避障、遵守信号灯），而固定目标函数无法规定学习这些行为的顺序，导致训练效率低下。

Method: CORAL将两个调度机制同步推进：(1) 五阶段课程，逐步延长路线并收紧行为约束；(2) 阶段感知奖励，其组件权重从任务进展逐渐转向路线跟随、安全性、平滑性和规则遵守。策略为多流actor-critic网络，使用PPO在CARLA中训练，输入是99维紧凑状态（极坐标LiDAR直方图、车辆遥测、自车坐标系路线几何、交通规则指示），无需点云编码器或鸟瞰图光栅化。

Result: 在最长路线和完整行为约束下，CORAL在20个评估回合中全部到达目标，而两个PPO基线仅达到5%和10%；因析消融表明单独任一调度都不及两者结合，移除任一调度降低成功率与路线完成率，同时禁用两者成功率降至55%。在单一城镇训练后，策略零样本迁移到7个未见城镇，在100-150米同长度路线上成功率为68-98%，平均横向偏差低于0.35米。

Conclusion: 课程学习与阶段感知奖励的协同组合是解决长时域目标导向驾驶中行为竞争问题的关键，且紧凑状态表征足以支撑高效训练与良好的跨城镇泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is promising for autonomous urban driving, but long-horizon goal-directed navigation asks a policy to acquire several competing behaviors at once--reaching a distant goal, tracking a route, avoiding obstacles, obeying signals--and a fixed objective gives no order in which to learn them. This paper presents CORAL, which advances two schedules together: a five-stage curriculum that progressively lengthens routes and tightens behavioral constraints, and a stage-aware reward whose component weights shift emphasis from mission progress toward route following, safety, smoothness, and rule compliance as the task hardens. The policy is a multi-stream actor-critic network trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in CARLA on a compact 99-dimensional state pairing a polar LiDAR histogram with vehicle telemetry, ego-frame route geometry, and traffic-rule indicators--no point-cloud encoder, no bird's-eye-view rasterization. Against two PPO baselines under an identical protocol, CORAL reaches the goal in all twenty evaluation episodes on the longest routes under the full set of behavioral constraints, where the baselines reach 5% and 10%; a factorial ablation shows that neither schedule alone matches their combination: removing either lowers both success and route completion, and disabling both drops success to 55%. Trained in one town, the policy transfers zero-shot to seven unseen towns, succeeding in 68-98% of episodes on routes of the same 100-150 m length, with mean lateral deviation below 0.35 m.

</details>


### [21] [Reflex: Enabling Fast and Predictive Vision-Language-Action Models for Reaction-Critical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14379)
*Yuxuan Chen,Wanruo Zhang,Xiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReflexBench基准测试（包含六个动态操作任务，支持可配置延迟的同步/异步推理评估）和ReflexVLA模型（通过潜在未来预测和多帧时序融合增强时序推理，利用批处理视觉编码和CUDA Graph回放降低部署延迟），在动态操作和静态基准上均表现良好，并经真实世界实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA基准主要评估静态操作任务的泛化能力，忽视了机器人控制中至关重要的动态交互场景（如反应关键性操作）。这一缺口导致现有模型在需要快速反应的真实场景中缺乏系统性评估与专门优化。

Method: 1) 构建ReflexBench：包含六个动态任务，并提出将仿真器步进与机器人控制解耦的评估框架，支持同步/异步推理下的可配置延迟测试。2) 提出ReflexVLA：在视觉骨干中引入潜在未来预测和多帧时序融合以增强时序推理；通过批处理视觉编码和CUDA Graph回放减少部署延迟，且无需大规模机器人数据预训练。

Result: ReflexVLA在动态操作任务上持续获得性能提升，同时在标准静态操作基准上保持有竞争力的精度；真实世界实验验证了其在实际部署条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: ReflexBench填补了动态反应关键型操作评测的空白，ReflexVLA证明了无需大规模预训练数据即可通过架构优化和延迟优化实现高效的反应关键型操作，为VLA模型在动态场景中的落地提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently achieved promising performance in robotic manipulation. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate generalization on static manipulation tasks and largely overlook dynamic interaction scenarios. To address this gap, we present ReflexBench, a benchmark for reaction-critical manipulation. ReflexBench contains six dynamic tasks and introduces an evaluation framework that decouples simulator stepping from robot control while supporting configurable latency under synchronous and asynchronous inference. Building upon ReflexBench, we propose ReflexVLA, an efficient VLA model designed for reaction-critical manipulation without large-scale robot-data pretraining. ReflexVLA enhances temporal reasoning through latent future prediction and multi-frame temporal fusion within the vision backbone, while reducing deployment latency through batched visual encoding and CUDA Graph replay. Experiments show that ReflexVLA consistently improves dynamic manipulation performance while maintaining competitive accuracy on standard static manipulation benchmarks, and real-world experiments further demonstrate its effectiveness under practical deployment conditions. Project website: https://reflexvla.github.io

</details>


### [22] [Effect of Twisted-Yarn Architecture on Pressure and Proximity Sensing Characteristics of Textile Capacitive Sensors for Robotic Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14406)
*Ishtia Zahir,Eslam Saleh,Maryam Rezayati,Güunter Grabher,Gaffar Hossain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于银包覆纱线和PDMS涂层、组装成一/二/四层加捻结构的纺织电容传感平台，系统研究了纱线层级架构对电容传感特性的影响，实现了压力和近距感知的可调设计。四层传感器在100 kHz下灵敏度达0.1331 MPa⁻¹，具有低迟滞、低热漂移和15000次循环稳定性；近距探测范围为40-60 mm，并通过4×4传感阵列和机械臂集成验证了实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纺织集成电容传感器虽具有柔性和共形触觉传感优势，但纱线层级架构对电容换能特性的影响尚未被定量表征，缺乏架构-性能关系的系统性理解，限制了传感器性能的优化设计。

Method: 采用银包覆纱线涂覆PDMS，构建单层、双层和四层加捻构型。系统研究有效电极重叠面积和纤维间间距对电容响应的影响；通过局部单纤维接触面积计算压力；测试各构型的力学性能、灵敏度、迟滞、热稳定性和循环稳定性，并组装4×4传感阵列及集成到机械臂进行实时触觉/近距检测。

Result: 层数增加使断裂伸长率从37.5%提升至85.0%，最大载荷从23.3 N增至89.7 N。四层传感器在100 kHz下灵敏度最高达0.1331 MPa⁻¹，具有低迟滞、25-90°C低热漂移和15000次循环稳定运行。近距探测范围随层数增加从60 mm降至40 mm，呈现架构依赖的灵敏度-距离权衡。4×4阵列实现空间接触映射，机械臂集成端到端延迟为403 ms。

Conclusion: 纱线架构（层数、加捻构型）是控制纺织电容传感系统测量特性的可调设计参数，通过架构调控可实现灵敏度-探测范围权衡与性能优化，为穿戴电子和人机交互中的纺织传感器设计提供了系统的设计准则。

Abstract: Textile-integrated capacitive sensors offer flexible and conformable tactile sensing for wearable electronics and human-robot interaction; however, the influence of yarn-level architecture on capacitive transduction characteristics remains insufficiently quantified. This work presents a textile capacitive sensing platform based on silver-coated yarns coated with polydimethylsiloxane and assembled into one-, two-, and four-layer twisted configurations. The influence of effective electrode overlap area and inter-fiber separation on the capacitive response is systematically investigated, enabling architecture-dependent tuning of pressure and proximity sensing characteristics. Pressure was calculated using the localized single-fiber contact area, corresponding to stresses of 0.4-3.9 MPa. Increasing the layer number improved mechanical strength and sensing performance: elongation at break increased from 37.5% to 62.5% and 85.0%, while the maximum load increased from 23.3 to 42.7 and 89.7 N. Sensitivity increased with layer number and frequency, reaching 0.1331 MPa$^{-1}$ for the four-layer sensor at 100 kHz. The four-layer configuration also exhibited low hysteresis, minimal thermal drift from 25 to 90 $^\circ$C, and stable operation over 15,000 cycles. Proximity detection ranges of 60, 50, and 40 mm were obtained for the one-, two-, and four-layer sensors, respectively, revealing an architecture-dependent sensitivity-range trade-off. A 4$\times$4 textile sensing array enabled spatial contact mapping, while robotic-arm integration demonstrated real-time touch and proximity detection with an end-to-end robotic system latency (from detection to robot reaction) of 403 ms. The results establish yarn architecture as a tunable design parameter governing the measurement characteristics of textile-integrated capacitive sensing systems.

</details>

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种控制信息驱动的在线轨迹规划框架，通过实时测量系统性跟踪偏差并动态调整空间约束，在保持25ms中位计算时间的前提下，使自主赛车圈速平均降低1.8秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主赛车在车辆动力学极限下运行，小控制误差即导致安全关键行为和性能损失。传统轨迹规划器假设完美跟踪、对执行误差无感知，只能通过保守的空间裕量保证安全，导致可用赛道空间未被充分利用。

Method: 提出控制信息注入的在线轨迹规划框架：在运行时测量系统性跟踪偏差，据此动态调整空间赛道约束条件，并迭代扩展自由空间规划区域。规划器在保持时间最优的同时补偿累积执行误差。

Result: 在高保真闭环仿真环境中验证，该方法在不增加计算负担（中位运行时间25ms）的前提下，将圈速降低1.8秒。

Conclusion: 将控制引起的偏差反馈至规划层，可解锁模块化架构中原先无法获取的性能空间，使自主车辆能够系统性利用赛道极限。

Abstract: Autonomous racecars operate at the limits of vehicle dynamics, where small control errors translate into safety-critical behavior and lost performance. Trajectory planners assume perfect tracking and remain blind to execution errors. To guarantee safety, trajectory planners therefore restrict themselves to conservative spatial margins, leaving usable track space untapped. To overcome these issues, we introduce a control-informed online trajectory planning framework that learns from its own execution errors. By measuring systematic tracking deviations during runtime, we dynamically adapt spatial track constraints and iteratively expand the free-space planning area. The planner remains time-optimal while compensating for accumulated execution errors. This method was analyzed in a high-fidelity closed-loop simulation environment with autonomous racecars. The results demonstrate that our approach reduces lap time by 1.8\,s without increasing computational burden, maintaining a median runtime of 25 ms. Our finding indicates that feeding control-induced deviations back into the planning layer unlocks performance previously inaccessible to modular architectures and enables autonomous vehicles to exploit track limits systematically.

</details>

Result: 该平台的机器人无关架构使其可适配多种机器人本体，降低了成本并提高了可扩展性；虚拟现实游戏结合自适应反馈机制为儿童提供个性化且具有激励性的治疗效果，为居家持续康复提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: THRIVE通过模块化设计和机器人无关架构，为儿童上肢运动障碍提供了一种可负担、可扩展且易于居家持续使用的康复方案，有望提高康复训练的依从性和效果。

Abstract: This paper presents THRIVE (Therapeutic Humanoid Robot In Virtual Environment), an at-home rehabilitation platform that integrates a suite of virtual-reality upper-body rehabilitation games, a real-time camera-based motion-tracking system, and a socially interactive robot therapist. The system is designed for therapy and intervention in children with upper-limb motor impairments, which can be improved through consistent, task-specific practice. THRIVE features a set of newly designed, engaging games that target functional reaching, grasping, and object-manipulation movements through customizable popping, hitting, catching, and grabbing tasks, while the camera-based tracking system captures the child's kinematic performance during play. A robot therapist - deployable either as a physical robotic coach or as a remote-presence virtual agent - delivers adaptive, dynamic feedback to motivate the child and guide their movements toward therapeutic goals. THRIVE decouples the therapeutic games from the robot embodiment, extending the platform to support various embodiments and different robots within one modular system. This robot-agnostic design makes THRIVE affordable, scalable, and readily adaptable for sustained use in the home, offering a practical pathway to more consistent and engaging upper-limb therapy for children with motor function impairments.

</details>


### [25] [Expected Free Energy-based Informative Path Planning for Robotic Mars Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14466)
*Ajith Anil Meera,Pablo Lanillos,Wouter Kouw*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将主动推理中的期望自由能（EFE）作为统一准则，用于受预算约束的机器人信息性路径规划，使自主机器人在探索未知环境时能同时实现信息地图构建和高价值区域搜索，并满足路径长度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在未知环境中探索（如火星寻找水源）面临双重需求：构建精确的信息地图与快速找到最大价值区域。经典的信息搜索准则和奖励搜索准则各自只解决其中一个目标，无法同时兼顾两者，亟需一个统一准则来处理这种多目标问题。

Method: 将主动推理中的期望自由能（EFE）作为动作选择目标函数，将其用作预算受限的信息性路径规划的统一定义准则。使用高斯过程维护信息场的信念，在硬路径长度约束下规划连续轨迹，以最小化期望自由能。

Result: 多次实验实现表明，基于EFE的规划能够同时生成精确的后验地图并定位最高价值区域，在相同设置下优于信息论基线的性能。

Conclusion: EFE作为一种统一、易于调参、原则性的信息收集策略，能够促进自主机器人在保持效率和资源约束前提下的部署，为机器人探索领域提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: An autonomous robot efficiently exploring an unknown environment, such as looking for water sources on Mars, faces two simultaneous demands: building an accurate information map while quickly finding the regions of greatest value, and paying for every meter of travel and the cost of every measurement it takes. Classical information-seeking and reward-seeking criteria address only one of these objectives at a time. Here, we propose Expected Free Energy (EFE), the principled action-selection objective from active inference, as a unifying criterion for budgeted robotic informative path planning. Maintaining a Gaussian-process belief over the information field, our agent plans continuous trajectories that minimize expected free energy under hard path-length constraints. The results from multiple realizations show that EFE-based planning yields accurate posterior maps and locates the highest-value regions simultaneously, outperforming information-theoretic baselines under the same settings. In robotic exploration, these unified, easy-to-tune principled information-gathering strategies facilitate autonomous deployment while enforcing efficiency and resource constraints.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人系统在城市各区域广泛部署，复杂人机交互下的安全性仍是关键挑战。现有方法往往将安全视为隔离在单个软件模块内的静态约束，缺乏系统性危险分析与运行时执行之间的有效衔接。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，核心是危险知情安全包络。通过跨层安全转换过程，将安全约束贯穿符号、空间和动态三种世界模型，并与物理AI运行时框架自然对接，从而保证安全的城市移动。

Result: 展示了该表示方法与物理AI运行时框架的自然接口能力，能够在城市环境中保证安全移动（本文为摘要，未提供具体定量实验结果）。

Conclusion: 该方法通过跨层安全转换和危险知情安全包络，弥合了系统危险分析与运行时执行之间的鸿沟，为异构机器人在复杂城市环境中的安全部署提供了一个统一的理论和工程框架。

Abstract: As heterogeneous robotic systems deploy across diverse urban zones, maintaining safety amid complex human-robot interactions remains a critical challenge. We present a unified framework that bridges systematic hazard analysis and runtime enforcement using hazard-informed safety envelopes. Rather than treating safety as a static constraint isolated within individual software modules, we introduce a cross-layer safety transformation process spanning symbolic, spatial, and dynamic world models. We show how this representation naturally interfaces with physical AI runtime harnesses to guarantee safe urban mobility.

</details>


### [27] [Spatiotemporal Tube-Based Safety-Certificate for Autonomous Navigation of Articulated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14531)
*Mohd. Faizuddin Faruqui,Ratnangshu Das,Ravi Kumar L,Pushpak Jagtap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空管道的规划方法，用于铰接式车辆（如牵引半挂车、卡车/拖拉机拖车、牵引AGV及公路列车）的自主导航。该方法利用链接元件的运动学与摆动约束，为被驱动的牵引车生成安全的管道，并确保被牵引的拖车系列始终保持在道路走廊内，同时通过允许的修正量对管道进行调整，为给定路线提供路线安全证书，并在复杂路线仿真中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接式车辆是货运运输的主力，但其自主导航极具挑战性。它们的物理特性和运动约束在狭窄路线上机动时构成重大困难，亟需一种能够保证拖车系列不偏离道路走廊的规划方法。

Method: 提出基于时空管道（spatiotemporal tube）的规划方法。该方法利用链接元件的运动学关系和摆动（sway）约束，为被驱动的牵引车（prime mover）构建安全管道；随后使用允许的修正量（permissible corrections）修改时空管道，从而为车辆在给定路线上提供路线安全证书（route safety certificate）。

Result: 所提出的规划方法在复杂路线的卡车-拖车导航仿真中得到了验证，证明了该方法能够有效保证拖车系列保持在道路走廊内，并提供认证的路径规划。

Conclusion: 该时空管道方法为铰接式车辆的自主导航提供了一种可认证的路径规划框架，确保拖车不偏离道路走廊，适用于多种铰接式车辆类型，具有较高的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: Articulated vehicles are the workhorses of freight transportation, and their autonomous navigation is challenging. Their physical characteristics and motion constraints pose significant challenges in manoeuvring these vehicles on narrow routes. This paper presents a spatiotemporal tube-based approach to plan autonomous navigation of vehicles like tractor semi-trailers, truck/ tractor trailers, towing Automated Guided Vehicles (AGVs), and road trains. This planning approach provides a certified path plan for the truck or tractor, ensuring that the towed series of trailers always remains within the road corridor, limited by permissible corrections. The planning leverages the kinematics of the linked elements along with sway constraints to arrive at a safe tube for the actuated prime mover. We modify the spatiotemporal tube using permissible corrections to provide a route safety certificate to the vehicle for the given route. The proposed planning method is verified on a truck-trailer navigation simulation for a complex route.

</details>

### [28] [Equivariant Relative Sectional Category and Induced Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13663)
*Navnath Daundkar,Rekha Santhanam,Bittu Singh*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了G-映射的等变相对剖面范畴（equivariant relative sectional category），建立了其基本同伦性质（比较、乘积、合成不等式以及定义域和陪域变化下的行为），并将其应用于发展等变的映射拓扑复杂度和等变Lusternik–Schnirelmann范畴，提供了若干示例说明这些不变量推广了经典等变概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对剖面范畴为多个数值同伦不变量（如Lusternik–Schnirelmann范畴、映射的拓扑复杂度、同伦距离）提供了统一框架，但该理论此前仅针对纤维化和一般映射，尚未在等变（G-空间）背景下发展，因此需要建立其等变类比。

Method: 在Gónzalez、Grant和Vandembroucq以及García-Calcines工作的基础上，形式化定义G-映射的等变相对剖面范畴，建立其基本同伦性质（比较、乘积、合成不等式，以及定义域和陪域变化下的行为），并以此统一框架推导出Scott和Murillo–Wu意义下的等变映射拓扑复杂度以及等变映射的Lusternik–Schnirelmann范畴，辅以示例验证。

Result: 成功建立了等变相对剖面范畴的基本理论框架，证明其满足相应的同伦不变量不等式性质；由此导出的等变映射拓扑复杂度和等变Lusternik–Schnirelmann范畴正确地推广了经典等变不变量，并通过具体示例进行了说明。

Conclusion: 等变相对剖面范畴为等变数值同伦不变量提供了统一且自然的框架，它不仅推广了已有的经典等变概念，还为后续在等变同伦论中研究更广泛的数值不变量奠定了基础。

Abstract: The relative sectional category, introduced by González, Grant, and Vandembroucq for fibrations and later extended by García-Calcines to arbitrary maps, provides a common framework encompassing several numerical homotopy invariants, including the Lusternik--Schnirelmann category, the topological complexity of a map, and homotopic distance. In this paper, we introduce and study the equivariant analogue of the relative sectional category for $G$-maps. We establish its fundamental homotopy-theoretic properties, including comparison, product, and composition inequalities, as well as its behavior under changes of domain and codomain. As applications, we introduce and investigate equivariant analogues of the topological complexity of a map, in the sense of Scott and Murillo--Wu, and the equivariant Lusternik--Schnirelmann category of a map. Several examples are provided to illustrate the theory and demonstrate that these invariants extend the corresponding classical equivariant notions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯同调计算是代数拓扑和计算几何中的核心问题。尽管已有多个成熟的软件系统支持单纯复形操作，但Wolfram语言生态系统中缺乏相应的支持工具。

Method: 开发了一个开源的Wolfram语言paclet（SimplicialHomology），支持构造抽象单纯复形并计算同调群，覆盖整数、有理数和有限域系数域，同时提供Betti数、自同构群以及join、star、link等多种构造功能。

Result: 提供了多个示例来演示该包的功能和性能，展示了其在单纯复形构造与同调计算方面的能力。

Conclusion: SimplicialHomology填补了Wolfram语言生态系统中单纯同调计算工具的空白，为代数拓扑和计算几何研究提供了新的开源方案。

Abstract: Computations in simplicial homology are crucial for algebraic topology and computational geometry in general. Although several mature software systems provide facilities for working with simplicial complexes, there is currently limited support for such computations within the Wolfram language ecosystem. This paper presents SimplicialHomology, an open-source Wolfram language paclet for constructing abstract simplicial complexes and computing their homology groups over the integers, rational numbers, and finite fields. Furthermore, the package supports many other functionalities such as calculating Betti numbers, automorphism groups, and other constructions such as joins, stars, links, etc. Several examples are presented to illustrate the package and its performance.

</details>


### [30] [Extending Goldberg's Exact Sequence to Braid Groups of Graphs and Simplicial Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14350)
*Byung Hee An*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究纯辫群（pure braid group）的结映射（strand map）的核，将Goldberg关于闭曲面的经典定理推广到任意有限连通单纯复形，给出了弱Goldberg性和Goldberg性的完全刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldberg（1973）定理刻画了闭曲面（除S^2和RP^2外）上纯辫群到各基点基本群乘积之结映射的核为某嵌入圆盘中纯辫的正规闭包。自然的推广问题是：这一描述在更一般的有限连通单纯复形上何时成立。

Method: 作者引入了"弱Goldberg"与"Goldberg"两个概念，并利用两个主要工具：（1）在X的一点处对构型空间进行"space-graph"分解（graph-of-spaces decomposition）；（2）一个约化程序，将任意单纯复形约化为简单模型。通过这些工具，对结映射的满射性、核的平凡性以及Goldberg性进行了完全分类。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）结映射满射当且仅当X不同构于S^1；（2）X弱Goldberg当且仅当其自由部分为森林；（3）X Goldberg当且仅当其承认"允许树"（admissible tree）——即与自由部分对厚分支的附着的边界和内部类型相容的脚手架极大树；（4）完全分类了核平凡的情形；（5）解决了例外曲面S^2和RP^2；（6）对流形和图给出了完整答案。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Goldberg定理在一般有限连通单纯复形上的完整推广，建立了结映射核结构与复形拓扑组合性质之间的精确对应关系，并覆盖了所有此前未解决的例外情形。

Abstract: For a finite connected simplicial complex $X$, the strand map $ι_\ast$, from $\mathbb{P}_n(X)$ to $\prod_{i=1}^nπ_1(X,x_i^0)$, sends a pure braid to the homotopy classes of its strands. A theorem of Goldberg (1973) computes its kernel when $X$ is a closed surface other than $S^2$ and $\mathbb{RP}^2$: the kernel is the normal closure of the pure braids supported in an embedded disc. We extend this picture to arbitrary finite connected simplicial complexes. Call $X$ $\textit{weakly Goldberg}$ if some contractible subcomplex $X_0\subseteq X$ realises Goldberg's description, $\kerι_\ast=\left\langle \operatorname{im}(\mathbb{P}_n(X_0)\to\mathbb{P}_n(X)) \right\rangle$, and $\textit{Goldberg}$ if $X_0$ can moreover be chosen so that $\mathbb{P}_n(X_0)\to\mathbb{P}_n(X)$ is injective. We prove that the strand map is surjective if and only if $X\not\cong S^1$; that $X$ is weakly Goldberg if and only if its free part is a forest; and that $X$ is Goldberg if and only if it admits an $\textit{admissible tree}$ -- a maximal tree of a scaffold, compatible with the boundary and interior types of the attachments of the free part to the thick components. We also classify the complexes for which the kernel is trivial, settle the exceptional surfaces $S^2$ and $\mathbb{RP}^2$, and obtain complete answers for manifolds and for graphs. The main tools are a graph-of-spaces decomposition of the configuration space at a point of $X$ and a resolution procedure reducing an arbitrary complex to a simple model.

The structural robustness of these phenomena is confirmed by incorporating effects such as waning immunity and isolation and extending the framework beyond the analytically tractable class. These findings provide a clear mechanistic explanation for multi-strain epidemic cycles and offer a highly tractable baseline for future theoretical developments.

Abstract: In social insects, mortality risk comprises distinct components that may not share the same predictors: lifespan duration, senescence trajectory, and thermal vulnerability. We tested these three axes in 18 Australian ant species using paired field-laboratory survival assays (2,363 cohort-day observations; 1,148 workers). Body size predicted duration (Cox HR = 0.67, p = 0.002), while colony size (p = 0.60) and the size x temperature interaction (p = 0.72) showed no detectable moderating effect. A weak but significant size x foraging-rate interaction was detected (LRT p = 0.014), suggesting that intrinsic physiology remains the most parsimonious explanation for the main size-longevity pattern, although ecological context may contribute. Senescence trajectory was associated with circadian niche rather than size: it was steepest in matinal species (Kruskal-Wallis p = 0.009; matinal vs. crepuscular p = 0.002) and was uncorrelated with body mass (Spearman p = 0.32). Thermal hazard plateaued above 20 degrees C (Delta AIC = -38; p < 0.001), with elevated thermal sensitivity in Rhytidoponera (Ectatomminae) above the plateau (5% per degree C, p = 0.015). Circadian regime and lineage identity, not body size, therefore emerge as the most climate-relevant axes, although they are strongly collinear (Cramer's V = 0.85). These results show that body size captures only one dimension of mortality risk and that size-based vulnerability indices may misrank taxa when senescence and thermal sensitivity are decoupled from body size.

</details>

Abstract: Extinction is inevitable; every species eventually dies out, impacting the ecosystem it is part of. Over the past few decades, extensive research stemming from the stability-diversity debate has addressed how species diversity contributes to the stability of large ecosystems. However, conventional stability criteria often rely on deterministic frameworks, overlooking the intrinsic population fluctuations that allow any species to go extinct by chance. In this paper, we incorporate demographic stochasticity into large complex ecosystems with a rule-based model. We demonstrate that such demographic fluctuations rapidly prune the low-abundance species from the ecological community, thereby securing higher systemic stability. By developing a bottom-up theory to characterise the statistics of extinction dynamics, we discover that the fraction of surviving species exhibits an anomalous heavy-tailed decay over time, revealing the emergence of remnant communities with robust metastability. Our results highlight that when demographic fluctuations are accounted for, ecosystems self-stabilise by reducing their diversity even when they are predicted to be chaotic in the deterministic limit.

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected solvable algebraic group acting rationally on a finite-dimensional vector space $U$. We apply the criterion to the action of $Aut_Q(M)$ on $Ext^1_Q(M,M)^*$ where $M$ is a multiplicity-free representation of a Dynkin quiver. Abstract: We investigate the collapse of rotating very massive and supermassive stellar cores using numerical relativity simulations including an alpha-chain nuclear reaction network and neutrino cooling. Our main survey focuses on newly constructed models with initial core masses of $2 \times {10}^{3}-5\times 10^4M_\odot$. The collapse is triggered either by pair instability in lower-mass cores or by general-relativistic instability in higher-mass cores. We find that higher-mass cores undergo a nearly homologous collapse, whereas lower-mass cores show a more runaway-like collapse because neutrino cooling becomes more efficient at their higher densities and temperatures. Consequently, the black hole formed in lower-mass models initially contains a smaller fraction of the core mass, and disk formation occurs while a larger amount of matter remains outside the black hole. The lower compactness of pair-unstable cores also allows larger dimensionless angular momentum, favoring the formation of rapidly rotating black holes and massive disks. The disk bounce drives mass ejection with ejecta masses of order $10-10^3M_\odot$ and kinetic energies of order $10^{53}-10^{55}\,\mathrm{erg}$. Significant $^{56}$Ni production in the disk-bounce ejecta occurs only in the lowest-mass models. For selected models, we further follow the viscous evolution of the disk and find that viscosity enhances the ejecta mass and kinetic energy. In models with $\lesssim10^4M_\odot$, the viscosity-driven ejecta can originate from disk matter that has reached nuclear statistical equilibrium and can therefore become rich in $^{56}$Ni. We present a systematic analysis of the flaring activity of the active RS CVn-type binary σ Gem, utilizing the first-year monitoring data from the Wide-field X-ray Telescope (WXT) aboard EP. Our goals are to demonstrate the unique capability of EP in monitoring stellar X-ray activity and detecting flares, by identifying and characterizing extreme X-ray flares on σ Gem and estimating their occurrence rate. Abstract: A tidal disruption event (TDE) occurs when a star approaches a black hole (BH) and is disrupted by its tidal forces. Although several hundred TDEs have been identified to date, only a small fraction are accompanied by relativistic jets. However, the origin of the magnetic flux and plasma needed to power BZ jets remain unclear. In this Letter, we propose a scenario in which magnetic flux is initially stored in a pre-existing low-Eddington accretion disk around BH, and is subsequently advected toward the BH by the super-Eddington accretion flow formed after the stellar disruption. We show that stars with low densities, such as red giants, can supply sufficient magnetic flux to power a BZ jet. Once sufficient magnetic flux accumulates near the BH, an equatorial current sheet forms where magnetic reconnection produces high-energy gamma rays. We find that photon--photon pair production by these gamma rays supplies the BH magnetosphere with sufficient plasma to launch and sustain a BZ jet. We further show that this mechanism simultaneously provides enough radiating particles to account for the observed prompt emission.

The surface of the OBF has accumulated a layer of contamination over the course of the mission, as it is the coldest surface exposed to the interior to the spacecraft. The analyses of the E0102, Abell 1795, and Mrk 421 data show that the current model of the contamination adequately predicts the additional absorption through mid-2026.

Abstract: We investigate the velocity structure of the intracluster medium (ICM) in the core of the relaxed cool-core cluster Abell 2029 using XRISM Resolve spectroscopy. We analyze combined XRISM Resolve observations and divide the central region into several subregions. To account for photon mixing caused by the XRISM point spread function, we perform a spatial-spectral mixing analysis. We detect an ordered line-of-sight bulk-velocity gradient across the cluster core: the northern regions are blueshifted relative to the brightest cluster galaxy (BCG), while the southern regions are close to zero velocity or slightly redshifted. The maximum velocity difference is about $280~{\rm km\,s^{-1}}$. In contrast, the turbulent velocity dispersion is smaller, with measured values and upper limits of $\lesssim150~{\rm km\,s^{-1}}$, implying a non-thermal pressure fraction below $\sim2.5\%$. The velocity pattern is consistent with gas sloshing associated with the spiral structure seen in Chandra X-ray images. Averaged over all regions, the inferred turbulent heating rate is below the radiative cooling rate, indicating that turbulent dissipation alone is insufficient to offset cooling in the entire core. These results reveal that A2029 is not kinematically featureless: sloshing-induced bulk motions are present, while the observed line-of-sight velocity dispersion indicates only a limited contribution to pressure support and core heating.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The search for Galactic sources capable of accelerating cosmic rays (CRs) to PeV energies has advanced significantly in recent years. High-energy observatories such as LHAASO have detected extended gamma-ray halos around several sources, suggesting that CRs escape their acceleration sites through anomalously slow diffusion. Theoretical studies propose that magnetic mirror diffusion combined with pitch-angle scattering in turbulent flow can naturally suppress CR transport. Here, we first show how mirror diffusion combined with scattering suppresses cosmic-ray transport, leading to an energy-dependent diffusion coefficient $D(E)\propto E^{1/3}$. We then combine a 3D magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) simulation of a young massive stellar cluster (YMSC) with Monte Carlo CR propagation calculations (CRPropa). The model includes the background gas density, magnetic field, stellar blackbody and dust emission, the cosmic microwave background, and the Galactic interstellar radiation field. Using the YMSC W43 as a benchmark, we compare two CR injection geometries: a central source and a spherical shell representing the cluster's collective wind shock. We show that mirror+scattering diffusion $D(E)\propto E^{1/3}$, combined with a CR injection spectrum $E^{-2}$, reproduces the gamma-ray spectrum observed by Fermi and LHAASO. In contrast, stronger energy-dependent diffusion requires a harder CR injection spectrum, $\sim E^{-1.6}$, to match the data. The relative contributions of leptonic inverse-Compton and hadronic emission depend sensitively on the diffusion regime. Finally, the resulting spectra show little dependence on the CR injection location, aside from a lower injection luminosity in the central-source case. Overall, our results indicate that the observed gamma-ray emission is shaped primarily by the diffusive propagation regime rather than by the precise location of the CR source.

</details>

### [58] [The Sharp Dimension Bound in the Johnson--Lindenstrauss Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13782)
*Vishesh Jain*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Johnson--Lindenstrauss引理中Larsen和Nelson所猜想的最优目标维度下界，即目标维度为Θ(min{d, n-1, log(2+ε²n)/ε²})，且该上界可由线性映射实现，而下界对非线性嵌入同样成立。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: Johnson--Lindenstrauss引理中的目标维度在全参数范围内已被精确刻画，且线性映射即可达到最优维度，这为降维理论和相关应用领域提供了坚实的最优性保证。

Abstract: The Johnson--Lindenstrauss lemma asserts that every set of $n$ points in $d$-dimensional Euclidean space embeds into $O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log n)$-dimensional Euclidean space with distortion at most $1+\varepsilon$. Larsen and Nelson conjectured that the optimal target dimension throughout the full range of the parameters $n,d, \varepsilon$ is \[ Θ\left(\min\left\{d,n-1,\frac{\log(2+\varepsilon^2n)}{\varepsilon^2}\right\}\right). \] We resolve this conjecture in the affirmative. In fact, we prove the stronger statement that the upper bound is attained by a linear map. The matching lower bound, due to Larsen--Nelson and Alon--Klartag, holds even for nonlinear embeddings.

As we show here, even when these conditions are not fulfilled, the point group still fixes, at a given geometry, momentum transfers at which the intensity vanishes for every Hamiltonian that has the symmetry of the cluster. We call such momentum transfers extinctions and derive an equation whose every zero is an extinction, built from the symmetry species of the two levels and from the positions and scattering amplitudes of the magnetic sites. The extinctions follow in closed form in two cases: when each orbit of symmetry-equivalent sites contributes a single occurrence of the mediating species, and when the site phases recur under a subgroup up to one common factor.

Abstract: We present the first application of simulation-based inference to resonant inelastic X-ray scattering spectroscopy. Using truncated marginal neural ratio estimation to efficiently restrict the prior and conditional flow matching as the joint density estimator, we infer full posteriors with a modest simulation budget for two Ni$^{2+}$ compounds---NiPS$_3$ as a representative covalent case and K$_2$NiF$_4$ as a more atomic one. Applying the validated method to experimental NiPS$_3$ and K$_2$NiF$_4$ data, we recover a joint posterior that reveals parameter correlations invisible to point estimators, and a posterior predictive distribution that closely matches the observed spectrum. Abstract: The microscopic origin of anomalous orbital polarization in a class of low-dimensional correlated oxides remains unresolved due to the competition among crystal-field effects, electronic correlations, and orbital-dependent dimensionality. Using dynamical mean-field theory, we investigate a two orbital Hubbard model with orbital-dependent dimensionality and reveal the mechanism for anomalous orbital polarization through orbital reconstruction beyond the bare crystal-field picture. We identify interorbital Coulomb interaction-induced charge competition as a key microscopic mechanism responsible for the orbital redistribution, which leads first to an orbital-polarized correlated metal and subsequently to an orbital-polarized Mott insulator. We further find that Hund's coupling acts as a filling-dependent regulator of orbital reconstruction. It weakens the correlation-induced orbital redistribution at quarter filling by competing with interorbital charge fluctuations, while at half filling it completely suppresses the orbital-polarized state by stabilizing high-spin orbital-balanced configurations. These results provide a unified picture of correlation driven orbital reconstruction and highlight the relevance of interorbital interactions and Hund's coupling for understanding orbital phenomena in low-dimensional transition-metal oxides.
