Abstract: How many Slater determinants does an accurate variational description of interacting fermions require? Exact expansions in a finite basis need combinatorially many, and state-of-the-art fermionic neural quantum states stack growing numbers of them. We prove that, in the norms that govern variational calculations, at most two are needed, independently of the number of particles and of the target accuracy. A single universal Slater determinant-specified in advance, independent of both the system and the state-multiplied by a smooth bosonic wave function approximates any fermionic wave function in up to three spatial dimensions in the first-order Sobolev norm, which controls the variational energy. Reaching the second-order Sobolev norm-for Coulomb interactions, the domain of the Hamiltonian, which bounds the variance of the local energy at the core of variational Monte Carlo-requires at most one additional fixed determinant, and only in three dimensions. Antisymmetry therefore costs at most two universal determinants and no expressiveness: generalized Slater-Jastrow neural quantum states are variationally complete.

</details>

Abstract: The conventional self-dual lines of the generalized Baxter-Wu (GBW) model with asymmetric complex couplings are known to be $\sinh(2K)=\pm \cos(2φ)$, where $K$ and $φ$ are the real and imaginary parts of the coupling. Simulations indicate that the self-dual lines at the partition-function minima $φ_{\mathcal{Z}_{\min}}=(2n+1)π/8$ constitute a critical threshold. They are genuine critical boundaries for $|K| \ge \frac{1}{2}\operatorname{arsinh}(\cos(π/4)) \approx 0.32924$, while for smaller $|K|$ they are not. We further clarify the capability and limitations of the average sign and its derivatives for detecting phase transitions. In particular, the negative peak of the average sign at $φ_{\mathcal{Z}_{\min}}$ does not correspond to a genuine phase transition. Abstract: The Nernst effect, which enables the conversion of a heat current into a transverse voltage under magnetic field or spin magnetization, holds significant promise for energy harvesting and thermal management in future electronics. However, the conventional Nernst effect is fundamentally constrained by the orthogonality requirement that the applied field or spontaneous magnetization must be perpendicular to the plane defined by the temperature gradient and the induced voltage. Here we report that symmetry-tailored ultrathin films of a prototypical ferromagnetic oxide exhibit anomalous Nernst effect arising from intrinsic coupling to spontaneous in-plane spin magnetization. Systematic magnetothermoelectric measurements under spherical rotations of the magnetic field reveal that a pronounced Nernst signal, comparable in magnitude to the out-of-plane response, emerges robustly associated with out-of-plane orbital magnetization. The resulting flat-band Kondo problem is a quantum geometric molecule, with an algebraic Kondo scale set by the total projected hybridization strength rather than a logarithmic-renormalization scale. In real space, the impurity-bath spin correlation defines a quantum geometric Kondo cloud. Its cloud-size tensor admits a gauge-invariant decomposition into a hybridization-weighted quantum metric, a dressed Berry-connection covariance, and a positive hybridization-gradient term, yielding the lower bound $ξ_{\rm K}^2\geq \sum_{\mathbf{k}}ρ(\mathbf{k}){\rm Tr}\,g(\mathbf{k})$, where $ρ(\mathbf{k})=|v(\mathbf{k})|^2/\sum_{\mathbf{k}}|v(\mathbf{k})|^2$. Our result reveals that, in flat bands, Kondo screening is governed by the quantum geometry and interference structure of the impurity-selected Bloch wave packet, rather than Fermi-surface kinematics.

These results sharpen our understanding of these emergent exotic topological phases and quantum criticality, and provide useful guidance to explore them further.

The resulting $c$-axis vs. temperature phase diagram mirrors that of the one-band Hubbard model, but its critical end-point spawns an orbital Widom line, rooted in an anomalous compressibility of orbital, rather than charge occupation. Abstract: Hund's coupling $J$ induces fundamentally different correlation effects from Hubbard $U$. We propose a quantitative framework to capture low-energy effectiveness through two correlation factors: $z_L$ for low-energy quasiparticle renormalization and $z_H$ for high-energy charge fluctuations, newly introduced in this study. The discrepancy between $z_L$ and $z_H$ reflects the Hund character of the correlation. By establishing a one-to-one correspondence between correlation factors and the spin and charge susceptibilities, we identify the spin-degree-of-freedom effectiveness as the microscopic origin of low-energy effectiveness. Our framework, validated across multiorbital models and real materials, provides a universal and quantitative measure of Hundness.

Abstract: Twisted transition metal dichalcogenides offer an exceptionally tunable moiré platform for studying correlation physics beyond conventional condensed matter systems. In particular, the intriguing interplay between the displacement field and the twist angle remains to be fully resolved. In this paper, we use large-scale density matrix renormalization group simulations to study the minimal moiré Hubbard model on a triangular lattice at half-filling, where the displacement field effect is captured by a spin-dependent staggered flux. We find that the displacement field significantly enriches the triangular Hubbard phase diagram in several qualitative ways. It rapidly destabilizes the chiral spin liquid phase beyond a narrow weak-field regime, induces pronounced chiral correlations in the strong-coupling $120^\circ$-antiferromagnetic phase, and stabilizes incommensurate spin-density wave phases at weaker coupling. We further find signatures of a continuous transition between the chiral spin liquid and chiral antiferromagnetic phases at a finite displacement field, potentially driven by spinon condensation. Our results uncover rich displacement-field-driven many-body physics and provide useful guidance for future experiments in moiré superlattice systems.

Abstract: Let $y^2 = f(x,T)$ be a hyperelliptic curve of genus $g\geq 1$, defined over $\mathbb{Q}(T)$. We begin by constructing such curves and prove the rank using two methods. First, we apply the generalized Nagao's conjecture, which relates the first moment and the rank of the Jacobian variety $J_\mathcal{X}(\mathbb{Q}(T))$, and that the conjecture holds for our curves, making the result unconditional. Abstract: We prove unconditionally that at least two thirds of the nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function, counted with multiplicity, are simple and lie on the critical line, and that at least five sixths are distinct; the previous unconditional records are $\frac{5}{12}$ and $0.6603$. With the Montgomery--Taylor window the constants become $0.6725$ and $0.8362$. The argument makes Montgomery's 1973 deduction unconditional: the Riemann hypothesis, classically needed to read the zero side as a positive sum over real ordinates, is replaced by a rank-trace inequality applied to a finite compression of Weil's Hermitian form, with Sylvester's law of inertia handling off-line pairs. The analytic inputs are those of Aryan and of Baluyot, Goldston, Suriajaya and Turnage-Butterbaugh. The results extend to primitive Dirichlet $L$-functions and are formally verified in Lean 4.

</details>

Result: 成功证明了Komatsu和Pandey提出的Conjectures 7.1和7.5，即针对两类连续奇整数族的$p$-Frobenius问题猜想成立。

Conclusion: 两类连续奇整数族的$p$-Frobenius问题猜想得到完整证明，且证明了高斯多项式的对称性与单峰性是统一处理这两类问题的高效工具。

Abstract: We prove the $p$-Frobenius problems proposed as Conjectures 7.1 and 7.5 developed by T. Komatsu and R. Pandey (Bull. Korean Math. Soc. 2025;62:1397--1409.) for two families of consecutive odd integers. For integers $r,L,n\ge0$, the bounded restricted partition function $p_{\le r}^{(\le L)}(\le n)$ counts partitions of $n$ into at most $r$ parts, each at most $L$. Thus the bounded restricted partition functions $p_{\le 3}^{(\le a)}(\le s)$and $p_{\le 3}^{(\le a+1)}(\le s)$ play central roles in the proofs. Their generating functions are Gaussian polynomials, whose symmetry and unimodality provide a common tool for treating both families.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对每个素数p≥7证明了R(π)在主属上的迹等于显式lift贡献（由权重2和4的椭圆newform的Atkin-Lehner特征空间决定）与权重3 paramodular非lift空间的带符号缺陷之和；得到了Fricke符号偏差d(p)≥0的闭式公式；在11≤p≤149范围内验证了特征值符号细化猜想，其中p=19首次验证了type-Va对的R(π)分离，p=61实现了一般型因子x+7。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Brandt算子与Atkin-Lehner对合在Richelot图上的精确迹公式，深化了对超特殊阿贝尔曲面同源图的理解，并为Ibukiyama主属多重性猜想提供了具有R(π)符号信息的精细化版本和计算验证。

Abstract: Let $B_2(2)$ be the degree-2 Brandt operator on the principal genus of binary quaternion Hermitian lattices of discriminant $p$. Geometrically, $B_2(2)$ is the weighted adjacency operator of the Richelot $(2,2)$-isogeny graph on superspecial principally polarized abelian surfaces, and it commutes with an Atkin-Lehner involution $R(π)$. We prove a structural formula for the trace of $R(π)$ on the principal genus. For every prime $p \ge 7$, this trace is the sum of an explicit lift contribution, determined by the Atkin-Lehner eigenspaces of elliptic newforms of weights 2 and 4, and a signed defect of the weight-3 paramodular non-lift space. The proof compares term by term the closed principal- and non-principal-genus trace evaluations of Ibukiyama. The resulting closed formula for the signed defect yields the Fricke-sign bias $d(p) \ge 0$ for every prime.
  We next formulate an eigenvalue-sign refinement of Ibukiyama's principal-genus multiplicity conjectures. It predicts a factorization of $\mathrm{charpoly}\, B_2(2)$ into Eisenstein, Saito-Kurokawa, opposite-sign Yoshida, type-Va, and general-type blocks, and specifies the $R(π)$-sign on each block. In particular, it predicts that the two members of every type-Va pair are separated by opposite $R(π)$-eigenvalues.
  Finally, exact-arithmetic certificates verify this prediction for every prime $11 \le p \le 149$. The verification constructs the Richelot matrices, checks their weighted graph structure, matches the lift blocks with separately computed elliptic data, and certifies the signed isotypic multiplicities by projector traces. At $p = 19$ the first verified type-Va pair is separated by $R(π)$; at $p = 61$ the graph realizes the general-type factor $x + 7$.

</details>

Abstract: We bound the total variation distance in the Kubilius model for sequences with positive level of distribution. We obtain a result that we expect is qualitatively optimal. In the classical case considered by Kubilius, our theorem gives a simple proof of the optimal bound discovered by Tenenbaum, up to factors of $x^{o(1)}$ and $u^{o(u)}$.

*Pascal Jelinek*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前La Bretèche、Stoll和Tennenbaum证明了两个乘法无关基数的数字和函数比值在Q^+中稠密，Spiegelhofer及其合作者仅在特例中证明了s_2(n)/s_3(n)=1有无穷多解以及(s_2(n),s_3(n))几乎取遍N^2的所有值。本文旨在将这一结果推广到任意一对乘法无关基数，并填补乘法相关情形的空白。

Result: 对任意乘法无关的基数对(p,q)，比值s_p(n)/s_q(n)能无限多次取到每一个正有理数。对于乘法相关的情形也得到了相应的结果，从而对两个基数的情况给出了完整的分类描述。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了两个基数情形下数字和函数比值取值的完整刻画问题，将此前仅对特定基数成立的结果推广到了一般情形，并补充了乘法相关情形的分析，使该问题在两个基数情况下得到闭合解决。

Abstract: In 2019 La Bretèche, Stoll and Tennenbaum showed that the ratio of the sum of digits function $s_{q_1}(n)/s_{q_2}(n)$ of two multiplicatively independent bases $q_1$ and $q_2$ is dense in $\mathbb{Q}^+$. Recently Spiegelhofer proved that in the special case $s_2(n)/s_3(n)=1$ we have infinitely many solutions. Spiegelhofer extended this jointly with Drmota to show that the pair $(s_2(n),s_3(n))$ attains almost every value of $\mathbb{N}^2$ and hence, in particular, that every rational ratio is attained infinitely many times.\\ In this paper we show that, indeed, for any pair of multiplicatively independent bases $p$ and $q$, that the ratio attains every rational number infinitely many times. We also study this problem in the multiplicatively dependent case, hence giving a complete characterisation in the case of 2 bases.

</details>

Abstract: A classical class number relation of Hurwitz expresses the Fourier coefficients of the product of a unary theta function and the class number generating function. These identities involve Cohen's generalized class numbers and depend only on the genus of the underlying quadratic form. For this, we construct a genus-dependent level-lowering operator.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心二项式系数和推广的中心三项式系数在组合数学与数论中有重要应用，研究其在素数幂模下的同余性质（尤其是模p²的同余式）是经典而活跃的课题。通过引入p-adic对偶序列这一新工具，作者旨在统一并推广已有结果，同时验证孙智伟未解决的猜想。

Conclusion: p-adic对偶序列是研究中心二项式系数同余性的有效工具，通过它得到的模p²恒等式不仅统一了已有结果，还解决了孙智伟提出的相关猜想，为该方向提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: Let $(a_n)_{n\geqslant 0}$ be a sequence of integers. Its dual sequence $(a_n^*)_{n\geqslant 0}$ is defined by \begin{equation*}
  a_n^* := \sum_{k=0}^{n} \binom{n}{k}(-1)^k a_k. \end{equation*}
  Let $p>3$ be a prime. In this paper we mainly investigate congruences modulo $p^2$ involving central binomial coefficients and $p$-adic dual sequences. For example, we prove that for any sequence $(a_k)_{k\ge0}$ of $p$-adic integers, \begin{align*} \sum^{(p-1)/2}_{k=0}\binom{2k}{k}^2\frac{a_{2k}}{16^k}\equiv\left( \frac{-1}{p}\right) \sum_{k=0}^{p-1}\frac{\mathcal{P}_{k}}{16 ^{k}}a_{k}^*\pmod{p^2}, \end{align*} where $(\mathcal{P}_n)_{n\ge0}$ are the Catalan--Larcombe--French numbers given by \begin{equation*}
  \mathcal{P}_0=1,\quad \mathcal{P}_1=8, \quad n^2 \mathcal{P}_n = 8(3n^2-3n+1)\mathcal{P}_{n-1}-128(n-1)^2\mathcal{P}_{n-2}
  \quad (n\ge2). \end{equation*}
  We also establish a new formula for $\sum_{k=0}^{(p-1)/2}\binom{2k}{k}a_{2k}^*/4^k \pmod{p^2}$ and as a consequence we confirm some conjectures of Z.-W. Sun \cite{Sun2014CANT} on the generalized central trinomial coefficients $T_{2k}(b,c)$, i.e., the coefficient of $x^{2k}$ in $(x^2+bx+c)^{2k}$, where $b,c$ are integers.

</details>

Abstract: The Shimura curve of discriminant $D$ for $D=14, 15$ is uniformized by a subgroup of an arithmetic quadrilateral Fuchsian group $(2, 2, 2, q)$, where $q=4, 6$. We relate the generator of the ring of quaternionic modular forms on this Shimura curve to explicit Heun functions for the quadrilateral group. We also discuss how the Picard-Fuchs equation of the associated family of abelian surfaces has solutions that are modular forms on $X^{D}(1) / W_{D}$, where $W_D$ is the full group of Atkin-Lehner involutions. This leads us to completely describe the rational exceptional sets of the associated Heun functions, and the algebraic values attained by the Heun function on these points, for example

### [81] [The inverse Galois problem of iterated Galois groups and their fixed-point proportion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14524)
*Santiago Radi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Odoni于1985年开创算术表示和不动点比例的研究以来，有理函数的动力学与其迭代Galois群之间的联系一直是核心问题。本文旨在深入理解迭代Galois群的自复制能力，并解决不动点比例这一主要开放问题，该问题对素数密度、有限域上周期点比例等有直接应用。

Method: 引入虚拟混合群的概念，刻画迭代Galois群的自复制性质；通过群论、遍历理论、概率论、数论、复动力学、图上的能量输运、算术动力学和代数几何等领域的交叉技术进行证明。

Result: (1) 有理函数的迭代Galois群总是虚拟混合的，且失败部分（有界指数子群的商）与有理函数的几何性质相关；(2) 有理函数由代数曲线自同态诱导（动力拉回）当且仅当该失败部分非平凡；(3) 解决了不动点比例的主要开放问题：当映射不是动力拉回时，几何迭代Galois群的不动点比例为0。

Conclusion: 本文通过虚拟混合群这一新概念，建立了有理函数动力学性质与其迭代Galois群结构的深刻联系，完整解决了不动点比例问题，为素数密度问题和周期点比例研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: In 1985, Odoni initiated the study of arboreal representations and the fixed-point proportion, motivated by prime density problems in arithmetic dynamics. Since then, many questions regarding the connection between the dynamics of rational functions and the Galois groups associated to their dynamics (iterated Galois groups) have been posed.
  In this article, we give several contributions to this connection with the introduction of the concept of virtually mixing groups. This new concept is related to the capability of self-replication of the iterated Galois groups, and when it fails to be completely self-replicated, the failure is always by a finite index normal subgroup. It turns out that the quotient by this subgroup contains valuable information of the properties of the rational function.
  First, we investigate the inverse Galois problem, showing that iterated Galois groups of rational functions are always virtually mixing and the failure (the quotient by this finite-index subgroup) is related to geometric properties of the rational function. As a consequence, we prove that the rational function is induced by an endomorphism of an algebraic curve (dynamical pullback) if and only if this failure is non-trivial.
  Finally, we solve the main open problem of the fixed-point proportion of geometric iterated Galois groups of rational functions, by showing that the fixed-point proportion is zero when the map is not a dynamical pullback. This result has direct applications to prime density problems, proportion of periodic points in the reduction of maps and proportion of periodic points over finite fields.
  The proofs in this article rely on a combination of techniques and results from group theory, ergodic theory, probability, number theory, complex dynamics, energy transport in graphs, arithmetic dynamics and algebraic geometry.

</details>

### [82] [Small Area Estimation under Spatial Regimes: Spatially Clustered Fay-Herriot Models for Agricultural Indicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13638)
*Paolo Maranzano,Raffaele Mattera,Shonosuke Sugasawa*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出一种空间聚类Fay-Herriot（SC-FH）小域估计框架，通过Potts型空间凝聚项惩罚似然，同时估计聚类特定的回归系数和随机效应方差，并生成空间上连贯的地理域分区。模拟实验和意大利波河谷农场产量数据实证表明，该方法在空间异质性关系下优于标准FH模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Fay-Herriot模型假定协变量与结果变量之间的关系在整个空间域上均匀一致，但当这种关系具有空间异质性（即随空间位置变化）时，模型的借用强度机制会失效，导致估计精度下降。

Method: 提出SC-FH框架：(i) 在FH似然基础上引入Potts型空间凝聚项作为惩罚；(ii) 同时估计聚类特定的回归系数和随机效应方差，并生成空间上连贯的分区；(iii) 采用轮换策略，交替进行顺序标签更新和聚类内的闭式FH更新，实现高效估计。

Result: 在真实地理格局的模拟实验中，当潜在机制在协变量-响应空间中分离时，该方法几乎能精确恢复潜在聚类，且预测精度优于标准FH基准。空间惩罚起到稳定分类和估计的作用。意大利波河谷农场平均标准产出应用中，识别出两个空间紧凑且回归系数显著不同的生产机制，聚类级预测优于直接估计同时避免合并模型的过度收缩。

Conclusion: SC-FH框架有效解决了协变量与结果变量关系存在空间异质性时标准FH模型的缺陷，通过同时进行空间聚类和估计，能够在保持空间连贯性的同时提升预测精度，是对经典小域估计方法的一个重要扩展。

Abstract: Area-level small area estimation (SAE) models, such as the Fay--Herriot (FH) model, borrow strength across domains through covariates and random effects, but they can struggle when the relationship between the covariates and the outcome is spatially heterogeneous, that is, when it changes across the spatial domain of interest. We propose a spatially-clustered FH (SC-FH) framework that simultaneously (i) estimates cluster-specific regression coefficients and random effects variances and (ii) generates spatially coherent partitions of the geographical domain. Estimation maximizes a penalized likelihood that augments the FH likelihood with a Potts-type spatial cohesion term over the areal adjacency graph, through an efficient strategy that alternates between sequential label updates and closed-form FH updates within clusters. In simulation experiments run on the real geography of the application, the method recovers the latent regimes almost exactly whenever they are separated in the covariate--response space and improves prediction accuracy over the standard FH benchmark, with the spatial penalty acting as a stabilizer of both classification and estimation. An empirical application to the average standard output of farms in the Po Valley (Northern Italy) identifies two spatially compact production regimes with significantly different cluster-wise coefficients, and shows that the clusterwise predictor improves on the direct estimates while avoiding the over-shrinkage of the pooled model.

</details>


### [83] [Joint Temperature-Precipitation Patterns in the U.S. South Central Region: Multivariate Functional Inference and Gaussian Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13808)
*Gihani V. W. W. Disanayakage,Asim K. Dey,Kumer P. Das*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文使用功能多元方差分析（FMANOVA）和高斯混合模型两种互补的多元统计方法，分析美国南部各州季节温度-降水联合模式，发现冬季和夏季各州双变量平均函数存在显著差异，且冬季气候区划分更清晰、空间连贯性更强，而夏季气候区变异性更大且降水作用更突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度与降水的联合变异性是刻画季节性气候结构和相关环境过程的核心，但许多区域分析仅依赖边际或单变量汇总，忽略了二者的联合分布特征。为弥补这一不足，需要采用多元统计方法系统分析季节尺度上的温度-降水联合模式。

Method: 采用两种互补的多元统计方法：(1) 功能多元方差分析（FMANOVA），利用基于置换的Wilks' lambda和Pillai's trace统计量检验州际双变量均值函数的相等性；(2) 对站点级季节汇总数据应用高斯混合模型，基于温度与降水的联合分布识别潜在气候区。

Result: FMANOVA结果表明，冬夏两季各州的双变量均值轨迹均存在统计学显著的差异，且两季的对比反映了温度和降水贡献的不同。聚类分析显示冬季气候区划分比夏季更清晰、空间上更连贯，夏季气候区变异性更大，且降水在夏季气候区划分中扮演更重要的角色。

Conclusion: 联合建模温度与降水能够揭示单变量分析所遗漏的季节气候结构信息；冬季与夏季气候区在清晰度和空间连贯性上的不对称性表明，季节性气候分类应考虑温度与降水的联合依赖关系，而非独立处理各变量。

Abstract: Joint variability in temperature and precipitation is central in characterizing seasonal climate structure and associated environmental processes, yet many regional analyses rely on marginal or univariate summaries. We analyze seasonal temperature-precipitation patterns across the Southern United States using two complementary multivariate statistical approaches. First, functional multivariate analysis of variance (FMANOVA) is employed to test the equality of state-level bivariate mean functions, with the permutation-based Wilks' lambda and Pillai's trace statistics. Second, Gaussian mixture models are applied to station-level seasonal summaries to identify latent climate regimes based on the joint distribution of temperature and precipitation. The FMANOVA results indicate statistically significant differences in bivariate mean trajectories between states in both winter and summer, with seasonal contrasts reflecting differing contributions of temperature and precipitation. Clustering analysis indicates more clearly defined and spatially coherent winter regimes than summer regimes, with summer regimes exhibiting greater variability and a stronger role for precipitation.

</details>


### [84] [Spillover-Informed Network Architecture for Global Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14171)
*Neha Gupta,Nishit Soni,Aditya Maheshwari*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了在波动率预测中引入市场间连接信息是否以及如何提升预测精度。基于2015-2025年29个全球股票指数的日度数据，使用三种连接度量（地理位置、收益率相关性、DY溢出网络）构建溢出信息预测模型，相比标准HAR基准模型实现了约13%的样本外QLIKE损失降低，且在压力时期（COVID-19、俄乌冲突、2025年关税冲击）改进效果最显著（约21%），对波动率目标投资者具有显著的经济价值（每年322-373基点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动荡时期，波动率冲击会跨越国界溢出，使得股票市场波动率预测对风险管理、衍生品定价和监管资本至关重要。作者希望探究：当预测模型纳入市场间连接信息时，波动率预测能否得到改善，以及连接方式的选择是否会影响预测效果。

Method: 使用2015-2025年29个主要地区股票指数的日度数据，让每个市场的预测基于其关联市场的近期波动率，连接强度分别由三种方式设定：地理距离、收益率相关性、以及从数据中估计的Diebold-Yilmaz（DY）溢出网络。使用QLIKE损失函数和模型置信集（MCS）进行样本外评估，并通过波动率目标投资者框架进行经济显著性检验。

Result: 溢出信息预测模型相比标准HAR基准实现约13%的样本外QLIKE损失降低（p<0.001），并获得最高MCS p值。在压力时期（COVID-19崩盘、俄乌冲突、2025年美国关税冲击）改进约21%（p=0.018），平静期无显著损失。经济上，波动率目标投资者愿为溢出信息预测支付322-373基点/年（扣除交易成本），而替代方案仅85-102基点且不显著。模型自主推断连接时能仅从预测目标中恢复DY网络（置换检验p=0.0005）。

Conclusion: 跨市场溢出网络信息能显著提升波动率预测精度，尤其在市场压力期间价值最大，且DY网络作为连接度量优于地理或相关性的简单选择。连接结构的选择至关重要，而模型可以从预测目标中自行学习到真实的溢出网络结构。

Abstract: Spillover of volatility shocks across borders during turbulent periods makes accurate equity market volatility forecasts especially critical for risk management, derivatives pricing, and regulatory capital. In this paper, we examine whether volatility forecasts improve when models incorporate information on how markets are connected, and whether the choice of connection measure matters. Using daily data on 29 equity indices from every major region over 2015-2025, we let each market's forecast draw on the recent volatility of the markets linked to it, with the strength of each link set either by geography, return correlation, or the Diebold-Yilmaz (DY) spillover network estimated from the data. The spillover-informed forecasting model achieved an approximately 13\% reduction in out-of-sample QLIKE loss relative to the standard Heterogeneous AutoRegressive benchmark ($p<0.001$) and attained the highest model confidence set $p$-value among the models considered. Several benchmark models that do not explicitly incorporate cross-market structure were excluded from the 90\% model confidence set. The greatest improvements were observed during periods of elevated market stress: the COVID-19 crash, the Russian invasion of Ukraine, and the $2025$ US tariff shock, spillover-informed forecasts achieved approximately 21\% lower QLIKE loss than an otherwise identical network-blind model ($p = 0.018$), with no significant performance loss during calm periods. The advantage is economically material: a volatility-targeting investor would pay $322$-$373$ basis points per year, net of transaction costs, for spillover-informed forecasts, versus an insignificant $85$-$102$ basis point for the alternatives. Finally, when the model is left to infer connections on its own, it recovers the DY network from the forecast objective alone (permutation $p=0.0005$).

</details>


### [85] [Scale-dependent contraction of spatial wet-bulb temperature contrasts in eastern China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14294)
*Jian Hou,Tan Meng,Maozai Tian*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究中国东部地区湿热（湿球温度）的空间分布特征，发现高湿热的月份在空间上呈现更"平坦"的分布格局，区域均值会掩盖这一特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域平均湿球温度无法反映湿热在空间上的分布差异，而空间分布对于理解高温热浪的影响范围与严重程度至关重要。作者旨在揭示高热日是否伴随特定的空间分布模式，从而弥补区域均值的信息缺失。

Method: 采用预先指定的多尺度架构：结合五个带宽的高斯加权半方差，并按月等值、等尺度及等夏季聚合。利用2015和2022年夏季开发规格，应用于1991-2025年共33个夏季。比较121个站点上区域月均值的上四分位日与中半日。使用精确拉普拉斯分解定位空间对比来源，并与1950-1990年数据及外部稀疏站对比验证。

Result: 高均值日的加权均方成对湿球温度对比度低7.28%，平方根变换后减少3.71%，记录级均方根成对差异减少3.97%。收缩随高斯带宽增大而增强（h=126km时为2.86%至h=2,013km时为13.27%）。拉普拉斯分解将宽尺度对比定位于与月北-南气候型相反的每日异常，异常能量变化不大。该模式在1950-1990年仍存在，外部站点比较方向一致。

Conclusion: 高区域湿热与更平坦的宽尺度空间场相关，这是区域均值无法识别的特征，表明湿热的空间均匀性可能是高湿热事件的伴随特征。

Abstract: Regional wet-bulb temperature means omit the spatial distribution of humid heat. We compare upper-quartile and middle-half days of the monthly regional mean at 121 sites in a specified eastern-China domain. A prespecified multiscale architecture combines Gaussian-weighted semivariances at five bandwidths with equal-month, equal-scale and equal-summer aggregation. The specification was developed on the 2015 and 2022 summers and applied unchanged to 33 other summers in 1991-2025. On high-mean days, the weighted mean squared pairwise WBT contrast was 7.28% lower. This corresponds to a 3.71% reduction after square-root transformation and a 3.97% mean reduction in record-level root-mean-square pairwise differences. Contraction strengthened as the Gaussian graph bandwidth increased, from 2.86% at h = 126 km to 13.27% at h = 2,013 km. Exact Laplacian decompositions localised the broad-scale contrast to daily anomalies opposing the monthly north-south climatological pattern, while anomaly energy changed little. The pattern persisted in 1950-1990. A sparse external station comparison showed concordant broad-scale direction. High regional humid heat was associated with a flatter broad geographic field, a feature that a regional mean cannot identify.

</details>


### [86] [Filling survey gaps in food security monitoring with spatio-temporal additive Gaussian process models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14314)
*Emma Kopp,Sahoko Ishida,Rebecca Leygonie,Francesca Panero*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时空加性高斯过程模型，用于估计次国家层面的粮食安全时间序列。通过利用协方差矩阵的Kronecker结构实现可扩展推理，并在尼日利亚和乍得的调查数据上验证了其优于其他统计和机器学习模型，尤其在协变量具有信息性时精度更高且不确定性可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保国家各区域的粮食安全需要持续监测，但家庭调查常因资源限制和运营优先级问题在时空上留下显著覆盖空白。需要一个能够填补这些空白、同时保持计算可行性和可靠不确定性的模型。

Method: 提出时空加性高斯过程（spatio-temporal additive Gaussian process）模型，对区域级粮食安全时间序列进行估计。为应对高斯过程的计算成本，利用时空协方差矩阵的Kronecker结构实现可扩展的推理。

Result: 在尼日利亚和乍得的粮食安全调查数据上评估，与统计和机器学习基准模型相比，该模型实现了更高的精度并保持了可靠的不确定性，尤其在协变量信息充分的情况下。此外，模型成功生成了尼日利亚未覆盖州的估计值，展示了其在填补粮食安全监测地理空白方面的运营价值。

Conclusion: 时空加性高斯过程模型是填补粮食安全监测中地理和时间空白的有效工具，在精度、不确定性量化和计算可扩展性之间取得了良好平衡，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Ensuring food security across all regions of a country requires continuous monitoring, yet household surveys often leave significant spatio-temporal gaps due to resource constraints and operational priorities. In this paper, we propose a spatio-temporal additive Gaussian process model to estimate sub-national food security time series by regions. To address the computational cost of Gaussian process models, we exploit Kronecker structure of the spatio-temporal covariance matrix for scalable inference. We evaluate the proposed approach on food security survey data from Nigeria and Chad comparing it against other statistical and machine learning models and show how our proposal achieves better accuracy while retaining reliable uncertainty, especially when covariates are informative. We further apply the model to generate estimates for Nigerian states not covered by the survey, demonstrating its operational value for filling geographic gaps in food security monitoring.

</details>


### [87] [Flexible covariance structures on metric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14404)
*Karina Lilleborge,Sara Martino,Geir-Arne Fuglstad*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种灵活扩展的Whittle-Matérn高斯随机场模型，通过在度量图上引入潜在高斯随机场来描述SPDE中空间变化的系数，从而大幅提高模型灵活性。模拟研究表明在数据充足时该灵活模型优于较不灵活的模型，并关注了恢复协方差结构所需的观测和重复数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WM高斯随机场在度量图（如道路和河流网络）上的研究日益活跃，现有工作已考虑了空间变化协变量的使用，但未充分利用SPDE系数可灵活变化的全部可能性。作者旨在通过引入潜在GRF来描述这些空间变化的系数，进一步扩展模型的表达能力。

Method: 在度量图上定义Whittle-Matérn随机偏微分方程（SPDE），将其系数视为由潜在高斯随机场描述的随机场，从而构造广义WM GRF的灵活扩展模型。通过模拟研究比较灵活模型与较不灵活模型在协方差结构估计和预测能力上的表现，重点关注观测数量和重复次数对恢复协方差结构的可靠性影响，并在马德里交通流量数据上进行实际应用验证。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在数据充足的情况下，灵活模型在协方差结构估计和预测方面均优于较不灵活模型。同时揭示了恢复协方差结构所需的观测数量和重复次数的重要性。在马德里交通流量数据的实际应用中，模型之间在样本内与样本外预测能力上表现出显著差异。

Conclusion: 灵活模型在数据充分时能有效提升模型表现，但样本内与样本外预测的巨大差异提示需要谨慎评估模型泛化能力。该工作拓展了WM GRF在度量图上的应用灵活性和实用性。

Abstract: Whittle-Matérn (WM) Gaussian random fields (GRFs) are defined as solutions of stochastic partial differential equations (SPDEs) and provide a natural analog of Matérn GRFs on non-Euclidean geometry where the Matérn covariance function is not valid. In particular, WM GRFs on metric graphs have been an active area of research motivated by road and river networks where spatial dependence is more naturally described by intrinsic distances in the network than by Euclidean distances. This family of GRFs is controlled by three parameters relating to marginal variance, spatial range, and smoothness, but can be extended to so-called generalized WM GRFs through spatially varying coefficients in the SPDE. Recent work has considered the use of spatially varying covariates, but the full possibilities of flexibility have not been considered. In this work, we introduce latent GRFs that describe the spatially varying coefficients of the SPDE. This flexible model is compared to less flexible models in a simulation study evaluating both the ability to estimate the covariance structure and predictive ability. An important focus is the number of observations and replications necessary to reliably recover the covariance structure. We find that the flexible model improves over less flexible models in the presence of sufficient data. We also demonstrate practical applicability on traffic counts in a part of Madrid, and observe major differences between in-sample and out-of-sample predictive abilities of the models compared.

</details>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [Refined outer-approximation algorithms for monotonic optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13694)
*Ahmed Rashwan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对单调优化中的polyblock外近似算法（POA）扩展性差的问题，提出了三项算法改进：广义锚点选择实现最优平衡单调性割、无需连续性假设的松弛最优性条件保证有限终止、以及向量化变体支持多节点并发处理。此外开发了高效的树形实现和开源Python包polyblocks，实验表明可实现数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POA算法在描述可行集外近似时顶点数量可能指数级增长，导致内存需求大且子程序计算成本高昂，严重限制了其在大规模问题上的适用性。

Method: 提出三个算法改进：1）广义锚点选择以获得最优平衡单调性割；2）松弛最优性条件，在无连续性假设下保证有限终止；3）向量化变体并发处理多个节点。同时设计基于树的实现以加速核心子程序并压缩存储外近似。

Result: 数值实验表明所提出的改进相比标准POA实现了数量级的速度提升，并能够解决现有变体无法处理的问题。

Conclusion: 通过算法改进和高效实现，显著提升了POA的扩展性和实用性，并发布开源Python包polyblocks以支持社区开发新的POA变体。

Abstract: Monotonic optimisation is a broad class of non-convex problems formulated in terms of monotone functions. Such problems are commonly solved via the polyblock outer-approximation algorithm (POA), a branch-and-bound method that iteratively refines a rectangular outer-approximation of the feasible set. POA scales poorly, however: the number of vertices needed to describe the approximation can grow exponentially, leading to large memory requirements and increasingly expensive subroutines. To address these limitations, we propose three algorithmic improvements: a generalised anchor selection that yields an optimal balanced monotonicity cut, a relaxed optimality condition that guarantees finite termination without continuity assumptions, and a vectorised variant that processes multiple nodes concurrently. We further develop an efficient tree-based implementation, which accelerates POA's core subroutines while storing the outer-approximation compactly. Numerical experiments show that these improvements yield order-of-magnitude speed-ups over standard POA and solve problems on which existing variants fail. Finally, we introduce polyblocks, an open-source Python package implementing the proposed algorithms alongside a framework for developing new POA variants, available at https://github.com/RashwanA/polyblocks.

</details>


### [89] [Multi-period Value-at-Risk Constrained Portfolio Optimization via DC Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13748)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多期投资组合优化中同时考虑VaR约束、交易成本和多样化正则化，使得问题呈现非光滑且非凸的特性，现有的DCA和BDCA算法收敛速度较慢或收敛性保障不足，需要设计更高效的求解算法。

Method: 利用有限场景VaR-CVaR恒等式，将带VaR约束的问题转化为凸可行集上的惩罚DC（Difference-of-Convex）规划问题；随后提出投影惯性Boosted DC函数算法（iBDCA），融合了惯性外推、目标函数保护和增强线搜索三种技术以加速收敛。

Result: 理论证明算法良定义、目标序列单调递减、聚点均为惩罚DC问题的临界点；在局部无平局条件下进一步证明了全序列收敛并具有局部R-线性收敛速率。数值实验表明iBDCA优于DCA和标准BDCA；样本外回测与等权及买入持有基准对比，展示了对收益、风险、交易成本和VaR控制的权衡。

Conclusion: iBDCA是一种兼具理论保障和实际高效性的求解带VaR约束多期组合优化问题的算法，并能通过回测揭示实际投资中多目标权衡。

Abstract: We study a multi-period portfolio optimization problem with finite-scenario Value-at-Risk (VaR) constraints, transaction costs, and diversification regularization. Using a finite-scenario VaR--CVaR identity, we derive a penalized difference-of-convex (DC) formulation over the underlying convex portfolio set. To solve the resulting nonsmooth and nonconvex problem, we propose a projected inertial Boosted Difference-of-Convex Functions Algorithm (iBDCA) that combines inertial extrapolation, an objective safeguard, and a boosted line search. We prove that the method is well defined, generates a monotonically decreasing objective sequence, and has only critical accumulation points for the penalized DC problem. Under a local no-ties condition at an accumulation point, we further establish whole sequence convergence with a local \(R\)-linear rate. Numerical experiments compare iBDCA with DCA and standard BDCA on matched problem instances. Out-of-sample backtests additionally include equal-weight and buy-and-hold benchmarks to illustrate the trade-offs among realized return, risk, transaction costs, and empirical VaR control.

</details>


### [90] [Best Response Dynamics for Zero-Sum Dynamic Games with Partial-Asymmetric Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13755)

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究部分非对称信息下的零和随机线性二次动态博弈（LQDG），提出基于最优响应动力学的求解方法，揭示了信念表示与心智理论挑战，并通过数值实验证明高阶信念状态对博弈价值的贡献迅速衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息不对称使LQDG的信念表示和心智理论（theory of mind）成为根本性挑战，玩家必须推断他人的信念状态和估计来制定策略。现有工作表明动态规划类分解方法难以直接应用于此类问题，亟需替代性求解框架。

Method: 提出基于最优响应动力学（best response dynamics）的替代方法。在每个玩家的纯线性动态输出反馈控制策略类（内部控制状态维度为系统状态维度的整数倍）下推导出显式的最优响应表达式。玩家迭代更新最优响应的过程会形成越来越高阶的信念状态，导致内部控制状态维数趋向无限。

Result: 数值实验表明：尽管理论上信念状态是无限维的，博弈价值仅需少数几次迭代即可收敛，说明高阶信念状态的边际收益趋近于零。此外，实验系统分析了非对称信念、信念阶数、相对可控性与可观测性以及直接馈通（direct feed-through）对线性二次追逃博弈价值的影响。

Conclusion: 最优响应动力学为部分非对称信息下的LQDG提供了一种可行的求解途径；有限阶信念状态足以近似博弈价值，有效缓解了理论上的无限维内状态问题。

Abstract: This work studies a class of zero-sum stochastic linear quadratic dynamic games (LQDGs) under partial and asymmetric information. Information asymmetry introduces fundamental challenges related to \textit{belief representation} and \textit{theory of mind}, where players must impute belief states and estimates of other players to inform their strategies. Existing work highlights the difficulty of applying dynamic programming-like decomposition approach to these problems. An alternative approach based on \textit{best response dynamics} is proposed, which provides insights into belief representation and theory of mind challenges. Explicit expressions for each player's best response within the class of pure linear dynamic output feedback control strategies are derived, where the internal state dimension of each control is an integer multiple of the system state dimension. As players iteratively update their best responses, they form increasingly higher-order belief states, leading to infinite-dimensional internal states. However, numerical results reveal that the game's value converges after only a few iterations, suggesting that higher-order belief states provide vanishing benefit. This work further conducts numerical experiments to analyze the impact of asymmetric beliefs, belief orders, relative controllability and observability, and direct feed-through on a linear quadratic pursuit-evasion game's value.

</details>


### [91] [Recursive Filtering and Stochastic Control under Finite Partition-Based Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13825)
*Saul Díaz-Infante Velasco,Yofre H. Garcia,Jesús Adolfo Minjárez-Sosa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对部分可观测随机系统的滤波与最优控制框架，其中每个观测对应隐藏状态空间有限可测划分中的一个类别，涵盖阈值、量化、删失、事件触发和间歇观测等机制。通过构造类别受限的未归一化条件测度，将原问题转化为完全可观测的马尔可夫决策过程，并给出了离散代价准则下的贝尔曼方程、平稳最优策略存在条件、有限维近似方案及滤波误差与值函数误差之间的抽象界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测随机系统的最优控制是一个经典难题，特别是当观测具有区域性质（如阈值、量化、删失、事件触发和间歇观测）时，传统滤波与控制框架难以统一处理。现有方法往往针对特定观测机制单独设计，缺乏一个能够统一涵盖多种区域信息结构的通用框架，也缺乏相应的有限维近似和误差分析。

Method: 1. 在每个可观测类别上定义类别受限的未归一化条件测度，通过其预测概率归一化得到后验分布；2. 构造由观测类别及其支持的条件测度组成的信息状态，将部分可观测问题转化为完全可观测的MDP；3. 推导离散代价准则下的贝尔曼方程并建立平稳最优策略存在的条件；4. 提出类别依赖的有限维近似，同时保留连续分量和原子质量；5. 推导近似滤波器误差与值函数误差之间的抽象界；6. 通过直方图近似参考模型说明构造。

Result: 成功建立了统一的滤波与控制框架，该框架覆盖阈值、量化、删失、事件触发和间歇观测等多种区域信息机制，可处理原子、连续或混合分量的可观测类别；导出了贝尔曼方程和平稳最优策略存在性条件；提出了保留连续分量和原子质量的类别依赖有限维近似方案，并建立了滤波误差到值函数误差的误差传播界。

Conclusion: 该工作为区域信息机制下的部分可观测随机系统提供了一个统一的测度级滤波-控制框架，兼具理论完备性与可计算性。从滤波误差到值函数误差的抽象界保证了近似方案的理论可靠性，直方图近似示例验证了框架在实践中的可行性。

Abstract: We develop a filtering and optimal-control framework for partially observable stochastic systems in which each observation identifies a class of a finite measurable partition of the hidden state space. This structure covers regional-information mechanisms associated with threshold, quantized, censored, event-triggered, and intermittent observations, and allows observable classes with atomic, continuous, or mixed components. The formulation is constructed first at the level of measures: for each observable class, we define a class-restricted unnormalized conditional measure, and the posterior distribution is obtained by normalizing with its predictive probability. Based on this recursion, we introduce an information state consisting of the observed class and the conditional measure supported on it, thereby transforming the original problem into a fully observable Markov decision process. We formulate the discounted-cost criterion, derive the Bellman equation, and establish conditions for the existence of stationary optimal policies. To address the infinite-dimensional nature of the information space, we propose class-dependent finite-dimensional approximations capable of preserving both continuous components and atomic masses. We also derive an abstract bound linking the error of the approximate filter to the error of the value function. A reference model illustrates the construction through histogram-based approximations

</details>


### [92] [Nondegeneracy Conditions for Control Problems with Nonregular Mixed Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13834)
*Jorge Becerril,Karla Cortez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有单个非正则混合不等式约束的最优控制问题，证明在模型问题中退化是内禀且不可避免的——每个可行过程都通过纯电荷（purely finitely additive set functions）以退化方式满足必要条件。为此提出了四个可验证的条件，逐步保证非退化乘子、纯电荷消失、正规性及电荷质量的显式界。在非正则终端时刻，平衡恒等式将电荷质量与成本乘子联系起来，决定完全异常性与正规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非正则混合约束的最优控制问题中，标准必要条件的乘子可能退化（即成本乘子为零），导致必要条件失去意义。已有研究指出纯电荷在这类问题中的出现，但对其是否内禀、是否可通过正则近似消除，以及如何获得非退化乘子的条件，缺乏系统分析。

Method: 首先针对单个时刻非正则的模型问题，证明退化是内禀且不可避免的；接着分析自然正则近似下归一化乘子的弱*极限行为，证明在Banach极限意义下仅产生纯电荷。基于该分析，提出四个渐进增强的可验证条件（分别保证非退化乘子、纯电荷消失、正规性和电荷质量上界）。最后在非正则终端时刻建立成本乘子与电荷质量之间的平衡恒等式。

Result: 证明了模型问题中退化不可避免，正则近似的归一化乘子在L^∞弱*拓扑下无极限，仅产生纯电荷。提出的四个条件分别从不同层次保证了乘子的非退化性、无纯电荷、正规性和电荷质量有界性。终端时刻的平衡恒等式给出了判断完全异常性与正规性的准则。提供的实例验证了各条件的可实现性并讨论了其局限性。

Conclusion: 退化在非正则最优控制问题中是一种本质现象而非正则化缺陷，纯电荷乘子在此类问题中不可避免。但通过四个可验证的条件，可以在实际应用中逐步消除退化并获得有意义的正规性结果。平衡恒等式为终端时刻的异常性判定提供了精确工具。

Abstract: We establish nondegeneracy and normality conditions for optimal control problems with a single nonregular mixed inequality constraint, where purely finitely additive set functions (charges) appear as multipliers. We first prove for a model problem nonregular at a single instant that degeneration is intrinsic and unavoidable: every admissible process satisfies the necessary conditions degenerately via a pure charge, and normalized multipliers of natural regular approximations admit no weak$^*$ limit in $L^\infty([0,1];\mathbb{R})^*$, yielding only pure charges under every generalized limit, in the sense of Banach limits. Motivated by this analysis, we propose four verifiable conditions that progressively guarantee nondegenerate multipliers, the vanishing of the pure charge, normality, and explicit bounds on charge mass. Furthermore, we show that at a nonregular terminal instant, a balance identity ties the charge mass to the cost multiplier, deciding between complete abnormality and normality. We provide examples confirming the realizability and discussing the limits of each condition.

</details>


### [93] [First-Order Optimization as Minimum-Time Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13915)
*Liraz Mudrik,Isaac Kaminer,Pramod P. Khargonekar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将一阶优化形式化为最小时间控制问题：迭代点作为状态、更新量作为控制、梯度范数有界点集为目标集，以值函数衡量每个具体实例（而非类别最坏情况）的复杂度。在强凸二次函数上，共轭梯度迭代自然涌现于离散庞特里亚金条件，值函数对应可控性指标；超越二次情形时，由Hessian生成的张成空间替代可控性矩阵，可行性成为可达性问题。曲率被视为资源，可验证条件可证明实例所需步数少于其极小点处二次模型的可控性指标，且差距可随维度无界增长。该值函数成为度量每个实例内在难度的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优化复杂度理论主要关注最坏情形下的复杂度上界，难以精细化刻画单个具体实例的内在难度。作者希望建立一种能衡量每个实例固有计算复杂度的框架，从而为任意一阶方法提供逐实例的评估基准。

Method: 将一阶优化重新建模为最小时间控制问题：以迭代点作为状态变量，以观测梯度组合而成的更新作为控制输入，以梯度范数不超过预设容差的所有点构成目标集。最小化达到目标所需的oracle查询次数即为值函数。在强凸二次问题上，通过离散庞特里亚金最优性条件推导出共轭梯度迭代；在一般非线性情形下，利用Hessian张成的可达空间推广可控性矩阵的概念，将临界点是否可达以及需要多少步归约为可控性/可达性问题。

Result: 在强凸二次情形中，共轭梯度法从离散庞特里亚金条件中自然涌现，值函数等于可控性指标。超越二次情形时，曲率成为可利用的资源：存在可验证的充分条件，证明某些实例所需步数严格少于其在极小点处二次模型预测的可控性指标，且该差距可随维度增加而无界增大。该框架为每个实例提供了一种新的复杂度度量标准。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个以控制论视角统一定义逐实例优化复杂度的理论框架，将复杂度从类别最坏情形推广到每个具体实例的值函数。值函数可作为任何一阶方法在该实例上表现的基准，同时揭示了曲率作为计算资源在加速优化方面的潜力，为设计实例自适应的一阶优化算法提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We formulate first-order optimization as a minimum-time control problem. The iterate is the state, the update, a combination of the gradients observed so far, is the control, and the points with gradient norm at most a prescribed tolerance form the target set. For a fixed objective and start, the minimum number of oracle queries needed to reach the target is a value function: it measures the complexity of the instance, not the worst case over a class. On a strongly convex quadratic, the conjugate gradient iterates emerge from the discrete Pontryagin conditions, and the value is a controllability index. Beyond the quadratic, a Hessian-generated reachable span replaces the controllability matrix, and feasibility becomes a reachability question: whether a critical point can be reached at all, and in how many steps, is read from the span. Curvature is a resource: a checkable condition certifies that an instance needs fewer steps than the controllability index of its quadratic model at the minimizer, and the gap can grow without bound with the dimension. The value is thus a benchmark for the intrinsic difficulty of each instance, against which any first-order method can be measured.

</details>


### [94] [Benders Decomposition with Partial Non-Anticipativity Relaxation for Multi-Stage Stochastic Clean Energy Transition Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14105)
*Ahmet Emir Şener,Burak Kocuk,Tuğçe Yüksel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了校园级电-热综合能源系统在战略层和运营层双重不确定性下的清洁能源转型规划问题，提出了一个多阶段随机混合整数规划模型，并开发了带有部分非预期性松弛的Benders分解算法来求解。通过中东技术大学校园案例验证，该方法能在合理计算预算内高效求解高时间分辨率的大规模模型，并通过滚动时域蒙特卡洛模拟证明了增加鲁棒性可以显著提高转型计划的运营可靠性，仅需适度增加总成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校园级综合电-热能源系统的清洁能源转型规划面临多重挑战：投资决策（战略层）和运营决策（操作层）均受不确定性影响，包括可再生能源出力、负荷需求、技术进步（成本与效率演变）等。现有方法难以在合理计算成本内求解此类大规模随机规划问题，需要开发高效的求解算法。

Method: 1) 构建多阶段随机混合整数规划模型，联合优化可再生能源发电、储能和热传输技术的投资与运营决策；2) 基于箱式不确定集推导鲁棒重构以处理短期运营不确定性；3) 开发Benders分解算法，将投资变量及其非预期性约束保留在主问题中，运营变量分配到各情景子问题中并松弛其非预期性约束；4) 通过一个较小的线性规划在终止时恢复运营变量的非预期性，并证明该校正步骤的目标函数值增量有有限上界；5) 引入有效不等式和两阶段割添加策略提升计算性能。

Result: 在中东技术大学校园案例中，所提方法能在合理的计算预算内以高时间分辨率求解大规模模型，而扩展形式和经典Benders分解均无法实现。滚动时域蒙特卡洛模拟的样本外分析表明，加入鲁棒性后转型计划的运营可靠性显著提升，总成本仅适度增加。

Conclusion: 所提出的部分非预期性松弛Benders分解算法有效解决了校园级综合能源系统转型规划的计算瓶颈；鲁棒建模能够在不显著增加成本的前提下大幅提升运营可靠性，为实际校园能源转型规划提供了可行的决策支持工具。

Abstract: We study clean energy transition planning for campus-scale integrated electricity-heat systems under both strategic level and operational level uncertainties. We formulate a multi-stage stochastic mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes investment and operational decisions for renewable generation, storage, and heat-transfer technologies whose costs and efficiencies evolve stochastically across stages. To account for short-term operational uncertainty, we further derive a robust reformulation based on box uncertainty sets for demand, renewable generation, and heat-transfer performance. To solve the resulting large-scale model, we develop a Benders decomposition algorithm with partial non-anticipativity relaxation. Investment variables and their non-anticipativity constraints are retained in the master problem while operational variables are assigned to scenario-wise subproblems where their non-anticipativity constraints are relaxed. Non-anticipativity of operational variables is restored only at termination through a smaller linear program. We prove that this correction step can only increase the objective function value by a finite bound. We improve the computational performance of the algorithm developed with valid inequalities and a two-phase cut-addition strategy. Using the proposed approach, we solve the Middle East Technical University campus case study at high temporal resolution within a reasonable computational budget, which is not otherwise possible with the extensive form or the classical Benders decomposition. We evaluate the resulting investment plans through rolling-horizon Monte Carlo simulations with an out-of-sample analysis and demonstrate that the added robustness can significantly improve the operational reliability of the transition plans with a moderate increase in total cost.

</details>


### [95] [Joint Inventory Placement, Assortment Personalization, and Order Fulfillment for Substitutable Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14275)
*Mikhail Fadin,Omar El Housni,Huseyin Topaloglu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在线零售商在分布式仓库网络中联合优化库存分配、个性化选品和订单履约的问题，提出具有理论保障的近似算法和策略，在一般概率选择模型下实现渐近最优，在MNL选择模型下获得常数因子近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代在线零售商依赖分布式仓库网络来快速履行客户订单，但现有的研究通常将库存分配、选品推荐和订单履约这三个关键决策割裂处理，缺乏一个统一的联合优化框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，为三个相互关联的决策提供可证明高效的算法，同时在模型复杂度和算法可实施性之间取得平衡。

Method: 建立了一个多周期随机规划模型：销售期开始前确定各仓库的库存分配（受容量和供应约束），随后在有限时间水平内按已知分布到达的不同客户类型被提供个性化选品，客户依据离散选择模型最多选择一个产品，企业则在可行范围内选择最优仓库履约以最大化期望利润。针对一般概率选择模型，提出随仓库容量和产品供应规模扩展而渐近最优的近似算法；针对MNL模型，推导出从0.080（最一般情形）到0.199（平稳且无限供应）的常数因子近似算法。

Result: 在一般概率选择模型下，算法在仓库容量和产品供应规模扩大时达到渐近最优性；在MNL选择模型下，获得常数因子近似保证，因子范围从最一般设置下的0.080到平稳且仅受仓库容量约束情形下的0.199。这些结果为该联合问题提供了首批可证明高效的算法。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统地解决了库存分配、个性化选品和订单履约的联合优化问题，为在线零售运营管理提供了坚实的理论支撑，同时也为未来在更复杂选择模型或动态库存补货情境下的拓展研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern online retailers leverage networks of distributed warehouses to rapidly fulfill customer orders. We study a problem of jointly deciding (i) how to allocate inventories across warehouses in the network subject to warehouse capacity and product supply constraints, (ii) how to dynamically select personalized product assortments based on customer preferences and location, as well as real-time stock levels, and (iii) which warehouse to use to fulfill the product chosen by the customer to maximize expected profit. In our model, the firm chooses an inventory placement across warehouses at the start of the selling horizon. Customers of different types then arrive at discrete time periods over a finite time horizon according to a known distribution. When offered an assortment, each customer type chooses at most one product according to a discrete choice model. The firm then chooses a feasible warehouse from which to fulfill the chosen product, depleting its stock and earning a profit that depends on the product, customer type, and warehouse choice. We develop approximation algorithms and policies with provable theoretical guarantees and strong empirical performance. Under general probabilistic choice models, we establish asymptotically optimal approximation algorithms as warehouse capacities and product supplies scale. Under the multinomial logit (MNL) choice model, we obtain constant-factor approximation algorithms, with approximation factors ranging from 0.080 in the most general setting to 0.199 under stationarity and unlimited product supplies, which means that only warehouse capacity constraints are present. To the best of our knowledge, these results provide the first provably efficient algorithms for the joint inventory placement, assortment personalization, and order fulfillment problem.

</details>


### [96] [Diffusion-Based Policies for Dynamic Control of Stochastic Processing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14289)
*Baris Ata,Jin Guang,J. Michael Harrison,Nian Si*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多类排队网络的近似布朗控制问题（BCP）方法，允许作业路由、输入控制和排程决策，通过矩阵布朗控制问题的数值求解转化为可实现的控制策略，在重流量参数域内近似最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类排队网络中的最优控制问题（包括输入控制、作业路由和排程决策）在一般模型下难以求解。需要一种既能处理高维状态空间又能在原网络中具有可解释性的近似最优策略。

Method: 将原始排队网络建模为m维非负象限上的布朗控制问题（BCP），控制为从有界多面体集合中选择的漂移向量。利用最近发展的计算方法对BCP进行数值求解（维度可达m=50），并将数值解转化为原始排队系统可实现的控制策略。

Result: 数值结果表明该策略在重流量参数域内近似最优，支撑了先前工作对重流量扩散逼近的猜想。相比之前提出的低维"等效工作量构形"方法，该方法在原网络中更易于解释。

Conclusion: BCP方法为多类排队网络提供了一种在高维空间中可计算且具有良好可解释性的近最优控制策略框架，是对此前低维等效工作量方法的改进。

Abstract: We consider a processing network model with $m$ job classes or buffers, exogenous input flows into some classes, $n$ processing activities, and $p$ servers. Each activity is either a specified server processing jobs of a specified class, or a fictional input server delivering jobs of a specified class; jobs change class in Markovian fashion after completing service. A standard multiclass queueing network, with its one-to-one correspondence between job classes and activities, is a special case, but our general model allows two or more ways to process a given class, and some or all input flows may be turned away at the system manager's discretion.
  Costs are linear: a holding cost per time unit for each class $i$ job in the system, and a rejection penalty for each class $i$ arrival denied access $(i=1,\ldots,m)$. The system manager makes input control, job routing, and order-of-service decisions to minimize expected discounted costs over an infinite horizon.
  We formulate an approximating Brownian control problem (BCP) whose state space is the $m$-dimensional nonnegative orthant; control is a drift vector chosen from a bounded polyhedral set, based on dynamic state observations. Using recently developed computational methods, the BCP can be solved numerically in dimensions up to at least $m=50$, and we explain how the numerical solution is translated into an implementable control policy for the queueing system of original interest.
  Previous work on heavy traffic diffusion approximations suggests that this policy is nearly optimal in the heavy traffic parameter regime, and numerical examples support that conjecture. We also discuss its advantage over an alternative approach, featured in our previous work, where the BCP is replaced by a lower-dimensional "equivalent workload formulation" that is computationally efficient but difficult to interpret in the network of original interest.

</details>


### [97] [Optimal Control with $L^\infty$ and Integral Cost Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14316)
*Madhu Dhiman,Veeraruna Kavitha,Nandyala Hemachandra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究同时包含积分代价（累计性能）与上确界代价（峰值性能）的组合控制问题，建立了松弛控制与纯控制的ε-最优存在性，并通过光滑逼近方法解决动态规划原则与非光滑计算挑战，最后用排队问题验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典控制问题分别研究积分代价（累计性能）和上确界/L∞代价（峰值或最坏情况行为），但两者的组合尚未被充分探索。实际系统中常需同时考虑累计性能和峰值性能，因此需要研究这两种代价组合下的控制问题。

Method: 首先在松弛控制框架下利用控制空间的紧致性和凸性建立最优松弛控制的存在性，进而证明对任意ε>0存在ε-最优的纯（经典）控制。针对计算困难，引入一族光滑逼近将原问题转化为标准控制问题，利用最大值定理（Maximum Theorem）证明光滑问题解在纯控制类中构成原问题的ε-最优，且ε随光滑参数收敛而趋于零。

Result: 建立了组合代价下最优松弛控制的存在性，以及任意ε>0下ε-最优纯控制的存在性。通过光滑逼近方法，证明了光滑问题的解是原问题的ε-最优，且误差随光滑参数收敛而趋于零。在排队问题中的应用表明该方法能够在峰值拥塞水平和累计性能之间实现所需的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地探索了积分代价与上确界代价组合的控制问题，提供了存在性理论框架与可计算的逼近方法。尽管动态规划原则对原始问题是否成立尚不明确，光滑逼近方法为实际求解提供了可行途径，排队问题的例子验证了其实际应用价值。

Abstract: Many control problems are classically formulated using integral costs that capture the cumulative performance of a system. Peak or worst-case behavior is captured via supremum or $L^\infty$-costs in another variety of control problems. When both cumulative and peak performance are important, it is natural to consider objective functions that combine the two costs. Although each criterion is well studied, their combination has not been explored extensively and we precisely work on such control problems.
  Towards establishing the existence, we first consider the relaxed framework, where control is considered using probability distributions. Using the well-known compactness and convexity properties of such control spaces, we establish the existence of an optimal relaxed control---we eventually establish the existence of an $ε$-optimal pure (or classical) control, for every $ε> 0.$
  Despite these existence results, computing optimal policies remains challenging due to the non-smoothness introduced by the supremum term, and it is not clear whether the dynamic programming principle holds for our combined problem. To address this, we introduce a family of smooth approximations that yield standard control problems with well-defined optimal (pure) solutions. Using Maximum Theorem, we establish that the solutions of the smooth problems among pure controls form $ε$-optimal for the original problem, with $ε$ tending to zero as the smoothing parameter converges to zero.
  Finally using the methods proposed in this paper, we study a queueing problem to illustrate (among others) that the required trade-off between peak congestion levels and cumulative performance can be achieved.

</details>


### [98] [Optimizing bounds for energy-constrained optimal cooling problems in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14334)
*Pedro Blöss Braga,Giovanni Fantuzzi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究二维有界区域中不可压缩流体的最优冷却问题，通过拉格朗日对偶构造适定的对偶问题，用半定规划层次法数值求解上限，并给出新的解析上界：一般情形下E(Pe)≲Pe²，盘/环域径向正热分布下E(Pe)≲Pe²/ln²Pe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在给定动能预算（由无量纲Péclet数Pe的平方度量）的条件下，如何配置二维不可压缩流体流动以最大化冷边界条件下的冷却效率，是传热优化中的核心问题。现有结果主要限于圆盘等特殊构型，缺乏对一般二维域和更广泛热源/汇分布的统一分析与上界估计。

Method: 采用拉格朗日对偶方法构建原最优控制问题的对偶形式，证明其对偶问题是适定的，从而得到冷却效率上限。通过对偶问题的离散化构造收敛的半定规划（SDP）层次，数值求解最优冷却问题（正方形和环形域），并利用问题对称性降低计算复杂度。此外，构造对偶问题的可行点以获得解析上界。

Result: 证明了对偶问题适定且给出最优冷却效率的上界；数值方法在正方形和环形域上成功逼近最优解；推导出两个新的解析上界：对于任意二维域和热分布E(Pe)≲Pe²，对于圆盘和环域中方位角平均为正的热源/汇分布E(Pe)≲Pe²/ln²Pe，推广并改进了已有圆盘中的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了能量约束冷却流体优化问题的一般对偶框架，提供了可计算的数值方法与新的解析上界，表明在对称（圆盘/环）几何下对数因子改善的冷却效率上界是可达的，并给出了进一步优化和分析的依据。

Abstract: We study optimal control problems for incompressible fluids in two-dimensional domains with a cold boundary and internal heat sources and sinks. Given a kinetic energy budget, measured by the square of a nondimensional Péclet number $\mathrm{Pe}$, the goal is to maximize a cooling efficiency inversely proportional to the mean square gradient of the fluid's temperature. Using Lagrange duality, we formulate a well-posed dual problem whose solution yields an upper bound on the maximum cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. We then numerically approximate the dual problem using a convergent hierarchy of semidefinite programs obtained via discretization. We illustrate this computational approach on optimal cooling problems in a square and in an annulus, explaining also how problem symmetries can be exploited to reduce computational complexity. Finally, we construct admissible points for the dual problem to obtain new analytical upper bounds on the optimal cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. Specifically, we prove that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe}) \lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}$ for arbitrary domains and heat distributions, and that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})\lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}/ \ln^2\mathrm{Pe}$ for cooling flows in disks and annuli with heat source/sink distributions with a positive azimuthal average. These results generalize and improve known efficiency bounds for energy-constrained cooling flows in a disk.

</details>


### [99] [A Shrinkage Path Heuristic for Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14336)
*Lingjun Meng,Ryan Cory-Wright,Wolfram Wiesemann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种收缩路径（shrinkage path）启发式方法，用于高效求解Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化（DRO）问题，将其转化为一维搜索，显著降低计算成本的同时保留了大部分DRO的样本外性能改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein DRO是一个广泛使用的决策框架，但其标准确定性重构通常包含非凸的内层子问题，求解困难。这限制了DRO在实际应用中的可扩展性和实用性。

Method: 提出收缩路径启发式方法，将DRO问题的求解简化为一维搜索，在样本平均近似（SAA）解与经典鲁棒优化解之间的线段上进行搜索。在设定场景下推导先验次优性界；对于一般情况，通过对偶形式应用类似启发式推导后验界。

Result: 在多物品报童问题和预约调度问题的数值实验中，该方法在计算成本仅为原来一小部分的情况下，分别获得了Wasserstein DRO相对SAA的样本外性能改善的85-110%和45-70%。

Conclusion: 收缩路径启发式方法能够以极低的计算代价逼近Wasserstein DRO的完整求解效果，为实际中大规模DRO问题的求解提供了实用且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization (DRO) is a versatile and widely adopted framework for decision-making under uncertainty, yet its standard deterministic reformulations generally contain non-convex inner subproblems that are challenging to solve. To address this issue, we propose a shrinkage path heuristic that reduces the solution of a DRO problem to a one-dimensional search over the line segment connecting the (typically benign) sample average approximation (SAA) and the (more demanding but practically solvable) classical robust optimization solution. We derive a priori suboptimality bounds in stylized settings and, for the general case, a posteriori bounds obtained by applying a similar heuristic to a dual formulation. Numerical experiments on a multi-item newsvendor and an appointment scheduling problem show that the shrinkage path heuristic attains 85-110% (resp. 45-70%) of the out-of-sample performance improvements of Wasserstein DRO over SAA, at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


### [100] [AI-Assisted Discovery and Construction of a Counterexample to the Convergence of Three-Block ADMM with the Identity Matrix as its Third Constraint Block](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14396)
*Kenan Xu,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对ADMM的直接三块扩展问题，证明了即使前两块为强凸二次函数，直接三块ADMM也可能不收敛（给出周期66的有界非收敛轨道反例），并探讨了乘子松弛恢复收敛的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果已知三块ADMM直接扩展可能不收敛，但第三约束块为单位阵（identity）这一特殊子类是否收敛仍是悬而未决的问题——既无一般收敛证明也无反例。作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 利用Codex与GPT-5.6 Sol构建显式有理反例候选，沿分段仿射（piecewise-affine）约化路径验证；通过精确计算确认直接三块ADMM产生周期66的有界非收敛轨道。同时在同一Codex工作流中研究乘子松弛，并引入Kimi Code与Kimi K3模型在无候选指引下独立探索，得到等价的周期23全单位阵实例。

Result: 给出否定答案：即使前两块为强凸二次函数，直接三块ADMM仍可能失败（周期66轨道反例）；问题相关的较小对偶步长可恢复收敛，但不存在对所有实例统一适用的正相对步长；另一工具链独立得到等价的周期23全单位阵反例。

Conclusion: 本文证实直接三块ADMM即使在强凸二次约束块且第三块为单位阵的情况下也可不收敛，彻底解决了该子类的理论悬案；乘子松弛在固定实例与整体类别层面恢复收敛的条件不同；不同AI研究工具（Codex vs Kimi Code）可引导出不同的数学对象和验证路径。

Abstract: The alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), as a landmark algorithm, has attracted tremendous research attention and extensive practical applications over the past two decades. It is well known that, although the two-block ADMM enjoys well-established theoretical convergence guarantees, its direct extension to the three-block case may fail to converge, as demonstrated by existing counterexamples [5]. However, to the best of our knowledge, the case in which the third constraint block is the identity remains unresolved: the existing literature gives neither a general convergence proof nor a counterexample for this subclass. In this paper, we give a negative answer: direct three-block ADMM may fail even when the first two blocks are strongly convex quadratics. Using Codex with GPT-5.6 Sol, we construct an explicit rational counterexample candidate and verify it along a piecewise-affine reduction path; exact checks show that direct three-block ADMM on this instance produces a bounded nonconvergent orbit of period 66. Within the same Codex workflow, we further guide a study of multiplier relaxation and clarify when convergence can be restored at the fixed-instance and class levels: a problem-dependent small dual step can restore convergence, whereas no positive relative step works uniformly over the whole class. Furthermore, we also test the recent Kimi Code with Kimi K3 model without the Codex candidate or project-specific route guidance; along a different path it produces an exact locally attracting period-23 certificate, convertible to an equivalent all-identity instance. The comparison suggests that different research-harness configurations can shape the mathematical objects explored and the certificates pursued.

</details>


### [101] [Residual-Controlled Douglas--Rachford Splitting for Differentiable Solver Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14470)
*Kang Liu,Jianchen Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出RCDRS，一种将可微分优化求解器层视为反馈控制动态系统的可微锥线性规划求解层，通过因果控制器自适应调节松弛与目标驱动参数，保留Douglas-Rachford分裂的投影分裂结构，在理论上保证收敛性与终端诊断可恢复性，实验表明在解质量、可行性和下游决策性能上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定深度展开的可微求解器层必须在解质量、可行性约束和计算预算之间进行权衡，缺乏自适应的机制来平衡这些相互冲突的目标。

Method: 将可微求解器视为反馈控制的动态系统：引入因果控制器自适应调整Douglas-Rachford分裂中的松弛参数和目标驱动参数；理论上分析固定可容许块仍为平均松弛DRS算子并具有有限步不动点残差界；将带安全保护的时间变化展开视为对极限平均算子的可和扰动；从最终分裂状态恢复终端原始-对偶诊断量。

Result: 在混合锥基准和工程应用中，RCDRS相比基线方法在解质量、可行性和下游决策性能上均有提升。

Conclusion: RCDRS通过将展开求解器建模为反馈控制动态系统，在保持DRS投影分裂结构的同时实现了参数自适应，为可微优化层提供了更优的求解质量-可行性-计算预算平衡方案，并已开源。

Abstract: Differentiable solver layers embed constrained optimization into end-to-end learning systems, but fixed-depth unrolling must trade off solution quality, feasibility, and computational budget. We propose Residual-Controlled Douglas--Rachford Splitting (RCDRS), a differentiable solver layer for conic linear programs. RCDRS treats an unrolled solver as a feedback-controlled dynamical system, where a causal controller adapts the relaxation and objective-drive parameters while preserving the projection-splitting structure of Douglas--Rachford splitting. Theoretically, we show that each fixed admissible block remains an averaged relaxed DRS operator and admits finite-step fixed-point residual bounds. We further analyze safeguarded time-varying rollouts as summable perturbations of a limiting averaged operator, and recover terminal primal-dual diagnostics from the final splitting state. Experiments on mixed-cone benchmarks and engineering applications show that RCDRS improves solution quality, feasibility and downstream decision performance. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RC-DRS-180C/.

</details>


### [102] [On quantitative sufficient second-order optimality conditions for elliptic optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14525)
*Francisco Fuica,Nicolai Jork*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类受半线性椭圆方程约束、带盒约束的无Tikhonov正则化的分布最优控制问题，提出了一个量化的二阶最优性条件，该条件保证关于线性化状态L²范数的强制性和二阶条件对扰动（状态与跟踪数据）的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在没有Tikhonov正则化的最优控制问题中，目标函数的二阶变分是关于线性化状态的二次型，其曲率系数函数可能消失或变号，导致传统的二阶充分条件难以保证强制性和稳定性。因此需要提出一种定量条件来确保二阶最优性条件的鲁棒性。

Method: 通过分析二阶变分在无Tikhonov正则化情形下的结构，引入一个量化的曲率条件，该条件隐含关于线性化状态L²范数的强制性（coercivity），并在此基础上证明二阶条件在状态和跟踪数据扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 给出了一个量化条件，该条件能保证关于线性化状态L²范数的强制性，并由此证明了二阶最优性条件对状态扰动和跟踪数据扰动具有稳定性。

Conclusion: 该量化条件为无正则化项的最优控制问题提供了可靠的理论基础，兼顾了最优性验证和稳定性分析两个层面。

Abstract: In this paper, a quantitative condition for optimality for distributed optimal control problems with box-constraints that are subject to a semilinear elliptic equation is considered. An important property of the investigated optimal control problems is the absence of a Tikhonov regularization. It is well known that at a given control, the second variation is a quadratic form in the linearized state, and its curvature coefficient function may vanish or change sign. We present a quantitative condition that implies coercivity with respect to the $L^2$-norm of the linearized-states. As a consequence, the stability of the second-order condition under perturbations of the states and the tracking data is shown.

</details>
