### [103] [A new approach to the reaction-diffusion systems modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13636)
*Maxim Nazarov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于常微分方程组建模反应-扩散系统的新方法，作为偏微分方程在模型层面的纯替代方案。该方法与有限体积法类似，但采用统计简化和几何概率原理描述扩散。其目标是简化反应-扩散系统的定性分析并提高数值实现效率：第一个目标成功实现（可用经典动力系统理论进行定性分析），第二个目标仅部分实现（仅在特定简单初始分子分布和特定扩散系数下效率增益显著）。文章还单独评估了该方法的建模误差以制定实际适用性标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反应-扩散系统数值方法（如直线法）主要基于偏微分方程框架，在定性分析和数值效率上存在局限。作者希望提出一种在模型层面替代PDE的新方法论，旨在简化定性分析并提升数值实现效率。

Method: 基于常微分方程组建模反应-扩散系统，方法描述上与有限体积法相似，但采用统计简化和几何概率原理来描述扩散过程，区别于传统专门数值方法（如直线法）。

Result: 定性分析目标成功达成，可利用经典动力系统理论对模型动态进行分析。数值效率目标仅部分达成，效率提升仅在特定简单初始分子分布及特定扩散系数条件下显著且保持可接受精度。同时通过单独评估建模误差制定了实际适用性判据。

Conclusion: 新方法在模型层面为反应-扩散系统提供了PDE的替代方案，在定性分析方面具有优势，但其数值效率优势具有条件性，需根据初始条件和扩散系数来判定实际适用性。

Abstract: We consider a new methodology for modelling the reaction-diffusion systems based on systems of ordinary differential equations. In contrary to the specialised numerical methods like straight line method, this new methodology is positioned as a pure alternative at the model level for partial differential equations. In its description, the new method is largely similar to the finite volume method, but unlike the latter, it uses statistical simplifications and principles of geometric probability to describe diffusion. The main objectives of this approach are to simplify the qualitative analysis of reaction-diffusion systems and to improve the efficiency of numerical model implementation. The first objective is successfully addressed, as it becomes possible to use the apparatus of classical dynamical systems theory for a qualitative analysis of model dynamics based on the systems of ordinary differential equations. The second objective is only partially addressed, as the gain in efficiency while maintaining acceptable accuracy for numerical implementation will be significant only for certain simple initial distribution of molecules and for specific diffusion coefficients. Furthermore, to formulate criteria for practical applicability, we separately evaluate the modelling error using this new methodology.

</details>


### [104] [An adaptive superconvergent mixed finite element method for exactly symmetric linear elasticity based on local residual minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13701)
*Ernesto Cáceres,Patrick Vega*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对线性弹性问题的严格对称混合有限元离散格式，提出了一种后验误差估计器。该方法将Stenberg型后处理方案重新表述为离散对偶范数下的局部残差最小化问题，从而以零额外计算成本获得对偶变量（Riesz代表），并构建误差指示器。文中建立了可靠性和效率估计，参数关于Lamé常数的依赖性被显式追踪，且一个指示器可同时适用于可压缩和不可压缩两种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于超圆（hypercircle）的误差估计器在不可压缩极限（λ→∞）下需要构建完全不同的结构，缺乏统一的误差估计框架。作者希望设计一个单一的后验误差指示器，能够同时适用于可压缩和不可压缩两种情形，并保证常数在λ→∞时保持有界。

Method: 将Stenberg型后处理格式重新表述为离散对偶范数下的局部残差最小化问题，通过对偶变量自然地获得Riesz代表作为误差指示器。在可压缩情形下利用可靠性估计（显式追踪Lamé参数依赖）和无辅助泡函数的局部效率估计；在不可压缩极限下基于鲁棒稳定性性质和Oswald平均算子建立替代可靠性估计，使常数在λ→∞时保持有界。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论结果，包括具有已知奇异解的基准问题和无解析解的基准问题。该估计器在所有情形下均保持效率和可靠性，且不随Lamé参数退化，同时实现了可压缩与不可压缩情形的统一框架。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种统一的、鲁棒的a posteriori误差估计器，以单一指示器和单一比较范数同时覆盖可压缩和不可压缩两种弹性情形，克服了现有hypercircle方法需要分情形构建的限制。

Abstract: We introduce an a posteriori error estimator for exactly symmetric mixed finite element discretizations of linear elasticity, based on recasting a Stenberg-type postprocessing scheme as a local residual minimization problem in a discrete dual norm. This construction yields, as a dual variable and at no additional computational cost, a Riesz representative of the associated local residual, from which we build an a posteriori error indicator. We establish a reliability estimate, with the dependence on the Lamé parameters tracked explicitly, and a local efficiency estimate, without auxiliary bubble functions, in the standard compressible regime, as well as an alternative reliability estimate, based on a robust stability property and an Oswald averaging operator, with a constant that remains bounded as $λ\to\infty$; the local efficiency estimate holds, with the same bounded behavior, uniformly in both regimes. Notably, a single indicator and a single comparison norm serve both regimes, in contrast with existing hypercircle-based estimators, which require a distinct construction for the incompressible limit. Numerical examples, including a benchmark with a known singular solution and one without an analytical solution, validate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [105] [Approximating matrix functions by block Krylov methods with randomized vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13714)
*Josh Kane,Lucas Onisk,Lothar Reichel,Giuseppe Rodriguez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用随机化分块Krylov方法近似计算f(A)b的问题，其中A为大型方阵、f为函数、b为向量。数值实验表明，将b与几个随机向量组合作为初始块向量的分块Krylov方法，相比标准Krylov方法可以缩短计算时间并减少迭代步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在应用数学的多个领域中（如指数积分、矩阵函数求值等），需要计算f(A)b的表达式。当A规模很大时，直接计算f(A)代价过高，通常通过Krylov子空间方法来近似。标准Krylov方法在大型问题中可能收敛缓慢或计算开销大，因此需要探索更高效的方法。

Method: 本文探索了几种随机化分块Krylov方法的变体，用于近似f(A)b。核心思想是构造初始块向量，不仅包含原始向量b，还加入若干随机生成的向量，从而在分块Krylov子空间中进行近似计算。这样只需将f计算在一个小矩阵上即可。

Result: 计算实例表明，采用包含b及少量随机向量的初始块向量的分块Krylov方法，比标准Krylov方法所需计算时间更短，并且减少了Krylov步骤的数量。

Conclusion: 随机化分块Krylov方法为大型矩阵函数-向量积计算提供了一种有效的替代方案，能够在不牺牲精度的前提下提升计算效率，值得在实际应用中进一步推广和验证。

Abstract: The need to evaluate expressions of the form $f(A)\mathbf{b}$, where $A$ is a square matrix, $f$ is a function, and $\mathbf{b}$ is a vector, arises in several areas of applied mathematics. When the matrix $A$ is very large, it is usually not attractive to evaluate $f(A)$. Instead, $f(A)\mathbf{b}$ often is approximated by computing an estimate in a Krylov subspace that depends on $A$ and $\mathbf{b}$, and only requires that $f$ be evaluated at a small matrix. This paper explores the application of several variants of randomized block Krylov methods to the approximation of $f(A)\mathbf{b}$. Computed examples suggest that block Krylov methods with an initial block vector that contains $\mathbf{b}$ as well as a few randomly generated vectors may require less computing time and reduce the number of Krylov steps than standard Krylov methods.

</details>


### [106] [An Efficient Solver for Finite Element-based Constrained Transport in 3D Magnetohydrodynamics Applied to Magnetic Confinement Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13829)
*Golo A. Wimmer,Konstantin Lipnikov,Ben S. Southworth,Xian-Zhu Tang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向托卡马克电阻磁流体力学中刚性磁波耦合的高效求解器框架，采用隐式-隐式(IMIM)时间分裂分离快速磁波与各向异性热输运，利用各向异性curl-curl结构结合AMS多重网格求解器，并通过curl-共形有限元空间保持离散磁散度约束，在三维非线性托卡马克测试中验证了求解效率与结构保持离散化的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托卡马克电阻MHD模拟中，快速磁波与慢声学动力学的时间尺度差异悬殊，形成刚性stiff问题，导致传统显式或统一隐式方法代价高昂。如何在真实托卡马克几何上高效求解这一耦合系统是核心挑战。

Method: 采用IMIM时间分裂方法分离快磁波与各向异性热输运及慢声学动力学，保持耦合的同时将磁波子系统化为各向异性curl-curl算子；对磁场使用curl-共形有限元空间，并设计速度空间以保留Lorentz力耦合诱导的curl-curl结构；采用AMS多重网格求解器求解所得线性系统，保持离散磁散度约束。

Result: 在完全非线性的三维托卡马克测试算例中，验证了求解器的效率以及结构保持离散化方法的精度与稳定性，证明了AMS求解器与传统方法相比具备可扩展性优势。

Conclusion: 该框架通过耦合友好的时间分裂与兼容离散化的组合，在保留物理结构（离散磁散度约束）的同时实现了高效可扩展的求解，为大规模托卡马克MHD模拟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present an efficient solver framework for the stiff magnetic wave coupling arising in resistive magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) on realistic tokamak geometries. The approach builds on an implicit-implicit (IMIM) time-splitting that separates fast magnetic waves and anisotropic heat transport from slower acoustic dynamics while retaining full coupling (Krzysik et al. 2026). Within this formulation, the magnetic wave subsystem appears as an anisotropic curl-curl operator, enabling the use of scalable auxiliary-space Maxwell (AMS) multigrid solvers. To exploit this structure at the discrete level, we employ curl-conforming finite element spaces for the magnetic field and design the velocity space to preserve the curl-curl structure induced by the Lorentz-force coupling. The resulting compatible discretization preserves the discrete magnetic divergence constraint while producing linear systems directly amenable to efficient AMS-based solvers. We demonstrate solver efficiency as well as the accuracy and stability of the resulting structure-preserving discretization on fully nonlinear three-dimensional tokamak test cases.

</details>


### [107] [Vanishing Clutter: Fast and Accurate Shape Imaging via Active Cloaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13839)
*Haoqiang Xiao,Guang-Hui Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出利用主动隐身技术消除近场扫描光学显微镜（NSOM）中探针引起的干扰伪影，通过最优控制理论和分层等离激元结构中局部异常共振理论，获得精确闭式解，实现快速、无伪影的定量形状重建，相比传统迭代方法加速数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NSOM成像本质受限于测量伪影和探针-样品多次散射导致的数据污染。探针虽为亚波长分辨率所必需，但会固有地扰动局域场，降低信噪比，使定量形状重建的逆问题高度不适定，亟需一种消除探针干扰的新机制。

Method: 首先建立正演模型的适定性，提供严格的数学基础；然后将主动隐身（传统上作为成像的对抗手段）重新定位为消除探针诱导干扰的赋能机制。通过将探针隐身问题建模为最优控制问题，证明其极小值的存在性和稳定性；利用分层等离激元结构中局部异常共振理论推导精确闭式极小值，避免计算代价高昂的迭代求解。

Result: 隐身驱动的干扰消除产生了近乎无探针的测量环境，实现了快速且无伪影的形状重建。大量数值实验表明，与传统直接对含探针污染数据应用迭代方法相比，所提方法在形状重建精度上表现优异，计算速度加速数个数量级，验证了方法的鲁棒性和变革潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了主动隐身与成像的创造性和谐结合，为高保真亚波长成像提供了一种兼具理论严格性和计算高效性的新范式，有望显著推动NSOM在纳米尺度表征领域的发展。

Abstract: Imaging a target sample in near-field scanning optical microscopy (NSOM) is fundamentally limited by measurement artifacts and data contamination from multiple probe-sample scattering. The probe, essential for subwavelength resolution, inherently perturbs the local field, degrading the signal-to-noise ratio and rendering the inverse problem for quantitative shape reconstruction highly ill-posed. We first establish the well-posedness of the corresponding forward model, providing a rigorous foundation for subsequent imaging. We then repurpose active cloaking--conventionally the antagonist of imaging--as an enabling mechanism to eliminate probe-induced interference. Rather than directly reconstructing the sample from corrupted data, we actively cloak the probe by formulating an optimal control problem and prove the existence and stability of its minimizers. Leveraging the theory of localized anomalous resonance in layered plasmonic structures, we derive an exact closed-form minimizer, thereby circumventing the computationally prohibitive iterative solution of the optimal control problem. The resulting cloaking-driven interference removal yields a virtually probe-free measurement environment, enabling fast, artifact-free shape reconstruction. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate accurate shape reconstruction and dramatic acceleration--often by orders of magnitude--over conventional iterative methods applied directly to probe--contaminated data without cloaking--based preprocessing, validating the robustness and transformative potential of the proposed approach for high-fidelity subwavelength imaging.

</details>


### [108] [PDE Realization and Structure-Aware Solvers for a Compact Two-Stage Fourth-Order IMEX Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13875)
*Zhixin Huo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对刚性分裂问题的紧凑型两阶段四阶二导数隐式-显式时间离散化的PDE实现与求解器框架，通过二阶ADER/Cauchy-Kowalevski局部演化实现四阶外层组合，采用非精确阶段分析得到保持四阶精度的非对称中点与端点残差容差，并利用矩阵自由方法保留完整混合作用，仅在求逆中近似主导刚性物理，从而构建二次型与移位预条件子、Fourier与多层实现等求解机制。数值消融实验与二维Brusselator及刚性前沿基准验证了精度、容差与区域依赖的效率主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的核心动机是解决紧凑型两阶段四阶二导数隐式-显式（IMEX）时间离散化在求解刚性分裂问题时的"一致性-成本耦合"难题：保持时间积分器的混合显式-隐式相互作用需要全场时间微分，但同一相互作用会拓宽隐式阶段算子，使每次求解显著变贵。因此需要设计一种既能维持四阶精度的混合相互作用、又能控制求解成本的实现框架。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 用二阶ADER/Cauchy-Kowalevski局部演化替代全场时间微分以保留混合相互作用并支持四阶外层组合；(2) 建立光滑重建一致性与全离散误差平衡，推导分裂场自微分产生的领先Lie括号缺陷；(3) 通过非精确阶段分析给出保持四阶精度的非对称中点与端点残差容差；(4) 对拓宽阶段，采用矩阵自由方法保留完整混合作用，仅在逆算子中近似主导刚性物理，从而得到二次型与移位预条件子、Fourier与多层实现、半线性反应-扩散约简以及松弛系统的源局域消去。

Result: 数值消融实验验证了一致性与容差结果的一致性；二维Brusselator在两种网格上的研究表明在有效精度范围内出现了有利的误差-墙钟时间权衡；经典刚性前沿基准测试识别出独立的空间激波-源限制。此外，导出了精确二次消去、扩散主导的Fourier估计以及Jin-Xin松弛的ε/Δx界等关键机制解释。

Conclusion: 该框架支持的是依赖于工况（regime-dependent）的效率主张而非普适加速。方法的精度与求解器有效性均有严格的理论保证和数值验证，但对于包含空间激波-源的挑战性工况仍存在独立限制，需要在特定刚性场景中选择性地使用该框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a PDE realization and solver framework for a compact two-stage fourth-order two-derivative implicit--explicit time discretization for stiff split problems. The key difficulty is a consistency--cost coupling: full-field temporal differentiation is required to preserve the mixed explicit--implicit interactions of the time integrator, but the same interactions widen the implicit stage operators and can make each solve substantially more expensive. We show that a second-order ADER/Cauchy--Kowalevski local evolution is sufficient for the fourth-order outer composition, establish smooth reconstruction consistency and a fully discrete error balance, and derive the leading Lie-bracket defect produced by self differentiation of the split fields. An inexact-stage analysis gives asymmetric midpoint and endpoint residual tolerances that preserve fourth-order accuracy. For the widened stages, the complete mixed action is retained matrix-free while only dominant stiff physics is approximated in the inverse. This yields quadratic and shifted preconditioners, Fourier and multilevel realizations, a semilinear reaction--diffusion reduction, and source-local elimination for relaxation systems. Exact quadratic cancellation, a diffusion-dominated Fourier estimate, and an $\varepsilon/Δx$ bound for Jin--Xin relaxation explain the main solver mechanisms. Numerical ablations verify the consistency and tolerance results, while a two-dimensional Brusselator study on two grids shows favorable error-versus-wall-time behavior over a useful accuracy range. A classical stiff-front benchmark also identifies the separate spatial shock--source limitation. The results support a regime-dependent efficiency claim rather than a universal speedup.

</details>


### [109] [Nonparametric Schrödinger Bridge Time Series Generator: Algorithm, Convergence Analysis and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13968)
*Daili Sheng,Minghui Song,Hui Sun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对Schrödinger Bridge Time Series (SBTS)数据生成器进行了完整的收敛性分析，证明了在适当的集成大小和核带宽选择下，Euler-Maruyama离散化以半阶收敛速度收敛到混合目标分布；同时证明了当混合概率趋于零时，正则化分布收敛到原始目标分布。该分析同时涵盖了集成逼近误差、核逼近误差和时间离散化误差，为SBTS生成器提供了完整的分步收敛性结果。实验部分进一步验证了用更一般的SDE诱导的路径测度替换Wiener参考测度的灵活性，两种方案表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBTS作为基于Schrödinger Bridge的时间序列数据生成方法已被广泛应用，但其理论收敛性质尚未得到严格证明。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为SBTS生成器提供完整的分布收敛性分析，并探索该方法在更一般参考测度下的灵活性。

Method: 从正则化框架出发，将数据集成与标准多元高斯分布按一定概率混合；运用Euler-Maruyama离散化方法，在合适的集成大小和核带宽条件下推导收敛率；通过让混合概率趋于零建立正则化分布到原始目标分布的收敛；同时通过数值实验将Wiener参考测度替换为更一般SDE诱导的路径测度来验证方法灵活性。

Result: 理论上证明了Euler-Maruyama离散化以半阶收敛速度收敛到混合目标分布，且正则化分布随混合概率趋于零而收敛到原始目标分布；实验上证明了基于Wiener参考测度和基于SDE诱导参考测度的方案性能相当，验证了SBTS框架的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文为SBTS数据生成器提供了严密的分布收敛性理论保证，统一处理了集成逼近、核逼近和时间离散化三类误差源，同时通过实验展示了该方法在更一般参考测度下的适应性和鲁棒性，为SBTS框架的理论基础和应用推广提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: We conduct a convergence analysis for the Schrödinger Bridge Time Series (SBTS) data generator. Starting from a regularized formulation in which the data ensemble is mixed with a standard multivariate Gaussian distribution with a prescribed probability, we prove that the Euler-Maruyama discretization converges to the mixed target distribution with half-order convergence rate, provided that the ensemble size and kernel bandwidth are chosen appropriately. We further show that the regularized distribution converges to the original target distribution as the mixing probability tends to zero. The analysis simultaneously accounts for the ensemble approximation error, kernel approximation error, and time-discretization error, and therefore provides a full distributional convergence result for the SBTS generator. Empirically, we further examine the flexibility of the method by replacing the Wiener reference measure with the path measure induced by a more general SDE. The numerical experiments show that the schemes based on both the original Wiener reference measure and the SDE-induced reference measure achieve comparable performance, demonstrating the robustness and stability of the SBTS framework.

</details>


### [110] [An $h$-adaptive Tetrahedral Spectral Element Method with Applications to Kohn-Sham Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14006)
*Zeyu Wang,Hongfei Zhan,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的四面体网格h-自适应谱元法框架，通过单元方向对齐与几何红绿细化相结合的策略消除代数悬挂节点约束，引入基于拓扑的点定位算法加速自适应网格间的插值，并在全电子Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论等应用中验证了其谱收敛性、精确解析核奇异性的能力以及出色的并行可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四面体网格上的高阶h-自适应谱元法虽能有效处理复杂三维几何中的局部奇异性和多尺度结构，但在细化界面维持C0连续性以及在自适应网格间高效传递解方面存在困难。这些限制在全电子Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论等严苛应用中尤为突出，因为这类问题对核奇异性和多物理尺度的精确分辨提出了很高要求。

Method: 提出一个高效的h-自适应四面体谱元框架，包含两个关键创新：(1) 结合单元方向对齐与几何红绿细化（red-green refinement）的自适应策略，无需代数悬挂节点约束即可保持单元间连续性；(2) 基于拓扑的点定位算法，用于加速自适应网格间的解插值传递。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在Poisson方程和Laplacian特征值问题上实现谱收敛；在全电子Kohn-Sham方程应用中能精确解析核奇异性；并行性能研究显示良好的可扩展性——矩阵装配和自适应模块在64核配置下相较单核通常获得15倍以上加速，所提出的插值算法获得25至35倍的加速比。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为大尺度高分辨率仿真提供了一种精确、稳健且高效的解决方案，兼具谱收敛性、核奇异性分辨能力和卓越的并行性能。

Abstract: High-order $h$-adaptive spectral element methods on tetrahedral meshes provide an effective framework for resolving localized singularities and multiscale structures in complex three-dimensional geometries. However, their development is often hindered by difficulties in maintaining $C^0$ continuity across refinement interfaces and efficiently transferring solutions between adaptive meshes. Such limitations are particularly relevant in demanding applications such as all-electron Kohn-Sham density functional theory, which place stringent requirements on the accurate resolution of both nuclear singularities and multiple physical scales. In this paper, we present an efficient $h$-adaptive tetrahedral spectral element framework. To address the continuity challenge, we develop an adaptive strategy that combines element orientation alignment with geometric red-green refinement, thereby eliminating the need for algebraic hanging-node constraints while preserving inter-element continuity. Furthermore, an efficient topology-based point-location algorithm is introduced to accelerate interpolation between adaptive meshes. Numerical experiments on Poisson and Laplacian eigenvalue problems confirm the spectral convergence of the proposed method. Applications to all-electron Kohn-Sham equations further demonstrate its capability to accurately resolve nuclear singularities. Moreover, parallel performance studies exhibit excellent scalability, with matrix assembly and adaptivity modules generally achieving speedups above 15 times and the proposed interpolation algorithm attaining speedups ranging from 25 to 35 on 64-core configurations compared to the single-core performance. These results indicate that the proposed framework provides an accurate, robust, and efficient solution for large-scale, high-resolution simulations.

</details>


### [111] [Unconditionally stable and energy conserving discretization of the dynamic von Kármán equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14087)
*Carsten Carstensen,Neela Nataraj,Ricardo Ruiz-Baier,Aamir Yousuf*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对动态von Kármán方程提出了一种全离散近似格式：空间上采用非协调Morley有限元方法，时间上采用能量守恒且修正的、无条件稳定的Newmark二阶时间步进格式。利用Brouwer不动点定理证明全离散格式解的存在性，在小载荷下进一步获得解的惟一性和稳定性估计，并在分片能量范数下推导了最优阶先验误差估计（时间方向二次收敛）。数值实验验证了理论误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态von Kármán方程描述了薄板的大挠度振动行为，其求解涉及强非线性耦合以及空间和时间方向的复杂性。已有方法要么缺乏稳定性保证，要么时间精度不足，需要一种既能保持能量、又无条件稳定且具有二阶时间精度的全离散数值方案。

Method: 采用非协调Morley有限元进行空间离散，结合修正的无条件稳定Newmark二阶时间步进格式（具有能量守恒性质）；通过Brouwer不动点定理证明解的存在性，在小载荷假设下建立解的惟一性与稳定性估计，并推导分片能量范数下的最优阶先验误差估计。

Result: 证明了全离散格式解的存在性（Brouwer不动点定理）、小载荷下的惟一性与稳定性；获得了分片能量范数下时间方向二次收敛的最优阶先验误差估计；数值实验结果与理论误差界一致。

Conclusion: 该全离散格式在保持能量守恒和二阶时间精度的同时，具备无条件稳定性，理论上完备（存在性、惟一性、稳定性、误差估计均有严格证明），数值实验验证了理论结果，为动态von Kármán方程的高效可靠数值求解提供了坚实方案。

Abstract: A fully discrete approximation of the dynamic von Kármán equations combines nonconforming Morley finite element methods for spatial discretization with an energy conserving modified unconditionally stable Newmark second- order time-stepping scheme. Brouwer's fixed-point theorem establishes existence of a solution to the fully discrete scheme and further uniqueness and stability estimates follow for small loads. Optimal order a priori error estimates in the piecewise energy norm with quadratic convergence in time are derived for the fully discrete scheme. The results of the numerical experiments validate the theoretical error bounds.

</details>


### [112] [Adaptive multigrid for high-order discontinuous Galerkin methods based on the full approximation scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14123)
*Jörg Stiller*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Brandt全近似格式（FAS）的自适应多重网格方法来求解椭圆问题的间断伽辽金（DG）离散格式。该方法利用重叠Schwarz光滑器（可选Krylov加速），在不引入悬挂节点的情况下实现对六面体网格的局部hp细化。数值实验验证了该方法的高效率、对高纵横比及变形网格的鲁棒性，并展示了其在动态并行网格自适应和不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题中的扩展能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在DG格式的多重网格求解中实现局部hp细化通常需要悬挂节点，增加了实现复杂度和计算开销。作者旨在开发一种无需悬挂节点即可实现局部细化的高效多重网格求解器，同时保持对非结构化弯曲网格的适用性和张量积结构带来的快速对角化优势。

Method: 采用Brandt全近似格式（FAS）构建自适应多重网格方法。核心组件是专为无结构弯曲网格设计的重叠Schwarz光滑器，该光滑器可选用Krylov子空间方法加速，并在保持张量积结构的基础上实现快速对角化。通过FAS框架实现局部hp细化，避免了悬挂节点的需求。

Result: 数值实验表明FAS-MG方法具有极高的求解效率。专门的鲁棒性研究证实该方法对高纵横比单元、单元变形和不规则网格拓扑均表现稳健。成功通过Červený-Dobrev-Kolev波前基准测试验证了动态并行网格自适应能力，并初步验证了向不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题的扩展可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的FAS-MG方法是一种高效且稳健的DG椭圆问题求解方案，无需悬挂节点即可实现局部hp细化，在极端网格条件和动态并行自适应场景下表现优异，并具有向更复杂流体问题（如Navier-Stokes）推广的潜力。

Abstract: We propose an adaptive multigrid (MG) method for discontinuous Galerkin formulations of elliptic problems using Brandt's full approximation scheme (FAS). Unlike common approaches, this method achieves local $hp$-refinement of hexahedral meshes without the need for hanging nodes. The core component of the FAS-MG method is an overlapping Schwarz smoother, which is optionally accelerated by a Krylov method. This smoother is designed for unstructured curvilinear meshes but maintains a tensor-product structure for fast diagonalization. Numerical experiments demonstrate the exceptional efficiency of the FAS-MG method. Dedicated studies confirm its robustness against high aspect ratios, element deformation, and irregular mesh topology. We also verify its capability for dynamic parallel mesh adaptation using the wave-front benchmark of Červený, Dobrev, and Kolev (SIAM J. Sci. Comp. 41, 2019). Finally, we present preliminary results of extending the method to incompressible Navier-Stokes problems.

</details>


### [113] [TOGEARI: Interaction-Space Preconditioning for Condensed Finite-Element Systems with IPC Contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14162)
*Yanlin Liu,Chao Huang,Kaixiang Yao,Yao Shen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出TOGEARI方法，将稠密有限元接触系统中薄层因子化接触更新压缩到低维因子空间，通过Woodbury右预条件子在不改变牛顿方程和残差测试的情况下加速求解。在325,260自由度的冻结系统中，8维子空间将Arnoldi基从11个向量减少到5个，中位时间从2.849秒降至1.629秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密有限元接触系统由大型材料体积核心和薄层因子化接触更新组成，接触更新的计算与求解成本昂贵。需要一种因子空间压缩方法，在不重构整个系统的情况下降低求解复杂度并提升效率。

Method: 方法包含：（1）TOGEARI框架：选择低维接触组合空间，通过Woodbury公式作为右预条件子安装其贡献，保持牛顿方程和独立残差测试不变；（2）核心响应选择：假设对称核心，支持不定核心的求解；对正定核心具有能量排序，给出精确广义谱、缩放逆误差恒等式和每维最优最坏省略交互；（3）替代性的原始接触因子选择器，设置成本更低；（4）推导了二次四面体位移公式、局部凝聚逐元素常压力及因子化正半定IPC法向切线的分裂。

Result: 在325,260自由度的冻结系统中，94行接触因子注册数值秩为42，8维子空间将Arnoldi基从11向量减至5；核心响应空间与原始空间在主要和近重复提取上紧密对齐；行范数和确定性随机控制需要12个向量。保留维度扫描将暖中位数从2.849秒降至1.629秒，但加载后设置加首次求解从53.885秒增至61.958秒。

Conclusion: TOGEARI在冻结无摩擦单一状态下识别出紧凑的交互修正，显著降低迭代求解成本。但不同牛顿状态、演化接触、接触秩与网格缩放、摩擦、嵌套压力空间性能等仍是开放实验问题，需要进一步验证。

Abstract: Condensed finite-element contact systems combine a large material-volumetric core with a thin factorized contact update. We formulate Truncated Operator-Gram Eigenspace Approximation of Relevant Interactions (TOGEARI), a factor-space compression of that update. It selects a low-dimensional space of contact combinations and installs their contribution as a Woodbury right preconditioner. The assembled Newton equation and independent residual test remain fixed. The inverse action requires an invertible core and reduced Woodbury system. Core-response selection additionally assumes a symmetric core and remains available when that core is indefinite. For a positive definite core, the response values have an energy ordering. The same analysis then gives the exact generalized spectrum, a scaled inverse-error identity, and the optimal worst omitted interaction at each dimension. A raw contact-factor selector provides a lower-setup empirical alternative. We derive the split for a quadratic-tetrahedral displacement formulation with locally condensed elementwise-constant pressure and a factorized positive-semidefinite IPC normal tangent. In a frozen system with 325,260 free coordinates, an eight-dimensional subspace of a 94-row contact factor with registered numerical rank 42 reduces the Arnoldi basis from 11 vectors to 5. The core-response and raw spaces are closely aligned on the primary and near-repeat extractions; row-norm and deterministic-random controls require 12 vectors. The retained-dimension sweep reduces the warm median from 2.849 s to 1.629 s. Post-load setup plus first solve changes from 53.885 s to 61.958 s. These results identify a compact interaction correction in one frozen, frictionless regime. Distinct Newton states, evolving contact, contact-rank and mesh scaling, friction, and nested pressure-space performance remain open experimental questions.

</details>


### [114] [Analysis of a Conforming Finite Element Method for Second-Harmonic Generation Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14300)
*Ansh Desai,Peter Monk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了含精确Dirichlet-to-Neumann边界条件的标量二次谐波产生散射问题的p次连续多项式有限元近似，在小数据条件下证明了连续与离散非线性解的存在唯一性，推导了拟最优H¹误差估计，并分析了不动点迭代的收敛性及数值实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性声学和电磁波传播在频域中可由两个耦合非线性Helmholtz方程（二次谐波产生系统）建模，但这类非线性散射问题的有限元逼近的严格数学分析（存在唯一性、误差估计、迭代收敛性）尚缺，需要理论保证和量化分析。

Method: 采用连续分段p次多项式有限元离散；利用精确Dirichlet-to-Neumann边界条件截断有界计算域；在涉及入射场和非线性极化率的小数据条件下，构造非线性Céa型论证，结合线性Galerkin拟最优性和耦合非线性项的小数据稳定性估计推导误差界；并分析用于求解离散非线性解的不动点迭代的收敛性。

Result: 在小数据条件下证明了连续和离散非线性解在小球内存在且唯一；推导了拟最优的H¹先验误差估计；量化了有限元逼近与非线迭代误差的联合效应；制造解实验验证了预测的有限元收敛阶，PML散射计算展示了二、三维中非线性不动点求解器的行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为二次谐波产生非线性散射问题的有限元方法提供了完整的理论分析框架，包括解的存在唯一性、误差估计和迭代收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: In the frequency domain, nonlinear acoustic and electromagnetic wave propagation can, in certain regimes, be modeled by second-harmonic generation systems consisting of two coupled nonlinear Helmholtz equations. We analyze a conforming finite element approximation of a scalar second-harmonic generation scattering problem truncated using an exact Dirichlet-to-Neumann boundary condition. The discretization uses continuous piecewise polynomial finite elements of degree $p$. Under small-data conditions involving the incident-field and nonlinear susceptibilities, we prove existence and uniqueness of the continuous and discrete nonlinear solutions in a prescribed small ball. In the same regime, we derive quasi-optimal a priori $H^1$-error estimates by a nonlinear Céa-type argument combining linear Galerkin quasi-optimality with small-data stability estimates for the coupled nonlinearities. We also analyze the convergence of the fixed-point iteration used to compute the discrete nonlinear solution and quantify the combined effects of finite element approximation and nonlinear iteration error. A manufactured-solution experiment illustrates the predicted finite element convergence rates, while additional PML-based scattering computations demonstrate the behavior of the nonlinear fixed-point solver in two and three dimensions.

</details>


### [115] [Coupling-Aware Vanka Smoothing for Multigrid Preconditioning of the Implicit Immersed Boundary Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14310)
*Cole Gruninger,Boyce E. Griffith*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种耦合感知Vanka（CAV）平滑策略，用于对隐式浸没边界（IB）方法中约化后的欧拉速度-压力线性系统进行有效的多重网格预条件。CAV补丁通过合并标准压力中心Vanka补丁构建，其合并方式由欧拉弹性矩阵的图结构决定。在网格细化下补丁大小保持有界，每个多重网格循环的计算成本随欧拉自由度线性增长。采用目标点、膜和梁力定律的测试表明，CAV预条件的FGMRES可在9–15次迭代内将相对残差降低十个数量级；在流经柔性纤维的非线性基准测试中，FGMRES平均迭代次数随网格从32×32细化到256×256仅从8.6增加到9.5。这是首个针对时变隐式IB公式的鲁棒多重网格策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浸没边界方法中显式时间离散化受结构刚度约束限制时间步长，隐式处理虽能解除此限制但导致线性系统求解难度随刚度增大而增加，亟需有效的多重网格预条件策略来高效求解该系统的约化形式。

Method: 提出耦合感知Vanka（CAV）平滑策略：将标准压力中心Vanka补丁进行组合，组合依据是欧拉弹性矩阵的图结构（即耦合关系），从而实现对该约化欧拉速度-压力IB系统的有效多重网格预条件。CAV补丁大小在网格细化下保持有界，保证了计算复杂度随欧拉自由度线性增长。

Result: 对目标点、膜和梁力定律的测试表明，CAV预条件的FGMRES在9–15次迭代内将残差降低十个数量级，且随网格细化增长很小。在柔性纤维流动非线性基准中，FGMRES平均迭代次数从8.6仅增至9.5（网格由32×32细化至256×256）。

Conclusion: CAV提供了首个针对时变隐式IB公式的鲁棒多重网格策略，在网格细化和结构刚度变化下均保持高效稳定的收敛性能，为隐式浸没边界方法在实际应用中的可扩展求解奠定了基础。

Abstract: The immersed boundary (IB) method models fluid--structure interaction using the natural Lagrangian and Eulerian formulations of structural mechanics and fluid dynamics, respectively, but explicit time discretization of the IB force imposes a stiffness-dependent upper bound on the time-step size. Treating these forces implicitly removes this restriction, but the resulting coupled linear systems become increasingly difficult to solve as the structural stiffness increases. Algebraically eliminating the Lagrangian degrees of freedom yields a reduced Eulerian velocity--pressure IB system. Here we introduce a coupling-aware Vanka (CAV) smoothing strategy to enable effective multigrid preconditioning of this system. CAV patches are built as unions of standard pressure-centered Vanka patches, with the graph of the Eulerian elasticity matrix determining which patches are combined. Under grid refinement, CAV patch sizes remain bounded, and the computational cost of each multigrid cycle thereby grows linearly with the number of Eulerian degrees of freedom. Tests using target-point, membrane, and beam force laws show that CAV-preconditioned FGMRES reduces the relative residual by ten orders of magnitude in $9--15$ iterations, with little growth under grid refinement. In a nonlinear benchmark modeling flow past a flexible fiber, the average number of FGMRES iterations per Newton solve increases only from $8.6$ to $9.5$ as the Eulerian grid is refined from $32\times32$ to $256\times256$ cells. To our knowledge, CAV provides the first robust multigrid strategy for time-dependent implicit IB formulations.

</details>


### [116] [Reduced Order Modeling and Applications to Inverse State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14328)
*Olga Mula,Agustín Somacal*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了用于参数化偏微分方程（PDEs）的降阶建模（ROM）方法，将其形式化为监督学习任务，涵盖线性近似和非线性方法，并展示其在状态估计逆问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最优控制、不确定性量化和反问题等领域中，需要对大量不同参数值反复求解参数化PDEs，而传统的经典离散化方法计算代价过高，难以满足快速在线评估的需求。

Method: 将参数化PDEs的近似问题表述为监督学习任务；首先回顾适用于椭圆和抛物问题的线性近似方法，然后引入处理解具有不连续性或陡峭梯度时所需的非线性方法；进一步展示如何利用降阶模型从有限测量数据中高效恢复物理系统状态（即状态估计这一反问题）。

Result: 本文侧重于具有理论保证的结果，同时讨论实用方法，并提供配套的Jupyter notebook来实现主要算法。

Conclusion: 降阶建模提供了一种对参数化解集进行压缩而精确表示的有效途径，能够实现快速在线评估，并在以理论保证为导向的同时兼顾实际应用的可操作性。

Abstract: Solving parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) repeatedly for many parameter values,as needed in optimal control, uncertainty quantification, and inverse problems, is often prohibitive with classical discretizations. This chapter introduces Reduced Order Modeling (ROM), which builds compressed yet accurate representations of parametric solution sets for fast online evaluation. We formulate the approximation of parametric PDEs as a supervised learning task and review linear approximation, very effective for elliptic and parabolic problems, before turning to nonlinear methods needed when solutions exhibit discontinuities or steep gradients. We then show how to use reduced-order models to efficiently recover the state of a physical system from limited measurements, which is an inverse problem known as state estimation. Throughout, we favor results with theoretical guarantees while also discussing practical methods, and provide a companion Jupyter notebook implementing the main algorithms.

</details>


### [117] [Geometry-Conforming Finite Element Methods for Interface Problems on Fitted and Unfitted Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14484)
*Yuanhui Lin,Tao Lin,Xu Zhang,Minfu Feng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个任意阶的几何一致有限元（GC-FE）框架，用于二维曲域上的椭圆边值及界面问题。通过Frenet-Serret变换精确表示弯曲边界和界面，避免了曲几何近似带来的几何变分犯罪，并建立了最优误差估计和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元方法在处理曲边界和曲界面时，通常使用多项式近似几何，这引入了几何变分犯罪（geometric variational crime），需要额外的几何一致性估计。作者旨在开发一种能够精确保持预定曲线而不需要几何近似的有限元框架，从而避免这一缺陷。

Method: 利用Frenet-Serret变换精确表示弯曲边界和界面适配段；在Frenet坐标中构造多项式以生成物理坐标中一般非多项式的局部形函数；在界面非适配网格上，将曲边界元上的GC-FE空间与界面切割元上的几何一致浸入有限元（GC-IFE）空间耦合，其余区域使用标准多项式空间。对于适配网格，采用对称内罚间断伽辽金（SIPDG）离散化。

Result: 建立了GC-FE空间的最优逼近、逆和迹估计；对适配网格证明了能量范数和L2范数下的适定性与最优误差估计；数值实验确认了预测的收敛阶，全局精度与节点等参有限元相当，且在实际测试中真实界面迹误差更小，并展示了耦合GC-FE-GC-IFE方法在界面非适配网格上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该GC-FE框架通过在几何表示中精确保留预定曲线，消除了曲几何近似导致的几何变分犯罪，无需相应的几何一致性估计。方法在理论上具有最优收敛性能，数值上精度与等参元相当且界面迹误差更优，并能与浸入有限元方法耦合处理非适配网格上的界面问题。

Abstract: We develop an arbitrary-degree geometry-conforming finite element (GC-FE) framework for two-dimensional elliptic boundary value and interface problems on curved domains. Using the Frenet--Serret transformation, curved-boundary and interface-fitted segments are represented exactly, while polynomials in Frenet coordinates generate generally nonpolynomial local shape functions in physical coordinates. For interface-unfitted meshes, GC-FE spaces on curved-boundary elements are coupled with geometry-conforming immersed finite element (GC-IFE) spaces on interface-cut elements, with standard polynomial spaces used elsewhere. We establish optimal approximation, inverse, and trace estimates for the GC-FE spaces. For fitted meshes, we prove well-posedness and optimal error estimates in energy and $L^2$ norms for a symmetric interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin discretization. By retaining the prescribed curves exactly, the method avoids the geometric variational crime associated with curved-geometry approximation and requires no corresponding geometric consistency estimates. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted rates, show global accuracy comparable to nodal isoparametric finite elements and smaller true-interface trace errors in the reported tests, and demonstrate the coupled GC-FE-GC-IFE method on interface-unfitted meshes.

</details>


### [118] [Hybrid Dealiasing and Implicit Packing for Real Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14497)
*Noel Murasko,John C. Bowman*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种扩展混合去混叠（hybrid dealiasing）算法以处理实值卷积的新方法。第一种算法利用变换域中的共轭对称性直接计算残差贡献；第二种算法通过新的隐式打包技术，将实数数据打包为复数数组，并在变换域中隐式完成所有打包/解包操作。两种算法的多维版本通过递归分解为低维卷积获得。这些算法已在开源FFTW++库中实现，在一维、二维和三维场景下均优于显式零填充的标准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合去混叠是一种基于FFT的计算复数数据线性卷积的方法，通过隐式执行零填充来降低去混叠成本。作者希望将此方法扩展到实值卷积，以获得更高的计算效率。

Method: （1）第一种算法利用实值数据FFT的共轭对称性，直接计算每个残差贡献； （2）第二种算法通过新的隐式打包技术，将实值数据打包成复数数组，并在变换域中隐式执行所有打包/解包操作； （3）通过递归分解为低维卷积，获得两种算法的多维版本； （4）在FFTW++开源库中实现等长输入数组卷积的高效例程。

Result: 新算法在一维、二维和三维卷积中均优于标准的显式零填充方法。在许多情况下，不带混合去混叠的隐式打包算法与完整的混合去混叠算法性能几乎相当，尤其是在多线程场景下。

Conclusion: 提出的两种实值混合去混叠算法在计算效率上优于传统的显式零填充方法，且隐式打包方法在多数情况下已足够高效，为实值卷积提供了一种更优的计算方案。

Abstract: Hybrid dealiasing is an FFT-based method for computing linear convolutions of complex-valued data that reduces the cost of dealiasing by performing zero padding implicitly. We develop two new algorithms that extend hybrid dealiasing to real-valued convolutions.
  The first algorithm exploits conjugate symmetries in the transformed data and computes each residue contribution directly. The second algorithm employs complex-valued hybrid dealiasing via a new implicit packing technique, which packs real-valued data into complex-valued arrays and performs all unpacking and packing operations implicitly in the transformed domain. Multidimensional versions of both algorithms are obtained by recursive decomposition into lower-dimensional convolutions.
  Based on these algorithms, efficient routines to compute convolutions of equal-length input arrays are implemented in the open-source FFTW++ library. These routines outperform the standard method of explicit zero padding in one, two, and three dimensions. In many cases, implicit packing without hybrid dealiasing is nearly as fast as the general hybrid dealiasing algorithm, particularly when multithreading.

</details>


### [119] [Nodal discontinuous Galerkin methods for non-ideal equations of state: pressure equilibrium preservation and entropy correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14506)
*Jesse CHan,Hendrik Ranocha,Raymond Park,Joshua Lampert,Eric Ching,Ayaboe Edoh*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了保结构不连续伽辽金（DG）方法在非理想状态方程下的性能，提出了EPEC和APEC通量差分格式的统一分析框架，并引入最小耗散熵稳定修正以提升长时间模拟和欠分辨解的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真度数值模拟真实流体时，传统DG方法虽保持守恒性，但难以同时保证压力平衡和熵不等式等关键结构性质；对于非理想状态方程，现有熵稳定格式往往引入过大的数值耗散，需要设计既能保持压力平衡又满足熵条件的离散格式。

Method: 提出基于Tadmor shuffle条件推广的EPEC格式设计新流程；分析APEC DG格式并引入耗散界面罚项；结合APEC通量差分格式，采用最小耗散修正实现熵稳定。

Result: EPEC格式分析给出了新的设计原则；APEC格式中界面罚项不会显著增加压力平衡误差（高阶更明显）；APEC与熵修正结合在欠分辨解和长时间模拟中显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 非理想EOS下，APEC通量差分格式配合最小耗散熵修正，在保持压力平衡和熵稳定性的同时可有效提升高阶模拟的鲁棒性，是实际流体模拟中兼顾精度与稳定性的优选方案。

Abstract: Structure-preserving discontinuous Galerkin (DG) methods typically improve the robustness of high order simulations of real fluids. In addition to conservation, key structures include the preservation of pressure equilibrium and satisfaction of at least one entropy inequality. In this work, we investigate conservative discretizations using exactly pressure equilibrium conserving (EPEC) and approximately pressure equilibrium conserving (APEC) flux differencing DG formulations, as well as entropy stable formulations through the use of minimally dissipative corrections for non-ideal equations of state (EOS).
  We introduce an analysis of EPEC schemes and a new procedure for designing such fluxes based on a generalization of Tadmor's shuffle condition. We also analyze APEC DG schemes and show that the incorporation of dissipative interface penalization terms does not significantly increase pressure equilibrium errors, especially at higher orders of approximation. Finally, we observe that when combined with APEC flux differencing formulations, entropy correction improves robustness for under-resolved solutions and long-time simulations.

</details>


### [120] [Comment on arXiv:2106.08363v3 [math.NA], E. Abreu, A. Espirito Santo, W. Lambert, and J. Perez, Convergence of a Lagrangian--Eulerian scheme by a weak asymptotic analysis for one-dimensional hyperbolic problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14520)
*Frederico Furtado,Felipe Pereira,Li-Ming Yeh*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过逐方程比对指出，arXiv:2106.08363v3所提出的"无流动曲线"(解dsigma/dt=H(u)/u)并非新概念，而正是Douglas、Pereira和Yeh(2000)提出的欧拉-拉格朗日局部守恒方法中的时空积分曲线，其"无流动区域"即为该曲线所包围的管状区域，标量前向追踪形式也已在Mancuso等人(2007)的论文中出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对E. Abreu等人2023年发表于Numerical Methods in Partial Differential Equations的文章声称其基于"无流动曲线"构建的时空控制体积方案为新贡献，本文旨在通过数学推导和文献考证纠正其归属问题，澄清该方法并非首创，而是对既有DPY方法的重新描述。

Method: 采用逐方程对比的数学分析方法，将所评论文章的核心方程与DPY(2000)和Mancuso等人(2007)的对应方程逐一比对，证明数学等价性；同时利用全文语料库追踪术语和归属的变化，并检视2025-2026年相关出版物的引用情况。

Result: 证明了"无流动曲线"正是DPY时空积分曲线的等价形式；"无流动区域"即为DPY的积分曲线管；其标量前向追踪形式已出现在Mancuso等人(2007)的论文中。值得注意的是，H'(u)与H(u)/u仅在线性通量下恒等，只有后者能使横向质量通量消失。2025-2026年的论文仍使用同一构造但未引用DPY，一篇2026年论文的归属表述含混。

Conclusion: 所评论文章所称的"无流动曲线"并非新对象，而是对既有DPY方法的等价重新描述。所评论文章的归属声明与其自身定义矛盾（其文内称DPY论文首先引入时空局部守恒，摘要却宣称该对象为作者自创）。后续的离散方案和数值分析贡献未被质疑，但连续曲线的归属应归功于DPY。

Abstract: This Comment concerns arXiv:2106.08363v3 [math.NA] by E. Abreu, A. Espirito Santo, W. Lambert and J. Perez, published in Numer. Methods Partial Differential Equations 39 (2023) 2400-2443. That article builds its scheme on space-time control volumes whose lateral boundaries, called "no-flow curves", solve dsigma/dt = H(u)/u and are presented as new. We show, equation by equation, that this object is the space-time integral curve of the locally conservative Eulerian-Lagrangian method of Douglas, Pereira and Yeh [Comput. Geosci. 4 (2000) 1-40], that the no-flow region is the tube those curves bound, and that its scalar forward-tracked form appears in Mancuso, Pereira and de Souza [TEMA 8 (2007) 269-276, 277-286]. At issue is not moving a control volume, which is generic, but which curve moves it: H'(u) and H(u)/u coincide identically only for a linear flux, and only the latter makes lateral mass flux vanish. The commented article itself calls the 2000 paper the first to introduce space-time local conservation, with an integral tube bounded by integral curves, while its abstract calls the same object introduced by the authors. A full-text corpus documents the changing terminology and attribution. Papers published in 2025 and 2026 use the same construction while citing later work but not DPY. Another 2026 paper gives mixed attribution: it calls the no-flow curve an "extension" of the DPY integral curve, although its own zero-flux definition and ratio ODE show identity, and it states that diffusion and dispersion do not modify the defining vector field. It extends the model, scheme and analysis, not the continuous curve. The later discrete contributions are not challenged.

</details>

### [121] [Form factors of field exponentials in the 1+1 dimensional Sine-Gordon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14365)
*Karol K. Kozlowski,Alex Simon*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在1+1维Sine-Gordon量子场论中，给出了Fock空间所有扇区（孤子、反孤子和呼吸子）中广义积分核（表示场算符指数）的基本构建块——形状因子（form factors）的显式表达式，并验证了这些结果与文献中已有的自由场方法结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sine-Gordon量子场论是1+1维可积场论的重要模型，其场算符的指数在量子理论中对应广义积分核。此前这些核的形状因子已在文献中通过自由场等方法得到，但缺乏系统、统一的显式表达式，覆盖所有Fock空间扇区。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者推导了Sine-Gordon量子场论中场算符指数所表示的广义积分核的形状因子，并分别在Fock空间的三个扇区——孤子（soliton）、反孤子（antisoliton）和呼吸子（breather）扇区中给出显式表达式。通过将所得结果与文献中早先利用自由场方法获得的结果进行对比验证。

Result: 在所有扇区中均获得了形状因子的显式表达式，且这些结果复现了文献中已有的多种结果，特别是通过自由场框架获得的结果，证明了新表达式的正确性和统一性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了Sine-Gordon场论中广义积分核形状因子的完整、统一的显式描述，覆盖所有Fock空间扇区，并与已有文献结果一致，为后续在该理论中的进一步计算奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: This work provides explicit expressions, in all sectors of the Fock space: soliton, antisoliton and breather, for the fundamental building blocks -the form factors-
  of the generalised integral kernels which represent the exponential of the field operator in the 1+1 dimensional Sine-Gordon quantum field theory. The obtained form factors reproduce the various results obtained earlier in the literature using, in particular, the free field approach.

</details>


### [122] [A semiclassical limit of reduced Hartree-Fock theory at positive temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14436)
*Dominic Shillingford*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究约化Hartree-Fock巨势在正温度下半经典极限收敛到Thomas-Fermi巨势，提出了极限泛函的密度-势对偶表示，证明对偶问题无对偶间隙且存在极大值点，并建立了均匀的半经典迹公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正温度量子统计力学中，约化Hartree-Fock巨势在半经典极限下收敛到Thomas-Fermi巨势这一事实已被确立，但需要进一步从数学上严格刻画极限泛函的结构，特别是密度-势对偶表示及其性质，以统一理解半经典极限下的热力学行为。

Method: 针对一般类别的费米子熵函数，构造Thomas-Fermi巨势的密度-势对偶表示；证明对偶问题无对偶间隙并存在极大值点；建立对偶表示与零温巨势的联系；对费米-狄拉克熵验证其与标准正温度Thomas-Fermi自由能泛函的一致性；通过受共同约束条件限制的局部预紧势族，建立关于半经典迹公式的均匀性证明。

Result: 得到的巨势在温度趋于零时收敛到已确立的零温巨势；对费米-狄拉克熵，该结果与标准正温度Thomas-Fermi自由能泛函一致；建立了在共同约束条件下对局部预紧势族均匀成立的半经典迹公式。

Conclusion: 该工作从数学上严格完成了正温度约化Hartree-Fock巨势半经典极限的完整刻画，不仅给出了极限泛函的对偶表示并验证其良定性，还通过均匀迹公式为更广泛势类中的半经典极限提供了统一的证明框架。

Abstract: It is established that the reduced Hartree-Fock grand potential at positive temperature converges in the semiclassical limit to a Thomas-Fermi grand potential. In particular, a density-potential dual representation of the limiting functional is proposed for a general class of fermionic entropy functions. The corresponding dual problem has no duality gap and admits a maximizer. The resulting grand potential converges to the established zero-temperature grand potential as the temperature tends to zero and, for the Fermi-Dirac entropy, agrees with the standard positive-temperature Thomas-Fermi free-energy functional. The proof also establishes a semiclassical trace formula that is uniform over locally precompact families of potentials subject to a common confinement condition.

</details>


### [123] [The Okinawa Lectures on Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14523)
*Klaas Landsman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于经典与量子熵理论的系统性综述/课程讲义，从经典概率论和量子概率论的视角出发，系统介绍了经典熵（如Kullback-Leibler熵）与大偏差理论（Sanov、Cramér、Gärtner-Ellis、Varadhan定理），以及量子熵（如Umegaki相对熵、Rényi熵）与von Neumann代数、Tomita-Takesaki模理论、Haagerup非交换L^p空间等数学工具之间的关系，最终目标是统一理解经典与量子熵结构，并与黑洞热力学相关联（但本文不讨论该应用）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典熵与量子熵虽然表面上形式相似，但其深层数学结构和理论基础截然不同。该论文旨在通过统一概率论框架（经典与量子概率论）以及大偏差理论、统计假设检验、模理论等工具，系统阐明经典熵与量子熵之间的深刻关联，为理论物理学家（特别是关注黑洞热力学的学者）提供一套既数学严谨又物理直观的熵理论体系。

Method: 论文采用课程讲义的形式，首先引入经典与量子概率论中的基本熵定义；然后借助大偏差理论的经典定理（Sanov、Cramér、Gärtner-Ellis、Varadhan）分析经典熵，并应用于Boltzmann与Gibbs统计物理；接着通过统计假设检验建立量子熵与经典熵的深层联系，引入Umegaki相对熵和Rényi熵的变形；利用von Neumann代数形式化子系统上的相对熵定义，借助Tomita-Takesaki模理论给出Araki-Uhlmann量子相对熵的框架；使用Haagerup非交换L^p空间将这些熵以密度算子和迹的形式重新定义与计算；最后通过Connes-Størmer-Narnhofer-Thirring熵给出量子版本的Kolmogorov-Sinai动力学熵。

Result: 论文系统地建立了从经典到量子熵的完整知识框架，将Kullback-Leibler熵、Umegaki相对熵、Rényi熵统一纳入统计假设检验的范畴；通过Tomita-Takesaki模理论将Araki-Uhlmann相对熵作为更广义的量子熵框架，覆盖了前两者；利用Haagerup非交换L^p空间解决了基于密度算子与迹的熵计算问题；同时在von Neumann代数框架下构建了量子动力学的CSNT熵作为Kolmogorov-Sinai熵的量子对应物。

Conclusion: 该课程以数学严格性为基础，为理论物理学家提供了一套完整的经典-量子熵理论体系，展示了经典熵与量子熵在深层结构上的统一性（通过统计假设检验、模理论与非交换积分），并为未来在黑洞热力学等领域的应用奠定了理论基础，尽管本文刻意不涉及该应用及热力学第二定律的讨论。

Abstract: After a historical introduction, the most important classical and quantum entropies are introduced as constructions in classical and quantum probability theory. Classical entropies are studied from large deviation theory, including theorems of Sanov, Cramér, Gärtner-Ellis, and Varadhan, and are illustrated in some applications to both Boltzmannian and Gibbsian statistical physics. Quantum entropies superficially connect to classical entropies at the formula level, but more deeply do so via the crucial role of entropy in statistical hypothesis testing. The classical (relative) Kullback-Leibler entropy, its quantum counterpart introduced by Umegaki, as well as their deformations proposed by Renyi all fit naturally in this context. Quantum entropy faces the new problem of defining and computing the relative entropy of a pair of states on a subsystem, here formalized as a von Neumann algebra. This requires modular (aka Tomita-Takesaki) theory, which provides the framework for the relative quantum entropies introduced by Araki and Uhlmann (these encompass both the Kullback-Leibler and Umegaki entropies as special cases). To (re)define and compute these entropies in terms of density operators and traces, further constructions are needed, namely Haagerup's noncommutative L^p spaces. Von Neumann algebras also provide the setting for the Connes-Stormer-Narnhofer-Thirring entropy, which is a quantum version of the Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy in dynamical systems and ergodic theory, to which we also provide an introduction. This course was originally inspired by, and should be relevant to, black hole thermodynamics, although we discuss neither this application nor the second law. The course tries to be both mathematically rigorous and interesting to theoretical physicists. Prerequisites are undergraduate probability theory, functional analysis, and quantum theory.
